# Pluribus

A MerrJep / FINN-style classifieds site for Albania. Rust (axum, sqlx, Askama) renders the pages on the server, Turbo and Stimulus make them feel instant in the browser, and PostgreSQL 15+ stores everything. The same pages can later be wrapped as iOS and Android apps with Hotwire Native.

## Running it locally

You need Docker and Rust 1.97 or newer. Start the database and the photo store, create your `.env`, and run the app, which applies the migrations at startup:

```bash
docker compose up -d
```

The photo store (Garage, see "Photos" below) needs a one-time setup after its first start, which creates its bucket and the app's key, the ones `.env.example` names:

```bash
sh dev/setup_garage.sh
```

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

```bash
cargo run
```

Then open http://127.0.0.1:3000. Every setting is in `.env` (see "Configuration" below); the copied values work locally as they are. On Windows, use `127.0.0.1` rather than `localhost`: `localhost` tries IPv6 first, which adds about 200 ms to every request through WSL.

To keep the release build running there instead, as the development site, deploy it with the script (from WSL): it builds it, keeps the one running as the previous, restarts it on `BIND_ADDR`, checks it answers, and puts the previous back if it doesn't. It stops on a migration git doesn't know yet, which the server would apply, and rebuilds from clean when the binary is older than the sources (a build folder shared with another checkout):

```bash
dev/deploy.sh
```

`dev/deploy.sh --rollback` puts the previous one back.

To load the sample listings, from a bash shell. Their cover photos are free Unsplash photos, loaded from Unsplash's image CDN; the fixture credits each one.

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < tests/fixtures/listings.sql
```

For a fuller site, also load 305 more listings across every category, each with a photo, a point on the map near its town, its seller's (fake) phone number, and the properties its category defines (a car's make, model, year, mileage, fuel, gearbox, body type and colour; a flat's rooms, area, floor and furnishing; and so on), so every filter has data to work on:

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < dev/sample_listings.sql
```

For a site at a real site's size, generate as many listings as you like (`dev/generate_listings.sql`): in every category, in about the shares a real site has them, each put together from lists of what Albanian listings say and cost ("Volkswagen Golf 1.6 TDI, 2012", "Shitet apartament 2+1, 95 m², Komuna e Parisit, Tiranë"), with the properties its category takes, a town and a point near it, a seller (a few with many listings, car dealers, agencies and shops among them), and two to four photos of its kind of thing (free ones from Unsplash, credited in the script). Runs add up; `-v seed=0.42` (between -1 and 1) repeats one, and `remove=1` takes out everything the script has made, and nothing else. 100 000 listings take about two and a half minutes:

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v count=100000 < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v remove=1 < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

The sample listings, the test fixtures and listings from earlier versions of the script have only a cover; `photos=1` makes nothing new but gives each of them more photos of its category, two to four in all (a million listings take about a minute):

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v photos=1 < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

A generated property listing stands in the zone its title names where its town has zones ("…, Blloku, Tiranë"), so the zones have their averages a square metre. Earlier versions of the script left them in the town; `zones=1` makes nothing new but moves those into their zones:

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v zones=1 < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

`only=<top-level category>` keeps making or removing to that category's listings (removing, with the sellers it leaves without any; making, with sellers and shops of their own): after electronics got their own properties (migration 0021), its listings were made anew with them:

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v remove=1 -v only=elektronike < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v count=150000 -v only=elektronike < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

`views=1` makes nothing new but gives a twentieth of the active listings the script made two weeks of views (`listing_daily_stats`), most of them few and a few hundreds a day, so "Më të klikuarat" has rows to show before anyone has looked around; runs add up:

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v views=1 < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

`drops=1` makes nothing new but makes a twentieth of the active listings the script made with a price (on sale, for rent or a service) 8 to 25% cheaper, rounded as people round prices, as if their sellers had lowered them, so the cards and pages have price drops to show (the price history's trigger records them); runs add up. Those who keep a listing hear only of the drops its seller makes on the site:

```bash
docker compose exec -T db psql -U marketplace -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v drops=1 < dev/generate_listings.sql
```

The tests need the database running; each test gets a fresh database of its own, which is most of what a test costs. Two things keep that cheap. `compose.yaml` runs the development database for speed over durability (no waiting for the disk on each commit), with the lock memory and connections for a dozen test databases at once. And `dev/test_template.sh` makes the cluster's `template1` a migrated copy of the schema (sqlx-cli needed: `cargo install sqlx-cli --no-default-features --features postgres`), so that the `create database` behind every `#[sqlx::test]` copies 12 MB of files in a tenth of a second instead of running the 41 migrations, which the migrator then finds applied. Run it once after `docker compose up -d`, and again whenever `migrations/` changes: a new migration only costs the tests its own run until then, but a migration changed under the tests fails them all with "migration N was previously applied but has been modified". Scratch databases made with `sqlx database create` come migrated too. The 193 request tests then take about 15 seconds, eight at a time in each of their fourteen files:

```bash
sh dev/test_template.sh
```

```bash
cargo test -- --test-threads=8
```

The tests' builds optimise every dependency, as the release does, and leave out rav1e's checks (`[profile.dev.package."*"]` in `Cargo.toml`): unoptimised, rav1e took seconds over each AVIF copy the photos' tests make, and image decoding and resizing are quicker too. The site's own crate builds as before; `media.rs` hands rav1e RGBA, since ravif's RGB path is inlined into the crate that calls it, where it ran twenty times slower.

`dev/check.sh` runs what a commit needs, clippy over every target and every test, side by side, each in a target directory of its own under `~/.cache` (a build there is far faster than one on a Windows drive from WSL), and prints only the warnings, the results and any failures; about 30 seconds once built. Given words, it runs only the tests whose names hold them, for while a change is being made:

```bash
sh dev/check.sh
```

```bash
sh dev/check.sh heading sort
```

Every request test names its file's one embedded copy of the migrations, `#[sqlx::test(migrator = "MIGRATOR")]` (with `fixtures("listings")` after it when it needs the sample listings); `MIGRATOR` is in `tests/common/mod.rs`, which every file takes in with `mod common;`. A plain `#[sqlx::test]` reads all the migrations from disk and embeds them again for its test alone: with 120 tests, that was three quarters of the file's build, and rebuilding the tests after a change to them took 21 seconds rather than 5 to 9.

The request tests are in a file for each part of the site ("Project layout" lists them; each file's first lines say what it covers), so that work on one part changes one file, not the `tests/web.rs` of ten thousand lines they all shared until 2026-10-12. Each file under `tests/` is a crate of its own, built into a test binary of its own, which cargo runs one after another, each as long as its slowest test: the fourteen take about 15 seconds where the one file took 9, and a change to `src/` builds and links all fourteen again (each about 475 MB with its debug information, in the tests' target directory). What their tests share is in `tests/common/mod.rs`; a helper only one file's tests use stays in that file, beside them. One file's tests alone:

```bash
cargo test --test posting -- --test-threads=8
```

**Windows:** Rust's MSVC toolchain needs Microsoft's C++ build tools for its linker (`link.exe`): Visual Studio Build Tools with the "Desktop development with C++" workload. Without them nothing links, so build and run in WSL instead.

## Project layout

| Path | Contents |
|---|---|
| `src/main.rs` | Startup: `.env`, the log (`RUST_LOG`), the database pool (ten connections, each planning every query for the values it runs with: `db::plan_for_values`), the migrations, the rebuilding of the words search corrects typos from, of the zones' prices a square metre, of what cars of each model ask (`db::refresh_car_prices`) and of how quickly each seller answers (`db::refresh_response_times`), and the forgetting of sign-in codes over a day old (`db::forget_old_codes`; at start and every 15 minutes), the HTTP server on `BIND_ADDR`, and a clean stop that first ends the newest listings' open streams |
| `src/lib.rs` | The application: `AppState` (the database pool, the photo store, the moderators, the AI, the views gathered, each visitor's recommendations kept), `app()`, which `main` serves and which starts the notices of unread messages (`notices::start`), the saved searches' alerts (`web::start_search_alerts`) the listings' expiry (`expiry::start`), the promotions' ends (`promotions::start`) and the euro rate (`rates::start`), and `app_with()` / `app_with_ai()`, which the tests build with photos in memory, a moderator of their own, views written at once and no recommendations kept |
| `src/web/mod.rs` | The HTTP layer: every route (see "Routes"), errors and the 404 page, the `Cache-Control` of static files (a year for the vendor files and for those asked for with the fingerprint, `cache_headers`), the check that refuses forms posted from other sites, the security headers of every response (the Content-Security-Policy and the rest, `security_headers`), the fingerprint of the site's CSS and JavaScript (`asset_version`), the import map (`import_map`, with `TURBO` and `STIMULUS`), the modules each page preloads, those it uses (`render`, `module_preloads`), the look's inline script (`THEME_SCRIPT`: the theme, the size and the accent), the stylesheets, which pages get as one, in their order and minified (`site_css`, `minified`, `STYLESHEETS`), and the scripts, each minified as it is asked for (`script`, `minified_js`) |
| `src/web/browse.rs` | The welcome page and the browse pages (a category, a search, the map page), and sellers' profiles (a store's with its cover, its logo, what it says of itself, its address, hours and ways to reach it, and "Ndrysho dyqanin" for its owner and admins): grids of listings with filters, orders and infinite scroll, the subcategory pills, the "Më të klikuarat" rows, and the listings a promotion features among a page's matches, the first two of them at the top of its results in their look, each with its price marked in the accent as the listing page marks its own, and not again among the first page's (`ListingPage::featured`, `FEATURED_IDS`, `FEATURED_SHOWN`); the main categories the menu on every page lists, in the welcome page's order (`menu_categories`, which a test keeps the database's) |
| `src/web/filters.rs` | A browse page's filters: read from the query string, turned into the listing query, and shown back as the filter form, the chips that remove them, the orders and the next page's URL; and numbers as people type them, in Albanian's way or English's (`parse_number`: "5 000", "5.000" or "5,000", a mark before three digits grouping thousands; "12,5" or "12.5", decimals; the later of a dot and a comma the decimals' mark), which the filters, the posting form, its properties and the AI's answers read |
| `src/web/listings.rs` | A listing's page (its layout, a car's as FINN's with its key facts in a row, its details and their icons, photos, price per square metre, loan, a property's or a car's map, the seller's other listings and the related ones), what it tells link previews and Google (`listing_meta`), the phone number's reveal, the note on a listing just posted (`posted` for one posted signed out, `just_published` signed in, said once by the `posted` cookie, with the ways to share it, `whatsapp_share`), the promotions on it for its seller and the way to give one for a moderator (`promotion_note.html`), a car's price against what cars of its model and year ask (`car_prices.rs`, `car_price.html`), and the views it reports |
| `src/web/car_prices.rs` | A car's price against what cars of its model and year ask: the one verdict (`verdict`: "Çmim i mirë" 15 % or more under the median, "Rreth mesatares" within 15 %, "Mbi mesataren" 15 % or more over, none under half of it, where a price says only that it is likely wrong or a lure), the car page's bubble (`CarPrice`: the median and the middle half's prices in the listing's currency, rounded to the hundred euros or the ten thousand lekë, from how many cars), and the listing form's hint (`hint`, `/shto/cmimi`) |
| `src/web/searches.rs` | Saved searches: "Ruaj kërkimin" on a category or search page, `/kerkimet-e-ruajtura` with how many listings came since each was last opened, opening, letting go, alerts on or off; a saved search's page made again from its path and query as `browse.rs` makes it (`replay`); and the alerts, every `ALERT_CHECK_EVERY`, at most one per search in `ALERT_GAP` (`send_search_alerts`, which the tests call; by e-mail, with a link that stops that search's alone, or by WhatsApp with `WHATSAPP_SEARCH_TEMPLATE`; the e-mail in its recipient's language, the notification's title and the template's texts in Albanian) |
| `src/web/notifications.rs` | The notifications centre, `/lajmerimet`: every notification the user had in the last 90 days, the latest first, each with its kind's icon and leading to what it is about, those unread marked until the page has been shown (`seen`, up to the latest it showed), their titles, kept in Albanian, worded for an English reader from the texts they were written by (`TITLES`, `shown_title`: a promotion's with its product's name, an invitation's with the role in its text too); and the account button's count (`counts`): how many are unread, and how many of those are about the user's own listings |
| `src/web/saved.rs` | The hearts: keeping a listing, the page of those kept (`/te-ruajturat`) with the price drops not seen yet and those no longer up, each saying why in its badge's tone (`GoneListing::label`, `status_tone`: the words and the tone `domain::Status` gives a listing off the site, `gone_label` and `tone`, which "Njoftimet e mia" shares), keeping one after signing in, and the header heart's dot |
| `src/web/messages.rs` | Messages between buyers and sellers: the page that starts a conversation from a listing, a conversation, the inbox (`/mesazhet`), the header's unread count, blocking, and the limits that keep spam out; a store's conversations shared by its people, who don't write to their own store (`MessageTarget::own`); when a message came, in the page's language ("Dje, 14:32", in English "8 Oct 2026, 14:32"), and the names the site gives people said in it too (`i18n::name`); what a conversation's head says of its listing once it is no longer simply up, so that neither side writes on about a thing gone ("Rezervuar", "Shitur" or "Dhënë me qira", "Jo më në faqe": `listing_status`, by `domain::Status::conversation_label`) |
| `src/web/post.rs` | Posting, editing, pausing, putting back and deleting listings, "Njoftimet e mia" with what each listing drew (views, contacts, saves), and the limits that keep spam out; a property's or a car's own point on the map (`mapped`: near its town, `POINT_NEAR_KM`, rounded to about a hundred metres, another category's let go; an edit's form starting from it); an edit's photos in the order its form sent (`photo_order`), and a form sent back with an error that doesn't ask for its photos again where the browser holds them (`photos_kept`, `photos_again`); posting as a store the poster may post for ("Posto si", `posting_stores`, `Poster::for_store`: the listing shows the store's name and logo, and its number, else the poster's contact); why a new listing waits for a moderator, a `domain::ReviewReason`; and on "Njoftimet e mia", each listing's badge and buttons by where it stands, as `domain::Status`'s rules say (`MyListing`: `status_label` by `seller_label`, `status_tone` by `tone`, `editable`, `pausable`, `resumable`, `sellable`, `renewable`), the rules the seller's actions themselves go by (`db::ListingAction`), where each once had its own copy |
| `src/web/properties.rs` | A category's properties in the listing form (the fields, a car's main ones first in the canvas's order, `CAR_ORDER`; tiles with icons once; the body types' drawings), and checking what a seller picked or typed |
| `src/web/ai_fill.rs` | "Plotëso pjesën tjetër me AI": the listing form filled in by the AI, its answer checked against the form's own lists; on an edit (`listing`, the seller's own), the listing's photos too, the cover first, before those just picked |
| `src/web/photos.rs` | Photos on their way in (the posting form, their checks, a form's photos made four at once, `AT_ONCE`; a form's files under their own names, a store's logo and cover, `Submission::files`) and out (`/media/...`, a store's pictures too, where a photo kept before there were small or AVIF copies gets its small one as it is first asked for, and an AVIF one's WebP twin in its place, `STAND_IN`); the copies a page offers the browser (`srcset`, and the cards' `avif_srcset`; the small and AVIF ones' addresses are the card's with their own endings, `small_url`); deleting a photo's files, or a store picture's one (`delete`) |
| `src/web/language.rs` | The language the pages speak: the layer that sets it around every request from the `gjuha` cookie, Albanian without one (`language`), and to a visitor without one whose browser reads no Albanian (Accept-Language naming neither Albanian nor its Tosk and Gheg, `reads_no_albanian`) offers English; `POST /gjuha`, which keeps the choice a year, and on a signed-in user's account, and goes back to the page it was chosen on, this site's alone (`choose`, `came_from`) |
| `src/web/account.rs` | Signing in with a code (a language chosen before it becomes the account's, for its e-mails), sessions, "Llogaria", the limits on asking for and guessing codes, and the cookie that tells the pages' scripts someone is signed in (`signed_in_cookie`) |
| `src/web/settings.rs` | "Cilësimet", the account's settings: its name; its phone number and e-mail address, either added or changed by a code sent to the new one (`add_contact`, `contact_confirmed`, which the code page calls) and either taken off while the other stays; what it hears of; those it blocked, each after their initial (`BlockedSeller::initial`); the devices signed in to it; and closing it (`close`), its listings' photos taken out of the store after, the page after it naming its stores where it owned any (`ClosedPage::stores`); the stores it is in, with its role in each (`SettingsPage::stores`), and the invitations into others waiting for its contacts (`SettingsPage::invitations`) |
| `src/web/unsubscribe.rs` | The links in e-mail notices and alerts that stop them without signing in (`/ndalo/{token}/{what}`, the token the account's own, `db::notice_token`): "mesazhet" stops the notices of unread messages as their box on "Cilësimet" does, "kerkimi-{id}" one saved search's alerts as its switch on "Kërkimet e ruajtura" does, "email" every notice by e-mail as "Edhe me email" does, the notifications staying on "Lajmërimet" (the expiry warnings' link: they have no box of their own); opened, a link asks first ("T'i ndalojmë?"), so that a link checker following it stops nothing; its form, or a mail program's one-click POST to the same address, stops them, and "Rikthe" takes it back (`Step`; a token, a kind or a search that isn't the account's is "not found") |
| `src/web/stores.rs` | Stores: making one (`/dyqani/krijo`, up to `STORES_MOST` an account owns) and changing it, for its owner and admins (`/dyqani/{slug}/ndrysho`): its name, what it says of itself, its logo and cover, each kept, replaced or taken off (`store_image`, which makes the picture off the request's thread), its number (`store_phone`: an Albanian mobile or landline, or a foreign one with its +), whether it has WhatsApp, its e-mail and website (`website`), its town, address, hours and NIPT; the files of a picture replaced or taken off deleted from the store after the row no longer names them. Its people (`/dyqani/{slug}/njerezit`, for its owner and admins): asking someone in by a phone number or an e-mail address with a role (`invite`, at most `INVITATIONS_MOST` open; an address hears of it by e-mail, in the language of the account it signs in to if one does, else Albanian, a number has WhatsApp's button with the invitation's link, `PeoplePage::whatsapp`), the roles each gives (`given_by`: an owner any, an admin those under its own) and their names (`role_name`, `role_does`), changing one's role or letting them go, withdrawing an invitation, leaving; an invitation's page (`/ftesa/{id}`, `Viewer`), taken or turned down by whoever signs in with its contact |
| `src/web/moderation.rs` | `/moderim`: the moderators put up or refuse the listings that wait (one of `REFUSALS`, which its seller reads), keep the listings people reported or take them down, suspend the account behind either (one of `SUSPENSIONS`) and restore it, and approve the makes, models and brands sellers typed in, or merge them into listed ones; and give a listing a promotion by its number (`grant_promotion`; a number no listing up or reserved has, or no promotion chosen, said beside the form) or end one before its time (`end_promotion`) |
| `src/web/promotions.rs` | Promotions as the pages show them: `/promovo`, those on sale (`db::promotion_products`), what each does (`does`) and costs, and how to get one, by writing to `CONTACT_EMAIL` (or through "Kontakt") with the listing's number, in the e-mail's subject when a seller's page led there (`njoftimi`), until there are payments |
| `src/web/safety.rs` | Trust and safety: reporting a listing (its reasons and their names), blocking the seller behind one and letting them be again, and the accounts a visitor blocked, which the pages of listings leave out for them |
| `src/web/named.rs` | The filters a search's words name: a category, a kind of listing (for sale, for rent, wanted), a town and, in property, a zone of it, read from the words in their forms ("apartament me qera ne tirane", "apartament 2+1 në Plazh"), and on an English page from English's words too (a category by its English name's last word, singular or plural, "flat for rent in Tirana"; `ENGLISH_FILLERS`, `english_about`, `same_english`), and the page with them on that the search goes to; and the zone a new listing's title or description names (`zone_in`), for its form |
| `src/web/suggestions.rs` | What the search boxes suggest as words are typed, each row's words split where they meet the words typed, for those parts to show bold (`marked`, `Part`) |
| `src/web/icons.rs` | Every icon of the site, [Phosphor](https://phosphoricons.com)'s since 2026-10-11 (core 2.1.1, MIT, its licence in `static/vendor/phosphor/LICENSE`), each drawn once as the inside of a 24-unit `<svg>`: the icon's 256-unit drawing scaled into it (`<g transform="scale(.09375)">`), its Regular weight's lines filled in `currentColor` and its Duotone weight's tone shape under them in the svg's own fill, none at rest and the accent where a hover or a press fills an icon (the welcome page's categories, the rail, the menu), the red of a kept heart, the knob's colour of the bookmark that keeps a search; the wrappers' strokes are left aside (`stroke="none"`). The lines are always the Regular weight's: the Duotone draws a few otherwise (its carets as triangles, its tick smaller inside a square, its wifi as a fan, its rising line with a closed head, its banknote's corners), which the first drawing took over until the user found the back button off the middle of its line, and those few, the four carets, the tick, the rising line, the wifi and the banknote, have no tone, theirs belonging to the other drawing. Phosphor draws its carets 8 of its units off the middle, towards their point; theirs are moved back to it (`translate(±.75)` before the scale), so that a chevron sits in the middle of its round button and level with the words beside it. The icons CSS draws are Phosphor's Regular ones too: a native select's chevron (`--select-chevron` in `base.css`, in each theme's muted grey) and the masks `welcome.css` paints in the words' colour (`--check`, a checkbox's or a chosen option's tick; `--chevron-down`, a picker's; `--info`, a notice's i in a circle). The templates take them by name (`crate::web::icons::SEARCH`) and draw none of their own, `pin.svg` and `mail.svg` too, and the three scripts that draw an icon carry Phosphor's (`photos_controller.js`'s arrows and cross, `picker_controller.js`'s chevron, `suggest_controller.js`'s clock); only the logo, a car's body types (`properties.rs`: Phosphor has no saloon, coupé or pickup) and the maps' markers stay the site's own drawings. Where a category shows two side by side they differ (a car from the front and from the side, a helmet and a hard hat), as `every_property_a_listing_shows_has_an_icon` checks. Among them, the details' on a listing's page (the posting form's car tiles once), the eye of the view counts, the heart and the handset (the hearts, the phone button, the sellers' stats), the flag, the bell (saved searches), the crossed-out circle and the shield with a check (reporting, blocking, a verified number), the pencil, the two bars, the bin, the circle with a check, the clock, the triangle and the two arrows round a circle (a seller's own buttons: editing, pausing, deleting, selling, reserving, putting back up, renewing), the speech bubble and the paper plane (the messages), WhatsApp's mark (the chat beside a revealed number), a storefront (an invitation into a store), the moon (the menu's dark theme), a house (the rail's "Kreu"), the main categories' and the subcategories'; and, drawn as the redesign's canvas has them before the pages that take them are redrawn, three lines (the header's menu), a bookmark (keeping a search, the searches kept), a globe (the language), a big A and a small one (the size, compact, normal or large), a door's frame with an arrow out of it (signing out), an i in a circle (a note on how things stand, the error page), a crosshair (finding where you are on the map) and an office block (a store without a logo, in its round avatar: the seller's row, its page, an invitation into it): each marked `#[expect(dead_code)]` until its page takes it (the office block taken since step 8, the lines, the bookmark, the globe and the A's since step 12, the menu, the crosshair since step 20, the maps' "Vendndodhja ime"), so that the compiler says when the mark should come off; and for the menu, two squares with lines (Njoftimet e mia), three sliders (Cilësimet), a cross (closing it), a plus (posting) and the palette (the accent, the details' colour's too); and for the results' row, a chevron down (its filter dropdowns) and a tick (a search kept); and, for the drawings the pages had inline before Phosphor, a magnifier (the searches, nothing found), a person (the account), chevrons left and right (back and on, a photo's arrows, the listing before and after), a line going up ("Më të klikuarat"), a calculator (the loan), a chain's links and two sheets (sharing an address, copying a number), an arrow out of a square (a square metre's price), four squares, a square and lines with their dots (the results' three views, `Cards::icon` in `browse.rs`) and an envelope (a seller's e-mail address, `mail.svg`) |
| `src/web/rail.rs` | The rail at the screen's left from 720 px up (`base.html`): which of its account places a page is (the visitor's listings, the listings kept, the searches kept, the notifications, the conversations, the settings: `here`), from the request's path, which a middleware (`keep_path`, in `web::router`'s layers) keeps in a task-local while the page is made, and on a category's page its main category, which the page says (`set_category`, `category`) |
| `src/web/pages.rs` | The pages about the site, which every page's footer links: how it works, the safety tips, the terms of use, privacy and contact (`PAGES`, which the sitemap lists too); when the terms and the privacy page last changed (`TERMS_UPDATED`, `PRIVACY_UPDATED`: the day, the month and the year, said in the page's language, `said`), and who runs the site and how to reach them (`OPERATOR`, `CONTACT_EMAIL`) |
| `src/web/seo.rs` | What search engines and link previews read: each page's description, canonical address and Open Graph tags (`PageMeta`, which `meta.html` writes), a category page's trail for the results' own (schema.org's `BreadcrumbList`, from `browse.rs`), the site's own description and picture, `robots.txt` and the sitemaps |
| `src/db/mod.rs` | Database access: how the site's connections plan their queries (`plan_for_values`), the page size and count limit, `price_was!` (the SQL for the price a listing dropped from, which cards, a listing's page and the notifications share), and every query of the files below, re-exported, so each is `db::...` |
| `src/db/categories.rs` | The categories and places (towns, their zones), as the pages and the forms list them |
| `src/db/properties.rs` | Categories' properties: what the filters and the posting form offer, and what a listing says |
| `src/db/listing_pages.rs` | Pages of listing cards: the browse pages' filters, orders and pages, counts, the rows under a listing, the most seen, the welcome page's recommended by their ids (`cards_by_ids`) and the newest after them without them (`ListingFilter::exclude`), the map's points, each query of a search with words run as planned for `FIRST_TRY`, then, its matches counted through the words' index up to `FEW`, through that index for few and in order for many (`planned`, `ListingFilter::matches`); a seller's profile (`user_profile`, `business_profile`, with how quickly they answer and when they were last here); the listings a promotion features among a filter's matches, in another order each time (`featured_cards`, at most `FEATURED`), and on every card whether one features or highlights it (`ListingCard::featured`, `highlighted`), and for a car up for sale at a price what cars of its model and year ask (`model_median`), by its model and year's ids, which migration 0002 fixes |
| `src/db/listing.rs` | One listing: its page (with a store's logo for its seller, `seller_logo`; how quickly the seller answers, `seller_answers`, from `seller_response_times` once they had three conversations and answered half; when they were last on the site, `seller_seen`, a store's latest of its people), its photos and properties (put in the order its edit form sent, `order_listing_photos`), its own point on the map (`set_listing_point`, `listing_point`), where its photos are kept, for the AI (`listing_photo_keys`), the contact it reveals, and its neighbours on its category's page |
| `src/db/saved.rs` | The listings a signed-in user keeps |
| `src/db/recommended.rs` | "Të rekomanduara për ty": the listings like those a visitor looked at, ranked for all of them at once (`recommended_ids`: the candidates off each seed's category's indexes, scored as `related_listings` scores them and weighed by the seed, two a seller, the categories taking turns by their seeds' weight, 300 ms at most), and a signed-in user's seeds (`seed_listings`: the latest 12 listings they keep and 6 they wrote to a seller about) |
| `src/db/searches.rs` | Saved searches: keeping one (once per page and filters, `SEARCHES_MOST` at most), opening one, which counts its new listings from then on and sees its alerts, its alerts on or off, letting it go; the searches due an alert (with the language their saver's e-mail is in), recording one (`notifications`, `SAVED_SEARCH`), and how many have alerts unseen |
| `src/db/messages.rs` | Conversations and their messages: starting one, writing, reading up to a message, the inbox with its unread counts, what an account sent lately (for the limits), blocks. A store's conversations are its people's (`conversation`, `may_see`, `inbox`, `unread_conversations`): whoever posted the listing takes part from the start (else its owner, `seller_side_id`), the others from their first message or read (`write`, `mark_read`), and who left the store sees them no more; a message is the other side's by who sent it, the buyer or anyone on the seller's, so a colleague's is "ours", with their name (`Message::author`, `InboxEntry::last_author`), and no news; a member's unread count runs from their last read, or from when they joined; blocks count across the seller's side, and lifting one lifts a colleague's (`blocks_with_seller`, `set_conversation_blocked`) |
| `src/db/safety.rs` | Reports (one open per person and listing; `HIDE_AFTER` people with a serious reason take a listing off the site, the rule's doing in `moderation_actions`), the moderators' decisions on them, blocks, and the contacts people proved theirs by signing in (`user_verifications`) |
| `src/db/review.rs` | The moderators' review: the listings that wait before they go up (`PENDING_REVIEW`, why in `listings.review_reason`, a `domain::ReviewReason`: `NEW_ACCOUNT`, a first listing posted without signing in under a contact no account had, or `DUPLICATE_PHOTOS`, a photo another seller's published listing has, the very same file by `listing_media.sha256`, `photos_elsewhere`), the queue with the other sellers' listings sharing a photo (`pending_listings`), approving and refusing (`approve_listing`, `reject_listing`); suspending an account, its listings and the stores it owns off the site, what each was kept in `moderation_actions` (`suspend_user`), restoring it as it was (`restore_user`), and the accounts suspended (`suspended_users`); whether a listing's seller is suspended, its account or its store, for its page to say why it is off the site (`seller_suspended`: "llogaria e shitësit është pezulluar", rather than that people reported it) |
| `src/db/expiry.rs` | Listings whose time is up taken off the site (`expire_listings`: status `EXPIRED`, the status history saying "Skadoi", a notification `EXPIRED` each), and those a few days from it whose sellers haven't heard of this expiry (`warn_expiring`: a notification `EXPIRING` with the expiry it warns of, so that a renewed listing is warned of again), each with how to reach its seller, and the language their e-mail is in: whoever posted it, or for a store's listing, once they no longer post for the store or their account is no longer active, the store's owner, who may renew it too (`TOLD`); listings posted without signing in whose code nobody typed, a day on, gone with their contact and photos (`forget_drafts`) |
| `src/db/rates.rs` | The euro rate prices are worked out at (`exchange_rates`): reading it (`euro_rate`), taking the official one when it is `RATE_STEP` (half a percent) or more from it (`take_euro_rate`), and the listings priced in lekë worked out in euros again, `REPRICE_BATCH` a time by id, their `updated_at` left as it was (`reprice_batch`, `repriced`) |
| `src/db/notices.rs` | The notices of unread messages: who is due one (`due_notices`: those who took part and still see the conversation, of the other side's messages, the store's name for a store's answer, and the language their e-mail is in), recording each sent (`notifications`, `NEW_MESSAGE`, the conversation in its payload), and whether a user wants them (`message_alerts`, `set_message_alerts`; `notification_preferences.message_alerts`, on unless a row says otherwise) |
| `src/db/promotions.rs` | Promotions (`promotion_products`, `listing_promotions`): those on sale of the kinds the site does something with (`promotion_products`), those on now (`promotions_on`; a seller's, `listing_promotions`), a moderator giving one (`grant_promotion`: what it does copied onto the listing, FEATURED into `featured_until`, HIGHLIGHT into `highlighted_until`, BUMP and CATEGORY_TOP into `bumped_at`, its time added to what is left of one of its kind, its seller told by a notification, `PROMOTED`, to whoever keeps the listing, and the moderators' record keeping who gave it) or ending one (`end_promotion`, the listing keeping what another of its kind on it still gives), and in time (`tick_promotions`); none of it moves `updated_at`, which the sitemaps give search engines |
| `src/db/car_prices.rs` | What cars of a model ask (`car_model_prices`): rebuilding it (`refresh_car_prices`), a listing's own price in euros with what cars like it ask (`car_price`, for a car up for sale at a price alone), what a model and year ask by the model's code in the form (`model_prices`), and a currency's rate (`eur_rate`) |
| `src/db/notifications.rs` | A user's notifications of every kind (`notifications`: the last `NOTIFICATIONS_DAYS`, at most `NOTIFICATIONS_MOST`, each with the listing, saved search, conversation or invitation into a store it is about while it is still there to open), how many are unread, all and those about their own listings (`unread_notifications`, `domain::NotificationKind::OWN_LISTING`: `EXPIRING`, `EXPIRED`, `PROMOTED`), and reading them up to one (`notifications_seen`) |
| `src/db/price_drops.rs` | Price drops: what a listing's price dropped from, for its page; the notifications to those who keep it when its seller lowers it (`notify_price_drop`); those not seen yet, and seeing them |
| `src/db/sign_in.rs` | Sign-in codes, sessions and users; a code may carry a listing to publish, a new account's name or the account its contact joins (`CodePurpose`); codes asked for over a day ago forgotten (`forget_old_codes`); the session's lookup moves the user's `last_seen_at` to now once it is ten minutes old, in the same statement (`session_user`) |
| `src/db/account.rs` | The account's settings: its name (`rename_account`), what it hears of (`NoticePreferences`, all on until it says otherwise), a confirmed contact put on it in place of the one of its kind and on its listings that showed that one (`set_contact`), one taken off while the other stays (`remove_contact`), its sessions (`session_count`, `end_other_sessions`), its language (`set_preferred_language`; and that of the active account an address signs in to, `language_of_email`, which a store's invitation by e-mail is in), the token in the links that stop its e-mail notices without signing in, made at random the first time one goes out (`notice_token`, `notice_token_user` for an active account's alone; migration 0039), and closing it as "Users are anonymised, not deleted" has it (`delete_account`, `DELETED_NAME`), with the stores it owns; a contact changed or taken off reaches its own listings, not a store's, which show the store's number |
| `src/db/stores.rs` | Stores (`businesses`, `business_members`): one by its address, with its town (`store_by_slug`); the stores a user is in, with their role (`my_stores`, `MyStore`: those who may post as it, `domain::Role::posts`, and change it, `Role::manages`), and how many they own (`stores_owned`); making one (`create_store`: its address from its name, with "-2" and on after it when that is taken, its maker its `OWNER`) and changing it (`update_store`, which puts a new number on its listings and gives back the files of the pictures it no longer shows); its people (`members`, `role_in`, `posts_for`, `GIVEN_ROLES`, the spellings of `Role::GIVEN`, `set_member_role`, `remove_member`) and its invitations (`Invitation`: one per store and contact, asked again the same one with two weeks again, `invite`, which says when the contact's account is in already, `Invited::Member`, and gives that account a notification, `STORE_INVITE`; `store_invitations`, `invitations_for` a user's contacts, `accept_invitation`, `decline_invitation`, `cancel_invitation`) |
| `src/db/posting.rs` | Posting listings, as the user or as a store (`NewListing::business_id`, why one waits for a moderator in `NewListing::review`), and editing them (a store's by its people who post, `manages_listing`, migration 0035), pausing them and taking them down (`change_own_listing`: each `ListingAction` applies to the statuses `domain::Status`'s rules give it, the same rules "Njoftimet e mia" offers its buttons by, so the two can't disagree, and selling and reserving only to the kinds `ListingType::SELLABLE` names; `SELLABLE_KINDS`, their spellings, for `web/listings.rs`, which still compares a listing's kind with them), renewing them (`renew_listing`: `LISTING_DAYS` from then, brought up once in `BUMP_DAYS` at most); a seller's own listings, or a store's, with what each drew (`my_listings`, `ListingStats`: its views, how many times its contact was shown, how many keep it, all along and over the last seven days) |
| `src/db/typed_in.rs` | What sellers type in where a list lacks theirs ("Tjetër"), and how moderators approve or merge it |
| `src/db/sitemap.rs` | What the sitemaps list: the active listings by their numbers, and the businesses with listings |
| `src/domain/mod.rs` | What the site's things are, apart from the pages that show them and the database that keeps them: the kinds of `kinds.rs`, re-exported, so each is `domain::...` (`domain::Status`, `domain::Role`) |
| `src/domain/kinds.rs` | The kinds the schema's CHECKs spell out, as Rust enums, which were text compared with literals all over `src/` before, the CHECKs their only record: a listing's `Status` (0001's nine and 0011's `PAUSED`), its `ListingType`, `PriceType` and `Condition`, a store's `Role` (with `Role::GIVEN`, those an invitation gives, 0035's own CHECK), why a listing waits for review (`ReviewReason`, 0037) and a property's `DataType`; and `NotificationKind`, the kinds of notification the code writes, a column no CHECK limits. One macro declares them all (`kinds!`): each variant spelt as the SQL has it (`as_str`, `Display`, and serde in SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE) and read back from that spelling (`parse`, `FromStr`), with `ALL`, `NAMES` and `names` (the spellings of a few, as a const: what a query's `ANY($n)` is given, or a form offers, like `post::CONDITIONS`). Rows keep their columns as text, parsed where the code decides by them (`Status::parse(&row.status)`), and queries are given `as_str()`, so the SQL, and the queries `.sqlx/` holds, stay as they were. The rules about where a listing stands are `Status`'s methods, which "Njoftimet e mia" (`web/post.rs`), the listings kept (`web/saved.rs`), the conversations (`web/messages.rs`), the seller's actions (`db/posting.rs`) and reports (`db/safety.rs`) share, where each once had its own copy: `up` (active or reserved: what can be reported and what expires), `on_site` (and sold, whose page stays), `editable`, `pausable`, `resumable`, `sellable` (by `ListingType::sellable` too), `renewable`, and the words and tone of its badge (`seller_label`, `gone_label`, `conversation_label`, `tone`, the texts given to `t` as before); with `Role::posts` and `Role::manages`, and `NotificationKind::OWN_LISTING`. `web/listings.rs` still has a copy of its own, for now. Its tests read `migrations/` and hold each enum to the CHECK that last defines its column (a table's own, an added column's, or a constraint made again, as 0011 made `listings_status_check`), so a migration that adds a value fails them until its enum has it too; they hold `NotificationKind` to the kinds `src/` inserts into `notifications`, and check that every spelling reads back as itself |
| `src/views.rs` | Listing views: counted as listing pages report them, gathered in memory and written into the day's statistics once a minute; and the most seen rows the pages show |
| `src/recommended.rs` | The welcome page's recommendations: the seeds (`Seeds`: the listings seen lately, the latest 1 and each before it .85 of the one after, those kept 1.5, those written about 2, added up per listing in tenths, 30 at most; as the next pages' addresses name them, `1002x24.1005x20`), how many (24 a seed, 120 at most, shown with 4 at least: `FEWEST`), and each visitor's ranking kept 15 minutes (`Recommended`, 4 096 at most) |
| `src/media.rs` | Photos: checked, turned the right way up, resized and re-encoded (WebP in four sizes, the cards' two as AVIF too, a JPEG, and a tiny copy for the pages), then kept in an S3-compatible store (Garage locally, Cloudflare R2 in production), or in memory without one; the address pages give for them (`MEDIA_URL`); the small and AVIF copies of photos kept before there were any, made from their WebP ones in the background as the server starts (`Media::fill_in_copies`), or a small one as it is asked for before then (`Media::make_copy`); a store's logo (`logo`: a square from its middle, `LOGO_SIDE` px, on white where it is see-through) and cover (`store_cover`: a band three times as wide as it is high, up to `COVER_WIDTH` px), a WebP each |
| `src/sender.rs` | Sending sign-in codes: e-mail by SMTP, phone numbers by WhatsApp (Meta's Cloud API); a channel not set up writes to the log. The notices of unread messages too (`Notice`), and the alerts of saved searches and of listings about to expire or expired (`Alert`, what its e-mail says and what its WhatsApp template fills in, `AlertTemplate`): by e-mail, or by WhatsApp with a utility template of each kind's own (`WHATSAPP_NOTICE_TEMPLATE`, `WHATSAPP_SEARCH_TEMPLATE`, `WHATSAPP_EXPIRY_TEMPLATE`; one not set up, the log has them); an e-mail of these carries the link that stops its kind (`web/unsubscribe.rs`) in its text and in the headers mail programs show a button of their own by, `List-Unsubscribe` and `List-Unsubscribe-Post` (RFC 8058: a POST to the link stops them, no page between; `ListUnsubscribe`, `OneClick`), which a code or an invitation hasn't. An e-mail is in its recipient's language: a code's in that of the page that asked for it (`code_email`), the rest in the one kept on their account (its caller's `i18n::LANG` scope); a WhatsApp template's texts stay Albanian, as Meta approved them |
| `src/notices.rs` | Notices of unread messages: every minute (`CHECK_EVERY`, started by `app()`), those who have a message from the other side of a conversation unread three minutes after it came (`NOTICE_AFTER`) hear of it by e-mail or WhatsApp, the latest message and a link to answer (an e-mail with the link that stops them too, `/ndalo/{token}/mesazhet`), at most once per conversation an hour (`NOTICE_GAP`) and only of messages newer than the last notice (`send_due`, which the tests call); the e-mail in the language kept on the reader's account, the notification's title kept in Albanian, which `web/notifications.rs` words for whoever reads it |
| `src/promotions.rs` | Promotions in time: every 15 minutes (`CHECK_EVERY`, started by `app()`), those whose time is up end, and a listing at the top of its category for a while goes back to the top of "newest first" once a day since it last went (`db::tick_promotions`) |
| `src/expiry.rs` | Listings that expire: every 15 minutes (`CHECK_EVERY`, started by `app()`), those up or reserved whose time is up leave the site, and their sellers hear of it, and three days before (`WARN_DAYS`), by a notification and an e-mail (with a link that stops every notice by e-mail, these having no box of their own; or a WhatsApp message, with `WHATSAPP_EXPIRY_TEMPLATE`) with the way to keep it (`run`, which the tests call; the e-mail in the language kept on the seller's account, the notification's title in Albanian, for `web/notifications.rs` to word); and the listings posted without signing in that their code never put up, gone with their photos' files (`db::forget_drafts`, `web::delete_photos`) |
| `src/rates.rs` | The euro rate: every six hours (`RATE_CHECK_EVERY`, started by `app()`) the Bank of Albania's official rate read off its page (`RATES_PAGE`, `parse_euro_rate`), taken when it moved far enough, and the listings priced in lekë worked out in euros again where that is due, a batch at a time with a pause between, going on after a restart (`reprice`, which the tests call) |
| `src/ai.rs` | Claude, Anthropic's AI, through its Messages API: what fills in the listing form (`web/ai_fill.rs`) |
| `src/i18n.rs` | The site in two languages, Albanian and English: `t`, which says an Albanian text in the request's language (its English from the catalogue, or itself where there is none; a template's text, a handler's, or one from the database), `t1`, `t2`, `t3` for texts with values ("{}" in turn, or "{1}", "{2}" where the English orders them otherwise), `tf` with named ones; `t_value`, a property's value as the database words it (a choice's label, "Jo", a number and its unit, "148 000 km", "6,1 inç", whose English takes a decimal point, the unit's English and its singular for one, a date as "4 Oct 2026"); `reworded` and `reworded_by`, a text the site keeps in Albanian (a notification's title) said again in the reader's language from the text it was written by, its values as they are or said too; `name`, a person's name as a page shows it, the ones the site gives ("Përdorues", "Përdorues i fshirë") in its language; the request's language (`LANG`, a task-local the language layer sets, `lang`, `english`) and whether its page offers English (`OFFER`, `offer_english`), what goes out on its own in its recipient's (`in_lang`), and the scripts' texts as JSON for an English page (`scripts_json`); its tests check that every text the templates, the code and the scripts give `t` has its English (a script's among the scripts' own), and that a text printed unescaped (`|safe`, for its apostrophe) has no markup in either language |
| `src/format.rs` | Albanian display formatting (in English its words, a decimal point, dates as "4 Oct 2026"): prices, counts, dates and when something happened in words ("Sot", "Dje", "para 3 ditësh"), and when a listing expires within the week ("Skadon pas 3 ditësh"), all by Albania's clock, phone numbers (`phone`: a mobile +355 69 123 4567, a fixed line +355 4 222 3333 in Tirana and +355 52 123 456 elsewhere, a foreign one as it is; a landline was one run of digits once); what a seller's card says of how they answer and when they were last here (`seller_activity`: `answers_within`, "brenda 1 ore"..., and `last_active`, "Aktiv sot", "Aktiv dje", "Aktiv para 3 ditësh" within the week) |
| `build.rs` | Rebuilds when a migration changes, so `sqlx::migrate!()` embeds the current files; and makes the English catalogue (`i18n/en/*.txt`) a sorted table (`EN`), its `js*` files' entries a table of their own too (`EN_JS`), an entry given twice with another English, or none, stopping the build |
| `i18n/en/` | The English catalogue, in British spelling: each entry the Albanian text on a line and its English on the next after "= ", a blank line between entries, by area: `base.txt` every page's frame, the names the site gives people and the language in "Cilësimet"; `format.txt` prices, counts and dates; `browse.txt` the welcome page, the categories' and search pages, the filters, the map and the cards; `listing.txt` a listing's page and a seller's or store's; `posting.txt` the posting form and "Njoftimet e mia"; `account.txt` signing in, "Cilësimet", the kept listings and searches, the notifications, unsubscribing; `messages.txt` the messages and the stores' pages; `pages.txt` the pages about the site, the promotions and the errors; `mail.txt` what goes out on its own (the code's e-mail, the notices, the alerts, the invitations) and the notifications' titles as the database keeps them; `data.txt` the database's own labels (the categories, the properties' names and the names categories give them, the units, the choices, the promotions). The scripts' texts are in `js-browse.txt`, `js-listing.txt`, `js-posting.txt`, `js-account.txt` and `js-messages.txt`, the only ones an English page carries for them |
| `templates/` | Askama templates, compiled into the binary and checked at compile time (see "Templates") |
| `static/css/` | The site's styles: eight stylesheets, loaded in order (see "Stylesheets") |
| `static/js/` | `application.js` and the Stimulus controllers (see "JavaScript") |
| `static/img/` | `pluribus.png`, the site's picture in link previews of the pages without a photo of their own: the wordmark as the light theme's header has it (its i's dot black and the inside of its b in mint since 2026-10-11, recoloured in place so that the rest stays as it was; the dot was in mint before, edged, and in lime before that) and "Njoftime në gjithë Shqipërinë" under it in Geist, on the page's grey, 1200 × 630; and the site's icon, the logo's p in Clash Display, light (#f7f7f7) on eerie black (#1b1b1b), with the i's square in mint, the accent there is unless another is picked, over its bowl's right shoulder (lime until 2026-10-11; the drawings were recoloured, nothing else changed): `icon.svg` (a tile with 22% corners, the mark 70% of its height), `favicon.ico` (16, 32 and 48 px, served at `/favicon.ico`), `apple-touch-icon.png` (180 px, square, the mark 60% of its height, for iPhones' home screens, which round it themselves) and the manifest's `icon-192.png` and `icon-512.png` (tiles as `icon.svg`'s, see-through at the corners) and `icon-maskable-512.png` (square, the mark 58% of its height, inside the middle circle that Android's masks keep). The PNGs and the picture were drawn by headless Chrome from SVG on 2026-10-11 (the creature, in the buttons' coral on the ivory, before) |
| `static/site.webmanifest` | The site's name, colours and icons for phones' home screens and app lists |
| `static/vendor/` | Turbo, Stimulus, Leaflet and the font (Geist, the whole site's), with their versions in their names, so there's no Node or npm; and Phosphor's licence (`phosphor/LICENSE`), whose icons are drawn into `src/web/icons.rs` rather than served as files |
| `migrations/0001_marketplace_schema.sql` | Schema: tables, indexes, triggers |
| `migrations/0002_marketplace_reference_data.sql` | Albania's 12 counties and 61 municipalities, categories in Albanian with English translations, a sample Cars attribute model, promotion products |
| `migrations/0003_category_properties.sql` | Properties for every category, the kinds of listing each takes, exchange rates, and helpers for display and search |
| `migrations/0004_plate_codes.sql` | `location_municipality()`, which finds the municipality a place is in, and each municipality's district code from the 1993–2011 number plates (TR, DR, VL...; stored, not shown at the moment) |
| `migrations/0005_map_search.sql` | Radius search: the `cube` and `earthdistance` extensions, the index radius searches use, and a point for each municipality (its seat) |
| `migrations/0006_rename_patundshmeri_to_prona.sql` | Renames the property category from Patundshmëri to Prona (`/c/prona`) |
| `migrations/0007_sign_in_codes.sql` | Sign-in codes and sessions, for signing in with a phone number or an e-mail address |
| `migrations/0008_subcategories.sql` | Subcategories: each category's kinds (flats, houses... phones, laptops...) as subcategories, with the listings moved into them |
| `migrations/0009_seller_options.sql` | "Tjetër": options sellers type when a list lacks theirs (a car make or model, a phone brand), kept inactive until approved |
| `migrations/0010_post_before_sign_in.sql` | A listing posted before signing in waits for the code (`login_codes.listing_id`), as does a new account's name |
| `migrations/0011_paused_listings.sql` | Sellers can take a listing down for a while and put it back (`PAUSED`) |
| `migrations/0012_parts_and_accessories.sql` | Pjesë dhe aksesorë: car parts get kinds of their own, a third level (Pjesë këmbimi, Goma dhe disqe, GPS dhe navigatorë...), and say which vehicle they fit; a category's own name for a property (`category_attributes.label`) |
| `migrations/0013_car_makes_and_models.sql` | The makes and models sellers pick from: 42 makes common in Albania with their usual models; makes and models sellers had typed that are on the list join it |
| `migrations/0014_options_name.sql` | A car's equipment (ABS, air conditioning...) is called "Opsionet" |
| `migrations/0015_generated_titles.sql` | A car's title made from what it is (make, model, body type, colour; its engine and year since 0027), kept in `listings.generated_title` |
| `migrations/0016_fuzzy_search.sql` | Search that forgives typos and finds a word's other forms: the words listings use (`search_words`), a rough Albanian stem (`sq_stem`), and `search_query()`, which widens a search's words with them |
| `migrations/0017_search_categories_and_places.sql` | A listing's category, the categories above it and its place become words search finds it by; searches leave out filler words (në, me, për...), and "ose" and "apo" mean or |
| `migrations/0018_category_order_indexes.sql` | A category's listings by price (cheapest or dearest first) and nearest a point first, read straight off indexes; `btree_gist`, for the category beside the place in one GiST index |
| `migrations/0019_zones.sql` | Zones: the parts of Tirana, Durrës and Vlorë people name (Blloku, Plazh, Lungomare...), places under their town, which property listings can say they are in |
| `migrations/0020_zone_area_prices.sql` | What a square metre costs in each zone, by category (`zone_area_prices`), which a property's price per square metre is compared with |
| `migrations/0021_electronics_properties.sql` | Electronics' own properties, subcategory by subcategory (a phone's model and battery, a laptop's processor, a TV's size and resolution...), and `category_attribute_options`: the options of a property each category offers, its own brands and storage sizes |
| `migrations/0022_saved_listings.sql` | The listings a signed-in user keeps with the heart over their photo (`saved_listings`) |
| `migrations/0023_more_properties.sql` | The properties every category lacked: a car's customs, plates, engine and power, a motorcycle's and a van's own, a tyre's size, a flat's bathrooms, condition and heating, a plot's permit, a job's contract, a service's areas, shoes' sizes, a pet's breed, a machine's hours...; what only some kinds of a category have moves down to them (a plot has no rooms, a bag no size), and central heating goes from a home's amenities to its kinds of heating |
| `migrations/0024_search_kinds.sql` | Words that name a kind of listing (shitje, shitet, shes; qira, qera; blej) find the listings of that kind, as well as those that say them: "golf 7 në shitje" finds the Golfs for sale |
| `migrations/0026_option_statistics.sql` | Statistics that tell the planner an option names its property (`CREATE STATISTICS ... (dependencies)` on `listing_attribute_values`): it took a filter on a car's make and fuel for a thousandth of the cars, and joined every match to pick the 25 newest (115 ms) rather than walking the newest cars (17 ms) |
| `migrations/0025_photo_copies.sql` | Photos kept as WebP: beside the full and card-sized copies, a middle-sized one (`middle_url`), a JPEG of the full one (`jpeg_url`) and a tiny copy for the pages (`placeholder`) |
| `migrations/0027_car_titles_with_engine_and_year.sql` | A car's title says its engine and year after its make and model ("Volkswagen Golf 1.6, 2016") rather than its body type and colour; every car's made again in one statement (23 s for 235 000 cars, where calling the function for each took three minutes) |
| `migrations/0028_category_names_with_a_slash.sql` | Categories' names join their two halves with "/" rather than "dhe" ("Shtëpi / kopsht", "Pjesë / aksesorë"), their slugs and addresses as they were; search reads a "/" as it read "dhe" (`web/named.rs`) |
| `migrations/0029_message_notices.sql` | An index of conversations by when their last message came (`idx_conversations_last_message`), which the notices of unread messages look through every minute for the last day's |
| `migrations/0030_saved_search_seen.sql` | Saved searches: when each was last opened (`last_seen_at`), which its count of new listings starts from, and one row per user and page with its filters (`idx_saved_searches_user_filters` on `filters`, `{"path", "query"}`) |
| `migrations/0031_listing_expiry.sql` | The index the expiry looks through (`idx_listings_expiry`): the listings up or reserved with an expiry, by when, in place of the schema's, which held every active listing, those without one too, and no reserved one |
| `migrations/0032_euro_rate.sql` | The euro rate's day (`exchange_rates.rate_date`) and when the listings were last worked out in euros at it (`normalized_at`, empty while that is due); a listing's `updated_at` left as it was by a statement that says so (`app.keep_updated_at`, `set_listing_updated_at`), as the re-pricing does |
| `migrations/0033_account_settings.sql` | The account a sign-in code adds its contact to (`login_codes.user_id`), for a phone number or an e-mail address added in "Cilësimet" |
| `migrations/0034_stores.sql` | What a store's page shows beyond the schema's `businesses`: its town (`location_id`), its address, its opening hours, whether its number has WhatsApp, and the files of its logo and cover in the store (`logo_key`, `cover_key`) |
| `migrations/0035_store_people.sql` | A store's invitations (`business_invitations`: a phone number or an e-mail address, the role, two weeks, one per store and contact), and `manages_listing()`, whether someone may change a listing: a private seller's own, or one of a store's people who post for its listings, whoever of them posted one, not someone who left |
| `migrations/0036_seller_activity.sql` | How quickly each seller answers (`seller_response_times`, a materialized view: over the last 90 days' conversations a buyer wrote in, how many, how many answered, and the middle time to the first answer), rebuilt every 15 minutes; and `users.updated_at` left as it was when only `last_seen_at` moves (`set_users_updated_at`) |
| `migrations/0037_listing_review.sql` | Why a listing waits for a moderator (`listings.review_reason`: `NEW_ACCOUNT` or `DUPLICATE_PHOTOS`), which the schema's `PENDING_REVIEW` and its index now serve |
| `migrations/0038_words_with_categories.sql` | One GIN index over a listing's category and its words (`idx_listings_active_words`, with `btree_gin`'s operators for the category), in place of the words' own: "these words in these categories" answered without reading a listing that doesn't match, for the searches that read their few matches through it (`planned`) |
| `migrations/0039_notice_tokens.sql` | The account's token in the links that stop its e-mail notices without signing in (`users.notice_token`, unique): made at random the first time an e-mail goes out (`db::notice_token`), gone with the account's contacts when it closes |
| `migrations/0040_promotions.sql` | `listings.highlighted_until`, which HIGHLIGHT's promotions are copied into as FEATURED's are into `featured_until`; the product that highlights a listing for a week (`HIGHLIGHT_7D`, a placeholder price as the others'); and `GRANT_PROMOTION` and `END_PROMOTION` among the moderators' actions |
| `migrations/0042_one_word_category_names.sql` | Every category's name one word where it can be (the user's ask of 2026-10-11): "Konsola lojërash" Konsola, "Goma / disqe" Rrota, "Makineri / biznes" Makineri, 32 in all ("Të tjera" stays), their slugs and addresses as they were; a search still names one by its old name's words (`FORMER_NAMES` in `web/named.rs`), and listings keep the old name among their search words until they are saved again |
| `migrations/0041_car_model_prices.sql` | What cars of a model ask, by year (`car_model_prices`, a materialized view): for each model and year among the cars up for sale at a price, those of that model two years either side, how many, and the quartiles of their prices in euros, where at least eight make one, cars for parts and starting prices left out; rebuilt every 15 minutes (about a second over the development database's 216 000 cars) |
| `tests/*.rs` but `schema.rs` | 193 request tests, in a file for each part of the site, a crate of its own each: each test sends requests to the app, with a database of its own, and checks the pages, redirects, cookies and what the database holds after |
| `tests/common/mod.rs` | What the request tests share, made `pub` (each file takes it in with `mod common;`): `MIGRATOR`, the migrations embedded once for every test of a file; the app as the tests run it, its photos in memory and a moderator of its own (`MODERATOR`); requests through the full router (`send`, `get`, `post_form`, `post_with_photos`) and what they answer (`TestResponse`); sessions and new sellers (`session_for`, `new_seller`); the listing form's fields (`listing_fields`, `car_fields`); and the readers of a page's cards and frames (`card_numbers`, `next_frame`, `section`, `positions`) |
| `tests/account.rs` | An account: signing in with a code and its limits, the number signed in with shown as verified, the cookie that tells the scripts someone is signed in, "Cilësimet", the notifications page and the e-mails' links that stop a kind of notice, closing an account |
| `tests/browse.rs` | The pages that list listings: the welcome page (its categories, the listings seen lately, the most seen and those recommended), a category's page (its way up, its subcategories), a seller's profile, the cards (small, big or a list), the results' count, order and following pages |
| `tests/design.rs` | The look the design system gives every page, from the stylesheet, the scripts and the frame: the one main colour and the accents, buttons, pills, fields and cards as the canvas draws them, the header, menu, rail and footer, the front page's tiles and search, a tap's mark, the stand-in a listing's page shows while it comes, the pickers |
| `tests/filters.rs` | The filters: the sheet and the row of dropdowns, the place, the zone and the price (its slider, quick choices, currencies and the euro rate), a category's properties, a part's vehicle, the chips that take each off |
| `tests/listings.rs` | A listing's page: its price, details and photos, its seller and how quickly they answer, the phone revealed, the listings like it and either side of it, the loan calculator, the price weighed against like cars or the zone's square metre, its map, a price lowered lately |
| `tests/map.rs` | The map page and a category's map card: a pin and its radius, the nearest first and the pages after, the town picked and kept, a dot for every result, what a page that finds nothing around its pin offers |
| `tests/messages.rs` | Messages between buyers and sellers: writing and answering, signing in first, the notices of a message left unread, the limits on new buyers, blocking a buyer, a business's listing |
| `tests/moderation.rs` | Moderators, and what readers report: the makes and models sellers typed, listings waiting for review, promotions, suspended accounts, a reported listing, a blocked seller's listings |
| `tests/posting.rs` | Posting a listing and looking after it: the form, its checks and the AI that fills it in, posting signed in or not and the limits, its zone and point on the map, its photos and their copies, editing, pausing, reserving, selling, deleting, renewing and expiring, "Njoftimet e mia" |
| `tests/properties.rs` | What the listing form asks of each kind of thing: electronics, property, clothes, pets, jobs, motorcycles, a car and the title made from it, a part and the vehicle it fits, the makes and models sellers type in |
| `tests/saved.rs` | What a reader keeps: a listing kept by its heart (before signing in too), a saved search and its alerts, the price drops those who keep a listing hear of |
| `tests/search.rs` | Searching by words: accents, typos and other forms of a word, the words that name a category, a kind, a town or a zone (in English too), the best matches first, the categories a search found, the suggestions |
| `tests/site.rs` | The site as a whole: its name, logo and icon, the pages about it and an address with nothing there, its static files, minified scripts and stylesheet, the headers that keep it safe, forms from other sites, what search engines and link previews get, English |
| `tests/stores.rs` | Stores: a store's page and its people, asked in by its owner, who post for it and share its conversations, whom its listing tells of its expiry, closing an account that owns one |
| `tests/schema.rs` | Runs `schema_checks.sql` against a freshly migrated database |
| `tests/schema_checks.sql` | 83 checks of the schema's constraints and behaviour (35 checks, 36 statements that must fail, 12 that must succeed), in one transaction rolled back at the end |
| `tests/fixtures/listings.sql` | The tests' sample listings, which can also be loaded into the development database |
| `dev/deploy.sh` | Builds the release and (re)starts it on `BIND_ADDR`, keeping the previous one to go back to |
| `dev/check.sh` | Clippy and every test side by side, as a commit needs them; given words, only the tests whose names hold them (see "Running it locally") |
| `dev/test_template.sh` | Makes the development database's `template1` a migrated copy of the schema, so the tests' databases start migrated (see "Running it locally"); run again when `migrations/` changes |
| `dev/setup_garage.sh`, `dev/garage.toml` | The local photo store's one-time setup, and its configuration |
| `dev/sample_listings.sql` | 305 extra sample listings for the development database (not used by the tests) |
| `dev/generate_listings.sql` | Generates as many realistic listings as you ask for, with sellers and photos, for trying the site at scale; removes them again with `remove=1`, gives listings with only a cover more photos with `photos=1`, puts property listings from earlier versions of it in their zones with `zones=1`, gives listings views with `views=1`, and lowers some of their prices with `drops=1` |
| `compose.yaml` | The development services: PostgreSQL 15 (port 5432), set for speed over durability (`fsync` and `synchronous_commit` off, lock memory and connections for the tests' databases, 256 MB of shared memory), and Garage (port 3900) |
| `.env.example` | Every setting, with values that work locally (see "Configuration") |
| `Cargo.toml` | The crates the site uses (see "Dependencies") |
| `.gitattributes` | LF line endings for migrations, which sqlx checksums byte by byte, and for shell scripts, which a carriage return breaks |
| `.sqlx/` | Cached query metadata, so the project builds without a database (set `SQLX_OFFLINE=true`) |
| `CHANGES.md` | What changed from the first version of the schema, and why |

## Routes

Every page and action, from `web::router`. Anything else is the 404 page. A form posted from another site is refused (the `Origin` check in `web/mod.rs`), and the forms that carry photos may be up to 64 MB; other requests up to 2 MB. Every response carries the security headers (`web::security_headers`, see "How the frontend works").

| Method | Path | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | The welcome page: the search, the main categories, "Shikuar së fundmi", "Më të klikuarat", then "Të rekomanduara për ty" and every other listing after them, newest first, or for a visitor who looked at none (or at too few with any like them) every listing, newest first; `?after=` is the newest's next page, `?sipas=…&nga=` the recommended's (their seeds, and where they go on from), `?sipas=…&after=` the newest's after them (`browse::home`) |
| GET | `/c/{slug}` | A category's page: its subcategories, the round buttons, "Më të klikuarat" (main categories and a few big subcategories, with no filter on), its listings, filtered by the query string (`browse::category`); words searched there that name a town, a kind of listing or, in property, a town's zone send it to the page with those filters on (`named.rs`); with `numri=1`, only how many it finds, up to 10 000, as JSON |
| GET | `/search` | A search, across every category or in the one `category` names, with the same filters and `numri=1` (`browse::search`); words that name a category, a kind of listing, a town or, in property, a town's zone send it on to the search with those filters on, the rest of the words searched there: "apartament me qera ne tirane" to `/search?category=apartamente&city=tirane&type=RENT`, "apartament 2+1 ne plazh" to `/search?q=2%2B1&category=apartamente&city=durres&zone=plazh` (`named.rs`) |
| GET | `/harta` | The map page: listings around a pin (`lat`, `lng`, `radius`), nearest first (`browse::map`) |
| GET | `/sugjerime?q=…` | The search boxes' suggestions, as rows of links (`suggestions::suggestions`) |
| GET | `/listing/{number}-{slug}` | A listing's page; an outdated slug redirects to the current one; one taken off the site by its reports or the moderators, or down for a while by its seller (paused), is for its seller and the moderators alone; one waiting for a moderator for them too, and for whoever just posted it, signed in or not, each told so; a refused one tells its seller why (`listings::show`) |
| GET, POST | `/listing/{number}/raporto` | Reporting the listing: the reasons, for someone signed in, and keeping the report (`safety::report_page`, `safety::report`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/blloko` | Blocks the seller behind the listing for the signed-in user, from the report page's thanks, which it goes back to, or lets them be again with `blloko=0`: the same block a conversation's makes (`safety::block`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/phone` | Reveals the seller's phone number or e-mail address, as a Turbo Stream, and counts it; beside a number WhatsApp can reach, a button that opens a chat with it about the listing (`listings::reveal_phone`, `listings::whatsapp_url`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/pare` | A listing's page was shown, for the view counts (`listings::seen`) |
| GET | `/listing/{number}/te-ngjashme?nga=…` | The next listings like one, from the `nga`th, in a frame for its page's infinite scroll (`listings::related`); asked for as a page, it leads back to the listing |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/ruaj` | Keeps the listing for the signed-in user, or lets it go with `ruaj=0` (`saved::save`) |
| GET, POST | `/shto` | The posting form, and publishing a listing, signed in or not: signed out, under the phone number or e-mail address typed, at once for one no account has (an account made with it), or for one an account has, once the code sent to it is typed on `/hyr/kodi`, which signs that account in (the listing waits as `DRAFT` meanwhile) (`post::new_listing`, `post::create_listing`) |
| GET | `/shto/fushat?category=…` | A category's property fields, for the form's frame (`properties::fields_for_category`) |
| POST | `/shto/ploteso` | What the AI made of the form's title, description and photos, as JSON (`ai_fill::fill`) |
| GET | `/shto/cmimi` | For the listing form, what cars of a model (`model`, its code: `VOLKSWAGEN_GOLF`) and year ask in a currency, in words, and how the price typed (`price`) will compare on the car's page, as JSON (`{"text", "verdict"}`); 204 with too few cars to say (`car_prices::hint`) |
| POST | `/shto/zona` | The zone a new listing's title or description names, with its town, as JSON (`{"city": "tirane", "zone": "blloku"}`, or `{}`), for its form (`post::guess_zone`) |
| GET, POST | `/listing/{number}/ndrysho` | Editing a listing, for its seller only (`post::edit_listing`, `post::update_listing`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/caktivizo` | Pausing a listing (`post::pause_listing`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/aktivizo` | Putting a paused or sold listing back up (`post::resume_listing`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/shitur` | The listing is sold, or let out for a rental ("U shit", "U dha me qira"): its page stays, saying so, and it leaves the lists; only for one on sale or for rent (`post::sell_listing`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/rezervo`, `/listing/{number}/zhrezervo` | The listing is reserved, still up and saying so, or the reservation is taken off; only for one on sale or for rent (`post::reserve_listing`, `post::unreserve_listing`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/rifresko` | Renewing a listing up, reserved or expired ("Rifresko"): 60 days from then, an expired one back up, and at the top of "newest first" again if it was last brought up a week ago or more (`post::renew_listing`) |
| POST | `/listing/{number}/fshi` | Deleting a listing (`post::delete_listing`) |
| GET | `/njoftimet-e-mia` | "Njoftimet e mia": the signed-in seller's listings, with what they drew, all together and each; `?dyqani=` a store's they post for, whoever of its people posted them (`post::my_listings`) |
| GET | `/te-ruajturat` | The listings the user keeps, the last kept first, under those whose price dropped since they last looked (`saved::page`) |
| POST | `/llogaria/zhblloko/{id}` | Lets someone the user blocked be again, from "Cilësimet", whichever page they were blocked on (`safety::unblock`) |
| GET | `/te-ruajturat/numrat` | For the hearts, as JSON: the numbers of those listings, how many dropped their price unseen, and how many saved searches have alerts unseen, for the rail's heart's dot (on phones the welcome page's, in the row with its search): `{"numbers": [1001, 1005], "price_drops": 1, "searches": 2}` (`saved::numbers`) |
| GET | `/kerkimet-e-ruajtura` | The visitor's saved searches, the last saved first, each with how many listings came since it was last opened, its alerts on or off and "Fshi"; `?plot=1` says they keep as many as they may (`searches::page`) |
| POST | `/kerkimet/ruaj` | Keeps the search at `adresa` (a category's or a search's page and its query) as its own page writes it, and goes back to it; signed out, signing in first, back to it after (`searches::save`) |
| POST | `/kerkimet/pare` | `/kerkimet-e-ruajtura` was shown: the alerts of the searches it lists are seen (`searches::seen`) |
| GET | `/kerkimet/{id}` | Opens a saved search, from its page or an alert: its new listings count from now, its alerts are seen, and it goes to the search's page (`searches::open`) |
| POST | `/kerkimet/{id}/fshi`, `/kerkimet/{id}/lajmerime` | Lets a saved search go, back to the page it was let go from; turns its alerts on (`lajmero=1`) or off (`searches::delete`, `searches::alerts`) |
| POST | `/te-ruajturat/pare?deri=…` | `/te-ruajturat` was shown with its price drops: they are seen, up to the latest it showed, by its notification's id (`saved::seen`) |
| GET | `/lajmerimet` | The user's notifications, the latest first, each leading to what it is about (`notifications::page`) |
| POST | `/lajmerimet/pare?deri=…` | `/lajmerimet` was shown with notifications unread: they are read, up to the latest it showed (`notifications::seen`) |
