| GET | `/lajmerimet/numri` | How many of the user's notifications are unread, and how many of those are about their own listings, as JSON, for the account button's dot: `{"unread": 3, "own": 1}` (`notifications::counts`) |
| GET, POST | `/listing/{number}/mesazh` | The page that starts a conversation with the listing's seller, and its first message; the conversation the visitor has about it already, if any (`messages::compose`, `messages::start`) |
| GET | `/mesazhet` | The visitor's conversations, the latest written in first, a store's among them for its people, marked with its name (`messages::inbox`) |
| GET | `/mesazhet/numri` | How many of the visitor's conversations have messages unread, as JSON, for the rail's "Mesazhet" and, on phones, the menu's button and the welcome page's in the row with its search: `{"unread": 2}` (`messages::unread`) |
| GET, POST | `/mesazhet/{id}` | A conversation, for its buyer and its seller, a store's people for a store's listing ("not found" for anyone else), and a message in it (`messages::conversation`, `messages::reply`) |
| GET | `/mesazhet/{id}/te-rejat?pas=…` | The messages after one, as a Turbo Stream that adds them at the page's end; 204 when none came (`messages::newer`) |
| POST | `/mesazhet/{id}/lexuar?deri=…` | The conversation's page showed its messages up to one: they are read (`messages::read`) |
| POST | `/mesazhet/{id}/blloko`, `/mesazhet/{id}/zhblloko` | Blocks the other side of the conversation, or lets them write again, whoever on a store's side blocked them (`messages::block`, `messages::unblock`) |
| GET | `/perdorues/{id}` | A private seller's profile and active listings (`browse::user_profile`) |
| GET | `/biznes/{slug}` | A business's page and active listings; a store's with its cover, its logo, what it says of itself, its address and hours, its number, WhatsApp, e-mail and website, and "Ndrysho dyqanin" for its owner and admins (`browse::business_profile`) |
| GET, POST | `/dyqani/krijo` | A new store: its form, and making it, its maker its owner, up to five an account owns (`stores::new_store`, `stores::create_store`) |
| GET, POST | `/dyqani/{slug}/ndrysho` | Changing a store, for its owner and admins, "not found" for anyone else (`stores::edit_store`, `stores::update_store`) |
| GET, POST | `/dyqani/{slug}/njerezit` | A store's people and its invitations waiting, for its owner and admins; asking someone in by a phone number or an e-mail address (`kontakti`) with a role (`roli`) (`stores::people`, `stores::invite`) |
| POST | `/dyqani/{slug}/njerezit/{user_id}`, `/dyqani/{slug}/njerezit/{user_id}/hiq` | Gives one of its people another role (`roli`), or lets them go, for whoever gives that role (`stores::set_role`, `stores::remove`) |
| POST | `/dyqani/{slug}/ftesat/{id}/anulo` | Withdraws one of its invitations (`stores::cancel`) |
| POST | `/dyqani/{slug}/largohu` | One of its people, not its owner, leaves it (`stores::leave`) |
| GET | `/ftesa/{id}` | An invitation into a store, while it is open: "Prano" and "Refuzo" for whoever signs in with its contact, which contact for anyone else; signed out, back here after signing in (`stores::invitation`) |
| POST | `/ftesa/{id}/prano`, `/ftesa/{id}/refuzo` | Takes it, one of the store's people with its role, or turns it down; to "Cilësimet" (`stores::accept`, `stores::decline`) |
| GET, POST | `/ndalo/{token}/{what}` | The link in an e-mail notice or alert that stops its kind, no signing in, the token its account's: "mesazhet", "kerkimi-{id}" or "email". Opened, it asks; posted, by its form or a mail program's one-click button (`List-Unsubscribe-Post`), it stops them; a token, a kind or a search not the account's is "not found" (`unsubscribe::ask`, `unsubscribe::stop`) |
| POST | `/ndalo/{token}/{what}/rikthe` | Takes it back: they come again (`unsubscribe::back`) |
| GET | `/llogaria` | "Llogaria": signing in or creating an account, or, signed in, the account (`account::account_page`) |
| POST | `/hyr` | Sends a sign-in code to a phone number or an e-mail address (`account::request_code`) |
| GET, POST | `/hyr/kodi` | The page for the code, and checking it: signs in, and puts a listing posted before signing in up, a draft or a new account's first listing waiting for a moderator (a draft with another seller's photo goes on to wait) (`account::code_page`, `account::check_code`) |
| POST | `/dil` | Signs out (`account::sign_out`) |
| GET | `/llogaria/cilesimet` | "Cilësimet", the account's settings, saying what a form did (`?u=emri`, `kontakti`, `hequr`, `njoftimet`, `pajisjet`, `zhbllokuar`) (`settings::page`) |
| POST | `/llogaria/emri` | The account's name (`settings::rename`) |
| POST | `/llogaria/kontakti` | A phone number or an e-mail address to add, or to take the place of the one of its kind: a code goes to it, and the code page puts it on the account (`settings::add_contact`) |
| POST | `/llogaria/kontakti/hiq` | Takes the phone number (`hiq=numri`) or the address (`hiq=email`) off, while the other stays (`settings::remove_contact`) |
| POST | `/llogaria/njoftimet` | What the account hears of, as its boxes say: `mesazhet`, `cmimet`, `kerkimet`, `email` (`settings::notices`) |
| POST | `/llogaria/pajisjet` | Signs the account out everywhere but here (`settings::other_devices`) |
| POST | `/llogaria/fshi` | Closes the account for good, once `konfirmo=FSHI` (`settings::close`) |
| POST | `/gjuha` | The language chosen (`gjuha=en` or `sq`), kept a year in the `gjuha` cookie and on a signed-in user's account (`users.preferred_language`); back to the page it was chosen on (`language::choose`) |
| GET | `/promovo` | The promotions on sale, what each does and costs, and how to get one; `njoftimi=1234` names the listing (`promotions::page`) |
| GET | `/si-funksionon`, `/siguria`, `/kushtet`, `/privatesia`, `/kontakt` | The pages about the site, from every page's footer: how it works, the safety tips, the terms of use, privacy (naming who runs the site, `OPERATOR`), and contact, with the address `CONTACT_EMAIL` names (`pages.rs`) |
| GET | `/moderim` | The moderators' page: the listings waiting, the reported listings, the names sellers typed in, the accounts suspended, then the promotions, the form that gives one (`njoftimi=1234` puts that number in it) and those on now (`moderation::page`) |
| POST | `/moderim/njoftim/{number}/mirato`, `/moderim/njoftim/{number}/refuzo` | Puts a waiting listing up, or refuses it for a reason (`arsye`) its seller reads (`moderation::approve_listing`, `moderation::reject_listing`) |
| POST | `/moderim/promovime` | Gives the listing with the number typed the promotion chosen; a number no listing up or reserved has, or no promotion chosen, is said beside the form (`moderation::grant_promotion`) |
| POST | `/moderim/promovime/{id}/ndalo` | Ends a promotion before its time, the listing losing what it gave (`moderation::end_promotion`) |
| POST | `/moderim/perdorues/{id}/pezullo`, `/moderim/perdorues/{id}/rikthe` | Suspends an account, never the moderator's own, for a reason (`arsye`): signed out, its listings and the stores it owns off the site; or restores it and them as they were (`moderation::suspend_user`, `moderation::restore_user`) |
| POST | `/moderim/njoftim/{number}/mbaje` | Keeps a reported listing: its reports dismissed, back on the site if they took it off (`moderation::keep`) |
| POST | `/moderim/njoftim/{number}/hiq` | Takes a reported listing off the site for good (`REJECTED`) (`moderation::remove`) |
| POST | `/moderim/{id}/mirato` | Approves an option sellers typed in, its spelling put right (`moderation::approve`) |
| POST | `/moderim/{id}/bashko` | Merges it into the listed one it is (`moderation::merge`) |
| GET | `/media/{key}` | A stored photo, WebP or JPEG, which browsers may keep for a year (`photos::serve`); unless a CDN serves them (`MEDIA_URL`) |
| GET | `/static/css/site.css` | The site's stylesheets as one, in their order and minified, without comments or white space the browser doesn't need (`web::site_css`), the only one pages load; kept a year when asked for with the fingerprint (`?v=`), as the rest of `/static` |
| GET | `/static/js/…`, `/static/vendor/turbo-8.0.23.js`, `/static/vendor/stimulus-3.2.2.js` | A script a page loads, the site's own module or one of the two libraries, minified (`web::script`, `web::minified_js`): without its comments and white space, its own names shortened, in fewer bytes to the same ends (Turbo 26 KB gzipped rather than 45); minified when first asked for and again once its file changes; kept a year with the fingerprint, as the rest of `/static` |
| GET | `/static/…` | The CSS, the JavaScript, the vendor files, the icons and the manifest: the vendor files, and the site's own CSS and JavaScript asked for with their fingerprint (`?v=`), kept by browsers for a year without asking again; anything else checked on each use (`web::cache_headers`) |
| GET | `/favicon.ico` | The site's icon (`static/img/favicon.ico`), where browsers and apps look for it without asking a page; checked on each use |
| GET | `/healthz` | "ok" when the server and its database answer (`dev/deploy.sh` checks it) |
| GET | `/robots.txt` | Where crawlers may go: anywhere but a visitor's own pages (signing in, "Llogaria", "Njoftimet e mia", the kept listings, the saved searches, the notifications, the moderators' page, the forms, a store's among them, the invitations, the links that stop e-mails) and the ones only scripts ask for; and where the sitemaps are (`seo::robots`) |
| GET | `/sitemap.xml` | The sitemaps: the pages', and the listings', one for every 50 000 listing numbers (`seo::sitemap_index`) |
| GET | `/sitemaps/{name}` | `faqet.xml`: the welcome page, the pages about the site, every category's and the businesses' with listings; `njoftimet-{n}.xml`: the active listings numbered from n × 50 000 on, each with the day it last changed (`seo::sitemap`) |

## Configuration

The server reads `.env` at start (`.env.example` has every setting, with values that work locally). A channel or service left out is turned off, and the log says so.

| Setting | Default | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `DATABASE_URL` | none (required) | The PostgreSQL database; sqlx's compile-time checks read it too, unless `SQLX_OFFLINE=true` |
| `BIND_ADDR` | `127.0.0.1:3000` | Where the server listens |
| `RUST_LOG` | `marketplace=info,tower_http=info` | What the log says, by module (`.env.example` has `marketplace=debug`) |
| `PUBLIC_URL` | the address each request came to | The site's address (`https://pluribus.al`), which the links it gives of itself start with: canonical addresses, link previews' addresses and photos, the sitemaps and `robots.txt`, and the links in the notices of unread messages, which have no request to go by (`web::site_origin`; without it, http://127.0.0.1:3000). Unset, a request's `Host`, over https when the proxy in front says so (`X-Forwarded-Proto`), as Cloudflare's tunnel does |
| `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_USERNAME`, `SMTP_PASSWORD`, `EMAIL_FROM` | none | Sign-in codes by e-mail, through any SMTP service; all four or none |
| `SMTP_PORT` | `587` | 587 uses STARTTLS, 465 TLS |
| `WHATSAPP_TOKEN`, `WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID`, `WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE` | none | Sign-in codes to phone numbers by WhatsApp: Meta's Cloud API, a system user's permanent token, and an approved authentication template with a "copy code" button |
| `WHATSAPP_LANGUAGE` | `sq` | The template's language |
| `WHATSAPP_API_VERSION` | `v21.0` | The Graph API's version |
| `WHATSAPP_NOTICE_TEMPLATE` | none | The notices of unread messages to phone numbers by WhatsApp: an approved template of category Utility, its body naming who wrote (`{{1}}`) and about what (`{{2}}`), and a URL button whose address ends in the conversation (`https://pluribus.al/mesazhet/{{1}}`). Without it, phone numbers get no notices (the log has them) |
| `WHATSAPP_SEARCH_TEMPLATE` | none | The alerts of saved searches to phone numbers by WhatsApp: a Utility template whose body says how many new listings (`{{1}}`, "3 njoftime të reja") a search of what name (`{{2}}`) has, as "Ka {{1}} te kërkimi yt „{{2}}“ në Pluribus.", and whose URL button opens the search, its address ending in its id (`https://pluribus.al/kerkimet/{{1}}`). Without it, phone numbers see the alerts in the site alone (the heart's dot, "Lajmërimet") |
| `WHATSAPP_EXPIRY_TEMPLATE` | none | The warnings of listings about to expire or expired to phone numbers by WhatsApp: a Utility template whose body names the listing (`{{1}}`) and what becomes of it (`{{2}}`: "skadon më 16.10.2026", or "skadoi"), as "Njoftimi yt „{{1}}“ në Pluribus {{2}}. Rifreskoje që të qëndrojë 60 ditë të tjera.", and whose URL button opens `https://pluribus.al/njoftimet-e-mia`, the same for all. Without it, phone numbers see them in the site alone (the account button's dot, "Lajmërimet") |
| `S3_ENDPOINT`, `S3_BUCKET`, `S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` | none | The photo store (see "Photos"); without them photos are kept in memory, and lost when the server stops |
| `S3_REGION` | `auto` | The store's region: `auto` for R2, `garage` for the local Garage |
| `MEDIA_URL` | `/media` | Where pages say the photos are: under `/media` the site serves them itself; a CDN's address that serves the bucket (an R2 bucket's public domain, say) has it serve them (see "Photos") |
| `SIGN_IN_CODES_ON_SCREEN` | `false` | Test mode: the sign-in page shows the code instead of sending it, so anyone can sign in as any number; only for trying the site before a channel is set up |
| `TYPE_TRIAL` | `false` | Trying typefaces and icons: the top bar gets an "Ff" button (none on screens under 400 px, which have no room left) whose panel picks the headings' and the text's typeface, the site's own and Google Fonts', and their weights, and an icon library (Lucide, Tabler, Phosphor, Heroicons) in place of the site's own drawings, for that browser alone (`type_trial_controller.js`); the policy then lets Google Fonts' stylesheets and files and unpkg's icon files in. Only while the site's look is being settled |
| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | none | Turns on "Plotëso pjesën tjetër me AI" on the listing form, new or edited |
| `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` | `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` | The model that fills in the form |
| `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` | `https://api.anthropic.com` | Where Anthropic's API is; the tests point it at a stand-in |
| `MODERATORS` | none | The phone numbers and e-mail addresses, comma-separated, of those who may decide on reported listings and approve what sellers typed in, on `/moderim`, and see the listings the reports took off the site |
| `CONTACT_EMAIL` | none | The e-mail address the contact page (`/kontakt`) and the privacy page give; without one, the contact page gives no address, only the quicker ways for what has one of its own (Raporto, Blloko), and the privacy page points to it |
| `OPERATOR` | none | Who runs the site, a person's name or a company's, whom the privacy page names as responsible for the data. Albania's law on personal data (no. 124/2024) asks the page to say who that is and how to reach them: set it, and `CONTACT_EMAIL`, before the site is public |
| `SQLX_OFFLINE` | unset | At build time: `true` builds from `.sqlx/` without a database |

Without e-mail or WhatsApp set up, sign-in codes go to the server's log.

## Dependencies

No Node, no npm, no bundler: the browser's code is plain JavaScript modules, with Turbo, Stimulus and Leaflet in `static/vendor/`. The server's crates (`Cargo.toml`):

| Crate | What it does here |
|---|---|
| `axum` (with `multipart`) | The web server and the router; `multipart` for the posting form's photos |
| `tokio` | The async runtime: the server, the background refresh, the views' writing, the notices, the saved searches' alerts and the listings' expiry |
| `tower-http` | Static files, request logs, gzip and Brotli compression |
| `sqlx` | PostgreSQL: queries checked against the schema at compile time (`query!`), built at run time where filters are optional (`QueryBuilder`), and the migrations |
| `askama` | Templates compiled into Rust and checked at compile time |
| `serde`, `serde_json` | JSON: the AI's answers, the hearts' numbers, stored properties |
| `rust_decimal` | Prices and properties' numbers, without a float's rounding |
| `time` | Dates and times |
| `uuid` | Ids (UUIDv7, sortable by when they were made) |
| `image` | Decoding photos, turning them the right way up and resizing them; the cards' AVIF copies (its `avif` feature: ravif and rav1e, Rust's AV1 encoder, without the assembly NASM would build); quick JPEGs for the AI |
| `libwebp-sys` | Photos as lossy WebP, by Google's libwebp, built from its C sources |
| `mozjpeg`, `mozjpeg-sys` | Each photo's JPEG, progressive, by Mozilla's encoder, built from its C sources without its SIMD code (which needs NASM) |
| `object_store` | The S3-compatible photo store |
| `sha2` | Photos' checksums, and sign-in codes and session tokens stored hashed |
| `oxc_parser`, `oxc_minifier`, `oxc_codegen` (with `oxc_allocator` and `oxc_span`) | [Oxc](https://oxc.rs/), JavaScript's tools in Rust: the scripts minified as the server serves them (`web::minified_js`), which needs Rust 1.97 |
| `base64` | Photos sent to the AI, and the tiny copies in the pages |
| `lettre` | E-mail by SMTP |
| `reqwest` | HTTP calls to WhatsApp's API and Anthropic's, and the Bank of Albania's rates page |
| `rand` | Sign-in codes and session tokens |
| `futures-util` | Streams: listing the photo store's files, as `media.rs` looks for photos missing copies |
| `form_urlencoded` | Query strings |
| `dotenvy` | Reading `.env` |
| `anyhow` | Startup errors |
| `tracing`, `tracing-subscriber` | The log |
| `tower`, `http-body-util` (tests only) | Sending requests to the router and reading the answers |

## How the frontend works

- Every page is plain HTML rendered by the server, so it loads fast and search engines index it as it is.
- The site speaks Albanian, and English for whoever chooses it: "English" at every page's foot ("Shqip" back), and in "Cilësimet", keeps the choice a year in a cookie (`gjuha`) and on a signed-in user's account; one made before signing in becomes the account's (`web/language.rs`). A visitor who hasn't chosen and whose browser reads no Albanian (a foreigner's, or the phone of someone abroad) is offered English at the top of the page, in English, until they choose either. Every text a page or a script says goes through `t` (`src/i18n.rs`, `i18n.js`), keyed by its Albanian, which is what the templates read as and what a page says where the catalogue has no English (`i18n/en/`, which `build.rs` makes a sorted table of); so do the database's own labels, at render (the categories, the properties' names, their choices and units, the promotions: `data.txt`), and a property's value by `t_value` ("148 000 km", "6,1 inç", "1 month"). A test fails the checks for any text given `t` without its English. The language is a task-local the language layer sets around each request, so templates and handlers need nothing passed; numbers take a decimal point in English and dates read "4 Oct 2026". The pages about the site have their English whole, the terms' and the privacy page's saying the Albanian applies. What goes out on its own speaks the language kept on its recipient's account: the notices of unread messages, the saved searches' alerts, the expiry warnings, a store's invitation by e-mail; a sign-in code's e-mail speaks that of the page that asked for it. WhatsApp's templates stay Albanian, as Meta approved them. The notifications keep their titles in Albanian, as the site wrote them, and the notifications' page words them anew for an English reader. What people wrote (titles, descriptions, messages, names), the names of towns and brands, and the moderators' pages stay as they are. Search reads Albanian words, and on an English page English's for a category, a kind and the words that join them ("flat for rent in Tirana"); numbers are read as either language writes them ("9,500", "12.5"). The addresses stay the same in both languages, and search engines read the Albanian.
- The pages are in Albanian and say so (`<html lang="sq">`), so a browser with a translator of its own offers to translate them for a visitor who reads another language: Chrome and Edge do, Google's and Microsoft's translators knowing Albanian. What a translator would turn into ordinary words keeps its name (`translate="no"`): people's and stores' names (a card's seller, the seller card's name, a profile's or a store's heading, a conversation's other side, the inbox's names; "Besa" would come out "Faith", "Drita" "Light"), places (a card's place line and town, a listing's place and its map's: "Blloku" would come out "the Block", "Kombinat" "combine"), the wordmark, and a revealed number or address. Prices stay translatable: a translator keeps the figures, and "Çmimi me marrëveshje" is better read translated. An English interface of the site's own would need every page's words in two languages, and the notifications, which are kept as finished sentences, kept otherwise; the categories' and properties' names have their `*_i18n` columns waiting.
- Every page says what it is in its head (`web/seo.rs`, `meta.html`): to search engines, a description and its canonical address; to the apps that preview a shared link (WhatsApp, Facebook, Messenger, X, Telegram, iMessage...), the Open Graph tags they show it with. A listing's preview has its title and price ("Mercedes-Benz C 220, 2018, automatike · 21 500 € (i diskutueshëm)"), where it is, what kind of listing and in what condition followed by the start of its description ("Durrës · Në shitje · E përdorur — Importuar nga Gjermania..."), and its first photo; a category's, its name, how many listings it has and its first listing's photo; a seller's profile, their name, since when they are on the site and how many listings they have. The welcome page, and any page without a photo of its own, show the site's picture (`static/img/pluribus.png`: the wordmark and "Njoftime në gjithë Shqipërinë" under it on the page's grey, 1200 × 630, both small enough to stay whole where an app crops the picture square). A listing with a price also gives it as `product:price` tags, and one on sale tells Google what it sells, as schema.org data: a Product and its Offer, with the price, the currency, the condition, whether it is still for sale (in stock, reserved, sold) and the seller, a person or an organization.
- A category page's canonical address keeps its filters but not its order, its page or the map's dots, so `/c/makina?sort=price_asc` counts as `/c/makina` and `/c/makina?vehicle_fuel=DIESEL` as a page of its own. A search's results (`/search`, the map page, a category's or a seller's searched with words) and a sold listing ask search engines to leave them out of their results (`noindex, follow`) but to follow their links to the listings. `/robots.txt` keeps crawlers out of a visitor's own pages and names `/sitemap.xml`, which lists the pages' sitemap (the welcome page, every category's, and the businesses' with listings) and the listings', one for every 50 000 listing numbers (Google reads at most 50 000 addresses from one), each listing with the day it last changed. Every address they give starts with `PUBLIC_URL` (see "Configuration") or, without it, the address the request came to.
- Turbo fetches pages and form responses in the background and swaps them in without a full reload. It also prefetches links on hover.
- Listing pages load the next page as the visitor scrolls near the end. A `<turbo-frame loading="lazy">` points at the next page, and the server answers that request with just the frame. Asked for without the frame (by a search engine, or a link opened anew), the same address is the whole page, so every page says it differs with `Turbo-Frame` (`Vary: Turbo-Frame, Cookie, Accept-Language`, `web::render`, the language's cookie and the browser's languages too), and a cache keeps the two apart rather than giving the frame to a visitor or a search engine. Cloudflare caches no HTML unless a rule tells it to, and then takes `Vary` into account only with its Cache Rules' Vary setting; the pages differ with cookies too (the town remembered, the cards' view, the sellers blocked, the language), so their HTML isn't for a shared cache as they are.
- Small updates come back as Turbo Streams. For example, the phone button ("Shfaq numrin") swaps in the phone number and counts the reveal.
- The little JavaScript there is lives in Stimulus controllers in `static/js/controllers/`, like the share button.
- Under a mouse, Turbo fetches a link ahead as the pointer rests on it; on a phone, a link starts fetching as soon as a finger touches it, any link Turbo would fetch ahead under a mouse (to another page of the site, not opened elsewhere, nor one that opts out with `data-turbo-prefetch="false"`, as "Raporto" does, nor one that leads a frame inside the page), and the visit the tap makes takes that response (`application.js`), so the page is on its way while the finger is still down: a category's pill, the chip that takes a filter off, "Shiko të gjitha" as well as a listing. A finger that moves, scrolling, drops it. A tap's head start is only the tap's own length, though, about a tenth of a second, where a mouse resting on a link gives a click most of a second, and a page's round trip is longer than a tap; so on a touch screen the page behind a link is fetched ahead once the link has been on screen for 75 ms: one the visitor stops on, not every one a swipe flicks past (a link that leaves sooner starts its wait again when it comes back; the cards' listings, the categories' pills), two at a time, the next as one finishes, and at most twelve a page, quietly, each kept half a minute for the visit a tap makes, as the touch's own is: not where the visitor asks to save data, nor on a connection the browser calls slow (`navigator.connection`).
- A listing's first photo loads ahead of anything else on its page (`fetchpriority="high"`), so it shows as soon as it can. So does the first photo of a page's first row or grid of cards (`ListingPage::lead`): on the welcome page the first of "Shikuar së fundmi", or without it of "Më të klikuarat", or without either the first of the listings under them (those recommended, or the newest); on a category page the first of its "Më të klikuarat", whose first two photos load straight away, or without it the results' first (not on the map page, whose map comes first). On a phone that is the biggest thing on the first screen, which Lighthouse times as the page's largest contentful paint.
- A listing's photos glow, as YouTube's ambient light takes the colours of what plays (the user's ask of 2026-10-11, "a blur / shadow similar to how youtube hues its background based on whats its playing"): behind them the one that shows once more, its tiny copy, which is in the page, in a box past the photos' own (on a car's or a property's page 64 px over the mosaic, 130 under it and 24 at either side; on the others' 64 over the tall photo, 100 under it, 24 at its left and 110 under the column beside it; on a phone 72 over, 150 under and the page's margin at its sides, so that the page grows no wider), enlarged a quarter, blurred 80 px with its colours livelier, 140%, and fading to nothing at the box's edge (`.listing-glow` in `listing.css`; `--glow`, `--glow-fade`, a radial mask, and `--glow-strength` in `base.css`, 40% on the light theme and 55% on the dark: the PluribusGoogle canvas's values, which the user asked for on 2026-10-11, "what values should i use to recreate the background blur in html? ... implement ONLY THAT for each listing type"; until then the blur's own spread, 110 px and 170% livelier over the box and 24 px past it, at 26% on both themes, which the user had tuned down from 45% and 60%, "waaaaay more subtle", through 12%, "a bit more in white", and 22%), so that the page round them, under the words, and the bar's frost take a hint of their colours. As the photos slide, the next one's colours fade in over the last's (`slides_controller.js`, `shine`); the stand-in a tap shows has its card's photo there (`listingStandIn`).
- Pages change with the browser's cross-fade (view transitions, `<meta name="view-transition">` in `base.html`), shortened to a tenth of a second (`base.css`): the browser's own quarter of a second, during which the page takes no taps, made each click feel slow. On touch screens a page comes in as a drawer instead, as Chrome and a phone's apps bring theirs (the user's ask of 2026-10-11): the next page slides in from the right edge over the one left, which drifts a quarter of the way left and dims under it, and going back by a button the page left slides away to the right over the one come back to, a quarter of a second easing out (`base.css`, by the way Turbo marks on `<html>` while a visit lasts, `data-turbo-visit-direction`), at once as the finger lifts: a page that hasn't come yet slides in as a stand-in (for any link that leads the page elsewhere, those not fetched ahead too, such as the report's; not for one that only changes the page's address, a filter's chip or a next page, `standable`), which the page itself replaces as it comes, once the slide is over (`application.js`, `standIn`: Turbo shows a visit's `snapshotHTML` as it shows a page it kept, the page's bar, as the page coming has it (a listing's with its search folded behind the magnifier, any other's with it open, and none with the front page's "Posto", its bar's alone), over grey shapes, `.stand-in`, and a listing's the listing page's own frame, which `listing.css` lays out as it lays out the page, so that the page fills it where it stands (`listingStandIn`, the user's ask of 2026-10-11, "not a lot of page shifting"): its path's row with the steps' circles and the listing's path in words (the category above its own and its own, `data-up` and `data-here` on the card, from `ListingCard::category_name` and `category_parent`, which `CARD_COLUMNS` reads; the user's ask, "you also know the category for the breadcrumb"), the card's photo in the photo's frame, 4:3 for a car or a property and 6:5 for the others on a phone (the card says which, `data-deal` on `.card-head`, from `ListingCard::deal`, which `CARD_COLUMNS` reads from the category, under `prona` or `makina`), its glow behind it (`.listing-glow`, the card's photo too), the title the page heads itself with (a car's as its seller wrote it where the card's was made from what the car is, `data-heading`, `ListingCard::heading`), the line under it in the page's own words, shown as words since the user's ask of 2026-10-11, its number whole on one line as the page has it (`data-line`, `ListingCard::page_line`, so that it wraps as the page's does: a property's has no condition, a car's has), a car's or a property's key facts, as many as its page has (`data-facts`, `ListingCard::facts`), the price with the circles at its line's end that the page has (the loan calculator's, `data-loan`, and the one that weighs the price, `data-compare`, `ListingCard::compares`, grey until the page colours it), the details as grey pills where the page has them under it, the description in the page's words, its lines as they are, under the page's heading, folded by the page's own controller at a dozen lines with "Shfaq të gjithë përshkrimin" under them (the card carries up to 1 600 characters of it, cut at a word, `data-description`, `PAGE_DESCRIPTION`, `ListingCard::stand_in_description`, more than a dozen lines at any width and text size; `fold_controller.js` is registered on every page, so that the stand-in is folded before it is first drawn), and the contact bar with the page's pills, their icons and words, the phone's or the e-mail's as the page offers (`data-reveal`, from `listing_contacts` as `db::visible_contact` reads it) and the message's (none where `data-closed`; the user's ask of the same day, "the chat and phone number button as we already know their value"), the parts as the server words them in the page's language (`#stand-in-parts`, a `<template>` in `base.html`, inert), what the card doesn't know grey bars where the words will be (`.stand-in-text`, words about as long, unseen, so that they wrap as the page's do); on a phone its contact bar at the screen's foot, with the floating bar's shadow, as the page's stands there (the page's is sticky, last in a column that runs on under the screen; the stand-in's column is short, and on a small page or text size it ended over the foot, the bar floating there until the page dropped it down, up to 140 px), and the loan's circle, empty, at its line's top (on the words' baseline it took the line's descent too, the price's row up to 7 px taller than the page's), so that nothing moves as the page fills it at any of the menu's page and text sizes (measured at all sixteen on a 390 px phone; the user's report of 2026-10-11, "on some settings site jumps"); and the card's copy of the photo softly blurred, in the stand-in and on the listing's page until its own photo has loaded over it (`.gallery-interim`, the user's ask: "a lower resolution photo which you can use as blur on the next page"), the page's own unseen until it has loaded whole and then fading in over the blur in a fifth of a second, where it drew itself in from the top down as its bytes came, a sharp edge sweeping down the blur (`.is-coming`, marked as the page shows, `turbo:render`, on a photo the browser doesn't have at hand yet; the user's report of 2026-10-11, "a glitch when photo loads"); the user's ask, "instant as soon as I click, fill the info as it comes"); a page that only changes in place still changes at once, as does a back the browser animated itself. From 2026-10-09 until then touch screens had no change at all (`application.js` took the meta off each page as it showed): for any of them the browser draws both pages whole first, which a phone's slower CPU draws out (on a phone's layout a visit rendered in 40 ms without it and 160 with, on a computer), and the fade itself was barely seen on a phone.
- The welcome page (`/`) asks what the visitor is looking for (the question, "Çfarë po kërkon?", is there for screen readers; the eye sees the search and the categories), in words only, as the user drew it on 2026-10-11, Material's search bar as the pluribus-tests canvas's front page draws it (the user's ask of the same day, "make search bar google style like in the design"): a grey pill with no outline or shadow (`--surface-muted`) 64 px tall, 56 px on phones, that asks "Çfarë po kërkon?" in the muted grey 24 px in from its start, its words 18 px (16 px on phones), ending, 8 px in, in its search button, 48 px to tap (40 px on phones): the magnifier alone, 22 px (20 px), on no circle, dark on the light theme and in the accent on the dark one (`--highlight-icon`; with any of the six accents, the royal blue's a lighter blue there), a grey circle under the pointer only (the user's asks of 2026-10-11, "remove the circle for the search icon. Make it darker on light theme": it stood dark on a circle in the accent on the light theme and in the accent on a grey a shade off the pill's on the dark one, `--search-knob`; the pill was 50 px tall with its words 17 px and a button of 38 px, and 46 px on phones with a button of 36 px), its edge clear, there for where the system draws the colours (`forced-colors`), staying as it is in use, Material's way, with no edge or ring round it (the user's ask of 2026-10-11; it turned the fields' white with the main colour's edge and a ring round it before, and the pill was the surface with a hairline and the soft shadow once, and the page's colour with white words before that), and searches every category and town; the results' filters narrow them. The pill is alone in a row of its own over the categories (`.welcome-tools` in `welcome.css`), in the middle from 720 px up, where the rail has posting, the listings kept and the conversations (below), and the page's width on phones. There "Posto", the site's main thing to do, leading to the posting form, is in the top bar, its plus alone just before the account on a grey circle of 36 px (40 px to tap; `--surface-muted`, the darker grey under the pointer or a finger), the plus dark on the light theme and in the accent on the dark one, as the search's magnifier (`--highlight-icon`; `.header-post` in `base.css`, the `header_post` block, which `home.html` alone fills), quiet at the user's word of the same day ("tone the plus button down": it was a circle in the accent with the plus dark on it, as the buttons that do a page's thing are, `--post`), named "Posto njoftim" for screen readers as the rail's is, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 ("put the plus icon near the account one"); from 720 px up the bar has none, the rail having it. Until then it stood after the pill, round, 46 px, its word kept for screen readers, the pill taking the width it left (`.welcome-post`; the main colour with white words on the light theme until earlier that day; on wider screens a pill 50 px tall with its word once). A stand-in that a tap on the front page shows while another page comes (`standIn`, above) leaves "Posto" out of the bar it copies, no other page's bar having it. After "Posto", until 2026-10-11, the listings kept (the heart, with its dot for prices dropped) and the conversations (the speech bubble, with its count of those unread), grey circles of 50 px, which went at the user's word, the menu having both (the heart wore the site's red once, a kept listing's heart's; the theme stood among them once, marked in the blue under its moon and the yellow under its sun). Under the row the categories, one line of tiles that slides sideways, each its icon on one of Material's soft shapes over its name (below; 96 px wide from 720 px up, where the search over them stands in the middle, 720 px at most, and 84 px on phones, the line running from the screen's edge to its edge: the user's asks of 2026-10-11), the shape a darker grey, the icon filled with the accent and the name underlined in it under the pointer or a finger, and a tapped tile keeping them until its page shows (`.shortcut.pressed`, which `application.js` sets; as the menu's and the rail's icons fill); then the sections, 26 px apart, each under a section title of 22 px (19 px on phones): "Shikuar së fundmi" with "Pastro" at its end, "Më të klikuarat" and the newest listings, or "Të rekomanduara për ty" in their place (below). Every page's top bar has the account alone at its right end, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (a bare round button, 40 px, its icon 22 px, a grey circle under the pointer; `.account-button` in `base.css`): the theme and the size are the menu's, and posting, the listings kept and the conversations the rail's (below), so that every page reaches them in a tap, the listing's page a seller reads a buyer's message from too; on phones, which have no rail, the menu holds them, its button carrying the count of conversations unread, and the front page's bar posting too, beside the account (above; the welcome page's row had the listings kept and the conversations too, until the user asked them away later on 2026-10-11; the bar had the text's size, the listings kept, the conversations and the theme, a moon or a sun, until then). On a phone a finger's target is bigger than any of the round buttons, as a pill's is. (The search waited behind a round button among the categories' pills once, opening over their line in glass, `--glass` in `base.css`, which a card's heart still wears over a photo.) Under the row, tiles lead straight to every main category, all at once, Automjete, Prona, Elektronikë and Veshje first (`MAIN_ON_WELCOME` in `browse.rs`), then the others in their order, on one line that slides sideways where the screen is narrower than they are, its scrollbar unseen (a finger or a trackpad slides it, a mouse drags it, `drag_row_controller.js`), as the user drew it on 2026-10-11 after the Material front page of the pluribus-tests canvas, in the site's accent: each its icon (`icons::main_category`, in `icons.rs` with every other icon; a main category added later gets a box until it has one) on a shape in the grey of secondary controls, Material's six soft shapes in turn as the canvas has them (cookies of nine lobes, four, seven and six, nine scallops, twelve: `clip-path: path()`, the board's outlines resampled to 72 points), its name under it, medium, on two lines at most; under the pointer, a finger or once tapped the shape takes the darker grey, the icon fills with the accent and the name is underlined in it. From 720 px up the tiles are 96 px wide, 28 px under the search, which stands in the middle, 720 px at most, and spread over the line where it has room, their shapes 64 px, their icons 28 px and their names 13 px; on phones, as the canvas's phone board has them (the user's ask of the same day, "do the phone too"), 20 px under the search, the line from the screen's edge to its edge with the first shape level with the page's side, their shapes 56 px (the same outlines, drawn smaller), their icons 26 px and their names 12 px, 84 px wide (the board's 78 px left none cut at the edge of the user's 317 px screen, nothing there saying that the line slides): three and a half to a line there and four and a half on a 390 px one, the one cut at the edge saying that it slides. The tiles are one `pill` macro in `home.html` (`.shortcut-shape`, `.shortcut-name`). (Until then they were pills like the listing page's details, all in one list that wrapped wherever a line ended, 44 px tall, and on a phone 36 px on three or four lines, each with a finger's target bigger than the pill, half the gaps round it.) A category page's subcategories are the same size on a phone, and its path, and a listing's, on every screen, their round buttons and icons too (`browse.css`, below).
- As words are typed in the welcome pill or the browse pages' search box, a white card opens 8 px under the bar's, as the canvas draws it (as wide as the box; what floats' shadow, no outline, no sliding in), and the welcome pill's search opens as Google's does (below), with suggestions, each row 48 px tall with a magnifier in the muted grey and the parts that meet the words typed bold (`Suggestions::marked`: lower case and accents aside, "shtepi" in "Shtëpi"; words of one letter meet nothing) (`suggest_controller.js`, which asks `/sugjerime?q=…`, `web/suggestions.rs`): the last word finished as listings use it, the most used first ("gol" → "golf"), or, typed whole and misspelt, the word it most likely misspells; the categories the first of those finds most in ("golf · në Makina", to `/c/makina?q=golf`); the makes, models and brands the words name, which lead to their filters in the category most of their listings are in ("Volkswagen Golf · në Makina", to `/c/makina?vehicle_make=VOLKSWAGEN&vehicle_model=VOLKSWAGEN_GOLF`), a brand of the very name the words have replacing the words searched in its category; and the towns the last word starts, all their listings or, after other words, those words there ("apartament · në Tiranë", to `/search?q=apartament&city=tirane`), without the word that joins them to the town, which the town's filter says ("apartament në tir", typed with its ë or without, or with "te", "nga", "afër"...); and after words and such a word ("apartament në"), the three towns those words have the most listings in (`db::listing_towns`), rather than words starting "ne". The box keeps the focus: the arrow keys mark a row (`aria-activedescendant`), Enter follows it or, with none marked, searches the words as typed, Escape closes the list; a tap follows a row as any link. Suggestions come 120 ms after the last key, and those fetched are kept for the words they are for, so deleting a letter shows them at once. An empty box offers the visitor's last searches instead, a clock before each one's words, each leading back to the page it searched on (the address "Ruaj kërkimin" keeps, which a search with words gives in its results' `data-search-words` and `data-search-address`), the latest first, each page once, six at most, kept in the browser alone (`localStorage`, `recent-searches`; `recent_searches.js`, which `application.js` adds to as a search shows), and last "Pastro kërkimet e fundit", which forgets them, the list named for which it holds ("Sugjerime", "Kërkimet e fundit"); while the welcome pill has words, a cross before its magnifier empties it and shows them again (`.search-clear`); those rows are made of elements rather than HTML, the words being the visitor's own. The welcome pill's search opens as Google's does, after the pluribus-tests canvas's Material boards (the user's ask of 2026-10-11, "when search is clicked make it open like google search bar"): from 720 px up the pill and its list are one card while the list shows (`.has-list`, which `suggest_controller.js` sets on the form: the list under the pill in its grey, a hairline between them, the card's corners 28 px, one shadow round the two, a `drop-shadow` on the form); on a phone the search opens over the whole screen as its words get the focus (`.is-open`): the screen the pill's grey, its row 72 px at the top, the way back first (`.search-back`, the site's chevron, 48 px to tap, "Mbyll kërkimin"), then the words, the cross while there are some and the magnifier; under a hairline the last searches or the suggestions, the rest of the screen to scroll in, the page under it still (its place in the row kept, 56 px, and the root's scroll held, `html:has(.welcome-form.is-open)`). The way back, Escape or Android's back (a `CloseWatcher`, where the browser has one) closes it, as the page being left does (`turbo:before-cache`, `dismiss`); the keyboard put away leaves it open, the list with it. There the rows are 56 px tall, their words 16 px and their icons 20 px, a category named 8 px after the words, the marked row the darker grey, and "Pastro kërkimet e fundit" a quiet button under them (the board's arrow that puts a row's words in the box, and its "Shikuar së fundmi" under the list, are not drawn).
- Over the newest, "Më të klikuarat" shows the twelve active listings whose pages were seen the most over the last seven days, the most first, in a row of cards that slides sideways, each saying how many times it was seen in words after its town on the details line, as the redesign's canvas has it ("Tiranë · 1 493 shikime këtë javë", "this week" left out on a phone; `.card-views`, `.card-views-short`; an eye and the number on a dark pill over the photo once), and nothing before any were seen (`db::most_viewed`). A listing's page says it was seen once Turbo shows it (`application.js`, `POST /listing/{no}/pare`), so a page Turbo fetches ahead under the pointer and never opens doesn't count, nor do bots that run no scripts or say they are bots, nor its seller; a visitor (their address and browser) counts once per listing per half hour. The views gather in memory and go into `listing_daily_stats.detail_views` once a minute in one statement (`views.rs`, as "Analytics are aggregated" asks), and the row is read again as often. `dev/generate_listings.sql` with `views=1` gives the listings it made two weeks of views.
- Over "Më të klikuarat", the welcome page shows the listings its visitor saw lately, "Shikuar së fundmi", the latest first, in a row of cards like it, but smaller, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 ("make shikuar se fundmi posts smaller, around 6 in the screen, and only put the title below"): about six to the screen, six whole and a quarter of the next on the page's column, three and a half on a phone (five and a quarter until the user asked them "a bit bigger on mobile" later that day, then four and a quarter until they asked "a bit bigger" once more), under the photo the title alone on two lines at most, and no heart over it; on the page's column the price on the photo a size down, on a phone, where the photos are 80 to 110 px, the photo alone, a reserved or a sold one's word small over it (`welcome.css`). A listing's page, once Turbo shows it (not one fetched ahead under the pointer, nor its seller's own, which gives no `data-listing-number`), goes first in a cookie of their numbers joined by dots (`shikuar`, twelve at most, each once, kept 90 days; `application.js`), which the server reads (`browse::SEEN_COOKIE`, `db::cards_by_numbers`), so that the row comes with the page, with no request after it and nothing moving as it arrives; a listing no longer on the site is left out, a reserved or a sold one says so over its photo, and an account the visitor blocked has none there. The row's first photo is the page's lead then. "Pastro", quiet at its heading's end, forgets them, the cookie and the row (`recent_controller.js`); the privacy page names the cookie.
- Under "Më të klikuarat", a visitor who has shown what they look for finds "Të rekomanduara për ty" ("Recommended for you") in the newest listings' place, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (`recommended.rs`, `db::recommended_ids`; `recommended-heading`). The site keeps no history of a visitor's, so the seeds are what there is: the listings they saw lately (the `shikuar` cookie, above; the latest weighing 1, each before it .85 of the one after), and, signed in, the latest 12 they keep (1.5 each) and the latest 6 they wrote to a seller about (2 each, writing being the nearest to buying; `db::seed_listings`), a listing in more than one adding them up, in tenths (`Seeds`). The 16 strongest still on the site lead, and the listings like each come as "Njoftime të ngjashme" finds a listing's (`related_listings`), cheaply off its category's indexes: the 20 closest in price either way, the 20 nearest, the 20 newest of each of their categories, and the 20 newest sharing a narrow choice such as a car's model (400 of its listings walked at most). Each is scored for every seed of its category, weighed by the seed: the choices shared (a narrow one 3, any other .5), the price (3 at the same, none at half or double), the place (2 at the same, none 50 km away) and how new it is (1 today, none two months on). Left out: the seeds themselves, the visitor's own listings (`manages_listing`, a store's people's too), those of the accounts they blocked, and a seller's past their two best; "Më të klikuarat" above may hold some of the same. The categories take turns, each as often as its seeds weigh among all of them, its best first (a phone seen after a car: a phone like it second, among the cars like the car). It shows with 4 at least (`FEWEST`), or else the newest as before (a first visit, a cookie cleared, a dog alone in its category); 24 for each seed, 120 at most, in pages of 24 as the newest's: the next frames name the seeds (`/?sipas=1002x24.1005x20`, each listing's number and weight in tenths) and where they go on from (`&nga=24`), so that the scroll goes on in the same order whatever the visitor sees meanwhile, and past the last the newest follow, those recommended left out (`ListingFilter::exclude`; `&after=` with the seeds), so that the page never runs out. A visitor's ranking is kept for 15 minutes (`Recommended`, by a hash of the seeds, the account and those blocked; 4 096 visitors at most, those past their time going first, then the oldest), and its query has 300 ms (`RANKING_TIME`; some 4 ms warm for one seed, 21 for twelve across four categories, 27 for twelve cars and 35 for thirty, against the dev data's million listings), past which the newest show instead.
- A row of cards that slides sideways is one component wherever it is (`card_row.html`, a macro the pages call with their row): "Shikuar së fundmi" on the welcome page and a seller's other listings under one of theirs ("Të veçuara" was one on a category's page until 2026-10-11, over the results; the listings a promotion features are the results' first since, below). "Më të klikuarat" is one too, on the welcome page and on a category's: the eight seen the most this week (`ListingPage::most_viewed`, `MOST_VIEWED_SHOWN`; `db::MOST_VIEWED` reads twelve, so that those of accounts the visitor blocked leave none missing), as "Shikuar së fundmi" has them, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (two big cards earlier that day, `card_pair` and `.big-cards`, and a row of small ones before those). Its cards are the browse pages' small ones (`listing_card.html`), 236 px wide and 16 px apart (smaller in "Shikuar së fundmi", above), as the design system has its shelves (180 px and 12 px on phones, where the row runs on to the screen's edge), so that on the page's column four stand whole and the next one shows at the edge (`.card-row` in `page.css`; they were a column of the grid wide once, narrowing a little when more were to come). The row is a line of flex items rather than a grid's columns. A card's id is its row's own, "most-viewed-1002", "seller-1002" (`ListingPage::row`), so that a listing in a row and in the grid under it is two cards with ids of their own.
- A heart over a listing's photo, at its top right corner, on every card and on the listing's own page, keeps the listing for a signed-in user (`saved_listings`, `web/saved.rs`, `heart.html`). `heart_controller.js` asks the server with each page which listings the visitor keeps (`/te-ruajturat/numrat`) and turns their hearts, a white outline with a soft shadow under it until then, as the redesign's canvas has it (16.5 px on the cards, a quarter under the canvas's 22 px, which the listing's own page keeps, at the user's word of 2026-10-11; the button 44 px; it stood on a small dark circle once), to the site's red (`--red`) filling its white line, in every part of the site (in the list view, where the heart is on the card off the photo, an outline in the text's colour with no shadow until kept), when someone is signed in, and keeps them in the browser too (`localStorage`, `kept-listings`, gone once no one is signed in), so that a page opened afresh, not only one Turbo shows, has its hearts filled with its photos rather than once the answer comes: the session cookie is out of scripts' reach, so a cookie beside it says there is one (`llogari`, which `account::signed_in_cookie` sets on the answer to a request with a session and clears on one after the session has gone; `controllers/signed_in.js`), and a page for someone not signed in asks for neither the listings kept nor the unread messages; a tap keeps a listing or lets it go at once (`POST /listing/{number}/ruaj`), every heart for it on the page with it. Someone not signed in goes to sign in first, and comes back to the page with the listing kept: the listing and the page wait in cookies (`ruaj`, `kthehu`), which signing in reads (`saved::after_sign_in`). The heart in every page's top bar (on the welcome page, the one in the row with its search) leads to `/te-ruajturat`, the listings kept, the last first. A kept listing reserved or sold since stays among them, saying so in a pill at its photo's top left, white on eerie black at 60 %, as the canvas has it, the photo fading for a sold one (`ListingCard::status`, `.card-status` in `page.css`: the cards' query reads each listing's status, and every other list shows active listings alone); those no one can open any more, paused, deleted or taken off the site, are listed under the cards, "Nuk shfaqen më", rows in a white box as the canvas has them, each saying why, one expired or taken off in red's tint, with "Hiq" to let it go (`db::saved_gone`). Both vanished from the page without a word before. A category or search page keeps its search for a signed-in visitor with "Ruaj kërkimin", at the end of the heading's line as the canvas has it (`.save-search-button` in `browse.css`: a grey pill 36 px tall as the row's pills under it (40 px until the user asked for the filter pills a bit smaller, 2026-10-11), its words 14 px, with the bookmark in the words' colour, empty, filled with the accent and its words underlined under the pointer or a finger, as the welcome page's categories are (the bookmark sat in the accent at rest until the user asked for it plain on 2026-10-11), the bookmark alone on phones; "Kërkimi u ruajt" once kept, the pill filled with the accent with a tick in the bookmark's place, the tick and the words dark on it, a shade off under the pointer (the user's ask of 2026-10-11; the same grey pill until then), a tap letting it go): its words, its page's category and its filters, as the page itself writes them (`Filters::search_address`, the town a cookie remembers written in, the order left out), so that the same search kept twice is one (`saved_searches.filters`, `{"path", "query"}`, unique per user; migration 0030), at most 30 (`SEARCHES_MOST`), named by its words, its category and its chips ("„golf“ · Makina · Tiranë · Naftë", `Filters::search_name`). Someone not signed in signs in first and comes back to the page. `/kerkimet-e-ruajtura`, a grey pill over `/te-ruajturat`'s heading after the bell (and a quiet link back after a chevron), lists them in a column of 760 px in the middle of the page, as the canvas has it, a row each in one white box, the buttons at its end (under the name on phones), the last kept first, each with how many listings came since it was last opened ("3 njoftime të reja", up to 99+; the search's page made again from its path and query as `browse.rs` makes it, `replay`, its listings newer than then, `ListingFilter::since`, by `sort_at`, so a listing brought up counts as new), "Ndalo lajmërimet" or "Lajmëromë", and "Fshi"; opening one (`/kerkimet/{id}`) counts its new listings from then on. Every 15 minutes the server looks for searches with alerts on whose last alert, or keeping, is three hours ago or more (`ALERT_GAP`), and tells their savers of the listings newer than that: a notification (`notifications`, `SAVED_SEARCH`: "3 njoftime të reja: Makina · Naftë", with the search and the link), which puts the dot on the heart that leads to the listings kept and says so by the link on `/te-ruajturat` ("Kërkimet e ruajtura · 1 me njoftime të reja", until `/kerkimet-e-ruajtura` is shown or the search opened), and an e-mail where the saver signs in with an address, naming the newest three with their prices and the link (`sender::Senders::send_text`; phone numbers have no WhatsApp template for these, so their alerts are the dot alone). Someone who turned these off (`notification_preferences.saved_search_alerts`) hears nothing, nor by e-mail someone who turned e-mail off. Each search with alerts on costs a count every 15 minutes while due, at most 200 of them a run.
- A price lowered lately says so (`price_was!` in `db/mod.rs`, from `listing_price_history`, which a trigger fills as prices change): a card, like a listing's page, puts what it was after its price, struck through, a touch smaller, then by how much in a pill at the photo's bottom right corner, light green in both themes as the canvas has it (in the list, off the photo, the theme's green) ("5 000 € ~~5 700 €~~ −12%"; on a small card the old price gives way first, ending in "…", where the line is short), and a listing's page likewise ("5 400 € ~~6 900 €~~ −22%", `price_drop.html`). A price counts as dropped when its last change, in the last 30 days, made it at least 1% lower, in the same currency, than the lowest it was over the 30 days before: so a price raised for a while and lowered back is no drop, and it shows by how much under that lowest. Only listings on sale, for rent or a service, with a price (fixed, negotiable or "from") and still up; a month after, a drop is just the price.
- Buyers write to sellers from a listing's page: "Shkruaji shitësit", a speech bubble and the words in a wide pill filled in the accent beside the phone's grey one (each wore the accent's light tint for a moment on 2026-10-12, the message and then the phone, until the user asked both back), opens, for someone signed in, the conversation over the page rather than a page of its own. On a phone, a sheet, white as the canvas has it (on the page's colour once), frosted as an iPhone's sheets are, in the header's glass (`--frost-sheet`: the white at 80%, 92% at first, a little of the dimmed page showing through it, blurred; the box to write in blurs the messages that scroll under it rather than hide them on a white band of its own; the user's ask of 2026-10-11): it slides up from the screen's bottom in an eighth of a second, 470 px tall as the canvas draws it on an 844 px screen (three quarters of a shorter one at most; the screen's lower half once), a card floating 8 px in from the screen's sides and over its bottom edge, its corners all round, 40 px, as round as the box to write in and its button 16 px inside them, as iOS's sheets are (the user's ask of 2026-10-11, "make lower chat box round": it stood across the foot, square on the bottom edge, only its top corners rounded, 18 px), with what floats' shadow, the page under it dimmed (`--scrim`, fading in; a tap on it closes the sheet: the shade is the listing's box's own, `.listing-page::after`, a box of no size itself, so that the tap reaches that box, which `message_sheet_controller.js` tells from any other) and held still, a short rounded bar at its top as on an iPhone's sheets (40 by 5 px, in the strong hairline's grey); the bar, or the conversation's head, drags it down (following the finger, by pointer events, moved once a display frame and on a layer of its own while dragged, so at 120 frames a second where the screen and browser run so; its slides are transforms the browser animates at the screen's rate): let go past a quarter of its height, or flicked, it slides away and closes, and sooner it goes back up, a drag on the head not following its link. A tap in the box to write in brings the phone's keyboard up, which would push the whole screen up to show the box, the page and all: the sheet rises instead, in the moment the box takes the focus, before the keyboard comes, to float 8 px over where the keyboard will be: as high as the keyboard was the last time it was up on that screen (its height kept in `localStorage`, by the screen's width), or, until it has been, a little higher than an iPhone's comes to (48% of the screen, where an iPhone's is 42 to 46 with its row of suggestions), so the box stands clear of the keyboard and the browser has nothing to push; once the keyboard is up it eases the rest of the way onto it (the `visualViewport` API: its foot 8 px over the keyboard's top, as tall as it was or as the room allows, its messages kept at their end; a change of less than 100 px is the browser's toolbar, not the keyboard, and moves nothing), the page under it unmoved, and eases back to the lower half as the focus leaves the box, or as the phone puts the keyboard away with the box still focused (Android's back button), when the box lets the focus go too, so that the next tap brings both up again. From 720 px up, a popup card from the button instead, white, with a hairline round it, its corners 22 px and the shadow of what floats (`--shadow-float`), 400 px wide, under it or over it where the screen has more room there, as tall as that room allows (360 to 520 px), the page brought along to show it whole, scrolling with the page, and a click outside it closes it as a menu's would, as does the button that opened it, pressed again; "Mbyll" (or Escape) closes it and empties it, so that its conversation stops asking for new messages; the focus goes back to the button for a keyboard alone, and a tap shows no highlight of the browser's own over the button, so no ring or flash is left on it (`message_sheet_controller.js`; `-webkit-tap-highlight-color` on `.button`). The button loads the conversation into the sheet's Turbo frame, `conversation`, which `conversation.html` wraps its page in: writing and sending go on in the sheet without leaving the listing (the conversation's "Blloko" at its end stays off the sheet, as the way back to the inbox does: the listing's page has "Blloko shitësin" under its buttons), its messages scrolling inside it (`data-scroll-root`, which `conversation_controller.js` scrolls in place of the page) and the box to write in at its foot, which takes the focus where there is a mouse while on a phone the keyboard waits for a tap in it; a response without the frame (the sign-in page, once a session ran out) opens as a page. Signed out, the button leads to the page as before, which asks to sign in first. The page, as the canvas has it, has the way back to the inbox, small and muted after a chevron, over a white box 720 px wide in the middle of the page (`.conversation-box`; the page's column, 760 px at the left, with no box, once), a new conversation's as tall as the canvas draws it, 720 px, or what the screen has room for, the box to write in at its foot under the room the messages will take (`is-first`); on a phone there is no box: who with and the listing on a white band across the screen between hairlines, and the box to write in at the screen's foot, across it on the page's colour with a hairline over it. The box, or the sheet, leads with who with (their initial in a ring of the main colour, 32 px, and their name, semibold; in the sheet a ring of 28 px and the name muted and smaller, "Mbyll" at the line's end, a circle of 36 px with a hairline; the name was quieter on the page too once) on a line of their own, a hairline under it; then the listing, its photo (56 px, its corners 12 px) beside its title, the heading, bold on one line (on up to two once), over its price, quieter (semibold in the sheet), after which, once it is reserved, sold or off the site, a badge says so ("Rezervuar", "Shitur", "Jo më në faqe"; `ListingHead::status`), one link to it; then the box to write in, with three quick first messages over it, grey pills 36 px tall (white ones with a hairline once), that fill it in, the box taking the focus where there is a mouse while a phone's keyboard stays down ("Përshëndetje! A është ende në dispozicion?", "Cili është çmimi i fundit?", "Kur mund ta shoh?"; `web/messages.rs`, `conversation.html`). The first message starts their conversation about the listing (`conversations`, one per buyer and listing, both sides in `conversation_participants`; a business's listing reaches whoever posted it), and writing about it again goes on in the same one. `/mesazhet` lists the visitor's conversations, the latest written in first, as the canvas has them, in one white box in a column of 760 px in the middle of the page, each with the listing's photo, who with, the listing, the start of the latest message and when it came; those with messages unread in bold, with how many in the accent's pill, as everything unread is (a coral pill once). The top bar's speech bubble, beside the heart (on the welcome page, in the row with its search, as the heart is), has how many conversations have messages unread in the accent, the number dark on it (`inbox_controller.js`, from `/mesazhet/numri`, asked for when someone is signed in). A conversation's page has the messages as the canvas has them, each a bubble as wide as its words up to 70% of the conversation (80% on a phone), the visitor's own on the right in the main colour (in the coral's tint once), the other side's on the left in the grey of secondary controls (white with a hairline once), rounded but for the corner on its own side, each with when it was sent under it by Albania's clock ("14:32", "Dje, 14:32", then the date; the database turns the times into Albania's, `AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Tirane'`), and the box to write in at the foot of the conversation's box, and at the bottom of the screen while the messages scroll past (on the box's white): a field with corners of 24 px, one line as tall as the button beside it, which grows with what is typed, up to a few lines, and with the focus takes its hairline in the main colour and a soft ring round it, as every field does (it lit up in the accent's tint once, with a border of the accent, rather than taking the site's focus ring); Enter sends where there is a keyboard and Shift+Enter starts a new line, while on a phone Enter is a new line and the paper plane sends: a circle beside the field as tall as a button, 48 px, filled as the buttons are (a smaller coral circle inside the field, at its right end, once), the plane turning to point up as soon as something is typed (`.composer-send`, by `:placeholder-shown`, without a script). While the page is open it asks every 8 seconds for messages that came, a Turbo Stream that adds them at its end (`conversation_controller.js`; server-sent events would be instant, but the development tunnel holds them back), and what it shows is read as it shows (`/mesazhet/{id}/lexuar`, up to the last message shown), not when Turbo only fetched it ahead under the pointer; the header's count follows. Someone who leaves a message unread three minutes after it came hears of it with the site closed (`notices.rs`, a look every minute): an e-mail to the address they sign in with, or a WhatsApp message to their number where a notice template is set up (`WHATSAPP_NOTICE_TEMPLATE`), saying who wrote and about which listing ("Besa të shkroi për „Volkswagen Golf 1.6, 2016“", the subject), quoting the latest message (its first 300 characters) and how many more are unread, with the link to answer; at most one per conversation an hour, and only of messages newer than the last notice, so a conversation going on brings one and not a stream. Without them a buyer's message was seen only if the seller happened to open the site. Each is a notification (`notifications`, `NEW_MESSAGE`, the conversation in its payload), recorded before it goes out, so a channel that fails doesn't make it go again every minute, and which a list of them on the site can show later; with no channel set up it goes to the log, as the sign-in codes do. "Cilësimet" has "Mesazhet që nuk i ke lexuar", a box ticked until it is unticked and saved (`/llogaria/njoftimet`, `notification_preferences.message_alerts`), which the notice itself names, as its e-mail's link stops them without signing in (see "Cilësimet"); someone who turned e-mail off (`email_enabled`) gets none by e-mail. The links start with `PUBLIC_URL`, since there is no request to go by. Someone not signed in goes to sign in first, and comes back to the page to write (the `kthehu` cookie, as for the hearts).
- Messages are limited so they can't carry spam (`web/messages.rs`): only someone signed in writes, which takes a code sent to their phone or e-mail, so every sender has proved a contact; one account writes to at most 5 new sellers an hour and 15 a day (conversations it starts), and sends at most 8 messages a minute and 200 a day; until the other side answers, it sends at most 3 messages in a row, and none with a link (`http://`, `https://`, `www.`), which is what spam and scams mostly carry; links never become links on the page anyway. A message has at most 1 000 characters. Whoever gets messages they don't want blocks the other side, under the conversation ("Blloko …", `user_blocks`): they can't write there any more, nor start a conversation about another of the blocker's listings, and "Zhblloko" lets them again. A message refused goes back in the box with why. The counts come from the messages and conversations themselves (`db::sent_lately`), so a restart forgets none of them.
- Those who keep a listing hear when its seller lowers its price (`db::notify_price_drop`, run in the edit's transaction): each gets a notification (`notifications`, type `PRICE_DROP`, with what it was and what it is), in place of one about it they haven't seen yet, keeping that one's "was", the price they last saw; not the seller, nor anyone who turned price drop alerts off (`notification_preferences.price_drop_alerts`, on unless a row says otherwise). The rail's heart (on phones the welcome page's, in the row with its search) gets a dot in the accent while they have drops unseen, and says how many to screen readers ("Të ruajturat: 1 me çmim më të ulët"; `heart_controller.js`, from `/te-ruajturat/numrat`), and `/te-ruajturat` lists them over the cards in a white box, each from what to what, the new price bold ("Çmime më të ulëta te njoftimet që ke ruajtur"). Once that page is shown, it says so (`data-seen-url`, the beacon `application.js` sends for a listing's views, to `/te-ruajturat/pare`), not when Turbo only fetched it ahead under the pointer, and the drops are seen up to the latest it showed (`?deri=`, the notification's UUIDv7 id), so a copy of the page the browser shows again from its cache doesn't see a drop that came after: the dot goes. Drops of a listing let go, or no longer up, are left out. E-mail or push notices could follow from the same rows.
- Every notification a user has had in the last 90 days, of whatever kind, is on `/lajmerimet` ("Lajmërimet", `web/notifications.rs`, `notifications.html`), the latest first, in a column of 760 px in the middle of the page as the canvas has it, a row each in one white box: its kind's icon in a grey circle of 40 px, muted once read (a price tag for a price dropped, the bell for a saved search's new listings, the speech bubble for a message left unread, with what it said in two lines at most, the clock for one of the user's listings about to expire, the two arrows round a circle for one expired), its title and when ("Sot", "para 3 ditësh"), leading to what it is about while that is still there to open (the listing, the saved search, the conversation; `db::notifications`, at most 100). Those unread have their titles in the semibold, their icons in the text's colour, and the accent's dot at the row's end, edged as every unread dot is (`--dot-edge`), and say so to screen readers ("E re: "), until the page has been shown (`data-seen-url`, `POST /lajmerimet/pare`, up to the latest it showed), which reads them, and with them the heart's dot for prices dropped and saved searches. Every account page's row of pills leads there, saying how many are new ("Lajmërimet · 2 të reja", `account_nav.html`). The top bar's account button wears the heart's dot while a notification about one of the user's own listings is unread, one about to expire or expired, which no other button shows (`account_controller.js`, `GET /lajmerimet/numri`, `{"unread": 3, "own": 1}`), but on `/lajmerimet`, which shows it; a bell of its own in the top bar would have been a sixth round button, with the dots the heart and the speech bubble already show. Renewing a listing reads what was said of its expiry, and reading a conversation with nothing left unread from the other side reads its message notices.
- The pages of every main category, and of a few big subcategories (`MOST_VIEWED_IN` in `views.rs`: Makina, Apartamente, Vila), have their own "Më të klikuarat", as the welcome page has it for every category: the listings seen the most this week in the category and those under it, under the page's buttons and over its listings, its two seen the most as big cards whichever size the listings' are, while no filter or words are on (they would take no notice of them). The rows are read in the background, so that no page waits for them (`Views::most_viewed`): all of them as the server starts, then every minute, after the views are written, those that pages asked for in the last ten minutes; a row no page asked for in a while is read as it is asked for, if older than five minutes. A round button with a rising line, after the view button in the browse row and only on a page with the row, shows it or folds it away at once, pressed while it shows (`most_viewed_controller.js`); a cookie keeps the choice (`most-viewed=hidden`, `MOST_VIEWED_COOKIE` in `browse.rs`), so the categories' pages after come the same way: folded, the row stays in the page, hidden, its photos all waiting (none loaded at once), and the results' first photo comes first instead.
- Category pages (`/c/makina`) and search (`/search?q=golf`) share one handler and one filter form. A search forgives typos and finds a word's other forms (see "Search can start in PostgreSQL"); when it searched a word in place of a misspelt one, a line under the heading names it: "Shfaqen edhe rezultatet për „golf“." A search across every category names, as chips under its heading after "Gjetur te" ("Found in"), each with its category's icon as the canvas has them, the categories it found listings in, the most first, with how many ("Makina 17"; the names alone once more than 1 000 are found): each narrows the search to its category, whose filters it then offers (`db::listing_categories`). Filters are ordinary query parameters (`/c/makina?vehicle_fuel=DIESEL&price_max=12000&sort=price_asc`), so any filtered page can be bookmarked, shared and indexed, and infinite scroll keeps them. Words open the page in the best match first ("Më të përshtatshmet", "Njoftimet më të përshtatshme në Makina" in a category's heading; `Sort::Relevance`, `sort=relevance` where another order is the default): `ts_rank` against the words, the title's weighing most, the details' next and the description's least, the newest first among equals; among the newest 240 matches, ten pages (`db::RANK_WINDOW`, `push_ranked`), the matches past them following newest first (`relevance_page`), so that the list goes on as far as newest first would. Ranking every match costs a heap read each: against a million listings a page of "makina" (252 000 matches) took 210 ms where newest first took 5. The window is read as newest first reads its page, off the newest first index, for as long as the planner thinks walking it cheaper than reading every match: at 1 000 it judged otherwise for "gjendje të mirë" (70 634 matches where it expected 14 126) and took 134 ms, at 240 it walks and takes 3. A search that finds fewer has them all ranked. Its menu offers it first, the newest first, the cheapest and the dearest after it; a page without words neither offers it nor takes it from its address. Its next page starts after the last card's rank, time and id (`Cursor::Relevance`, `<rank>_<microseconds>_<id>`, the rank as short as it reads back the same, compared as a `real`), or, past the window, after its time and id (`Cursor::RelevanceRest`, `past_<microseconds>_<id>`); the page where the window runs out finds where it ended, its 240th newest match (`window_edge`), and goes on from there. The map's dots come newest first for it.
- A listing card shows the photo, the title, the price, and a details line (the price on the photo in white, 15 px and bold, over a shade along the photo's bottom edge, clear to 4.5 rem from it and then black at 55 %, what follows it, /muaj or i diskutueshëm, a size down: `ListingCard::price_main`, `price_note_parts`; on a narrow photo, a small card's, a row's or the list's on a phone, the price, its month and its pill alone, "(i diskutueshëm)" and what a dropped price was left out, which cut the price short there ("9 800 € (i diskut…"), at the user's word on 2026-10-11, a big card having room for them (`.card-negotiable`, `.card-was` in `page.css`); the title 15 px semibold and the line 13 px muted under the photo, as the redesign's canvas has them) that always starts with the city, with no pin before it: "Tiranë · 2016 · 148 000 km · Naftë". The details line takes one line and ends in "…" when longer; in the list it is a pill of no colour (the surface, the menus' hairline) as wide as its words, and a card with nothing to say there has no pill.
