In a search limited to a city (`city=vlore`), the cards in that city leave the city out, since it says nothing new, and show instead the first property the listing page has that neither the card nor the title already gives (a phone's brand: "Apple · 128 GB"; for a "Volkswagen Golf 7", the body type); in the list the line leaves the city out, the place line under the price having it (`.card-town`, `.card-place` in `page.css`). The city is the listing's municipality, found by `location_municipality()`, so a town or neighbourhood added later under a municipality shows the municipality.
- A card is an `<article>` whose title link is stretched over the whole card with CSS (`.card-link::after`): a tap anywhere opens the listing, and screen readers name the link by its title. Tapped or clicked, it underlines its title in the accent until its listing shows (`.card.pressed`, which `application.js` sets; a box round the card in the main colour, black on the light theme and white on the dark, until the user asked it away on 2026-10-11), and under a mouse pointer too, but not while the pointer is on its heart; the keyboard's focus still rings the whole card. A finger's page having been fetched as it touched the card (below), the next page waits a fifth of a second after the tap before it is drawn, so that the mark shows (`turbo:before-render`, paused; a category's pill too, whose icon the user found unfilled on a phone: it was, for a frame). Under a mouse pointer, a rounded panel in the site's muted colour appears behind the card, a little larger than it (`.card::before`).
- The search is the top bar's on every page but the welcome page, whose own leads its row (above): a grey pill 44 px tall between the logo and the account (on phones 40 px, on a second row of the bar to itself, the whole of it between the page's gutters, the bar growing to 104 px and `--size-header` with it, which the pages under it reckon with, while `--size-header-row` keeps the first row's 56 px for the menu's own top: the user's ask of 2026-10-11, its words having been cut short beside the logo; and that row tucks away as the page scrolls down, past the bar's own height, and comes back as it scrolls up, the bar's own row staying, as the user asked on 2026-10-11, "when i scroll down i want the search bar to hide in mobile": `.search-tucked`, which `application.js` sets, the bar clipped to its first row as the search fades, a hairline under the row then, nothing moving, the page under the bar showing and taking taps where the search was; never while the search has the focus, the clip gone then so that its suggestions show, nor on a listing's page, whose search folds behind the magnifier), at most 720 px wide, in the middle of the page beside the rail (the bar's start and end, `.header-start` and `.header-end`, as wide as each other from 720 px up), the magnifier at its start, muted, which searches the words as Enter does, its question muted, staying as it is in use, with no edge or ring round it (the user's ask of 2026-10-11; it turned the fields' white with the main colour's edge and a ring before), a cross emptying it while it has words and its suggestions under it (`.top-search` in `base.css`; the `header_search` block of `base.html`, which `home.html` empties). A listing's page folds it away, as the user asked on 2026-10-11: its bar has the search's magnifier just before the account in its place, drawn as the account is, a bare round button of 40 px with a grey circle under the pointer, which opens the search where the other pages have it, its field ready for typing (on a phone the keyboard up, and under an empty field the last searches, as a tap in it gives), the magnifier grey while it is open; another tap, or Escape once its suggestions are closed (the first Escape closes those), folds it again, the focus back on the magnifier, and Turbo keeps the page folded for the back button (`.search-folded` and `.search-button` in `base.css`, `search-folded` from `listing.html`'s `header_class` block, `search_fold_controller.js`); on a phone the bar is then one row, 56 px (`--size-header` with it), and two with the search open. The magnifier is in every bar, hidden but for a listing's, so that a listing's stand-in, the bar of the page it is tapped on, folded (below), has it. It searches every category and town ("Kërko njoftime"), but on a browse page the page's category, its filters and order kept (`Filters::search_form_fields`), saying which ("Kërko në Makina"; `browse.html` fills the block): the row's round search button, which opened a box under the row, went with it on 2026-10-11. The row starts with Filtrat and Harta (the map), on the grey of secondary controls with no outline, as the listing page's share button is, side by side on every screen, the row's buttons 40 px as the canvas has them, their icons 18 px (17 px in the pills); while what one opens is open it is filled with the main colour, its icon and words in its own, as a picked pill is. The row is the head's last line, 14 px under the subcategories; the filters picked stand in it after Filtrat, each a chip 36 px tall, as the row's pills, in the accent, toned down (`--filter-on`: the accent mixed into the page, 40% of it on the light theme and 24% on the dark, its words the text's, as the user asked on 2026-10-11; the main colour before), with its cross, then the dropdowns of the filters buyers use most that aren't on (below), and "Pastro filtrat" last. Filtrat and Harta wear the category pills' grey and their kind of icon, an outline (three sliders, a map pin) in the words' colour, filled with the main colour while what they open is open, and say their names and what is on, as pills: "Filtrat" semibold with how many filters are on after it in bold ("Filtrat · 2"), the map "Harta", or once a town or a point is picked its pin alone, round, the place's chip in the row saying which (until the user's ask of 2026-10-11 it said the town, "Durrës", or the point's nearest town and the distance, "Tiranë · 10 km", beside the chip that says it too); where the row wraps, on a wider screen, the map's words give up room first, ending in "…". After Harta, at the row's end, a round button with the icon of the cards' view now opens the views (below), and last the order, its words and a chevron ("Më të rejat ⌄", no pill of its own); on phones the whole row is one line that slides sideways from one edge of the screen to the other (`.browse-filters-line`), as the canvas has the line of the filters: Filtrat, the chips, Harta, the order, a grey pill there as the others are, and the view, then the dropdowns, what the order and the view open placed by the head so that the sliding line cuts none of it (Harta, the view and the order stood on a line of their own under the others until the user's ask of 2026-10-11). What each is set to is for screen readers and their tooltips too ("Filtrat: Naftë", "Harta: Tiranë · 10 km" or "Harta: Kudo", "Rendit: Më të rejat"). The filters and the map open over the results rather than pushing them down, a little under the row and as wide as it, the page dimmed under them; Escape, or a tap on the dim or on the row beside its buttons, folds them again (`filters_controller.js`). The map's card is a square under the row (below), and the filters are a sheet, as the canvas draws it: 460 px wide at the screen's right and its whole height (on a phone nine tenths of the screen at its left, where the menu opens, the dimmed page showing beside it, a tap there folding it, as the user asked on 2026-10-11; the whole screen until then), white with what floats' shadow over the page dimmed, the header too, appearing without sliding, the page behind still; its form scrolls inside between its top, its name and a cross in a grey circle that folds it, and its foot, always in sight, with "Pastro" (when a filter is on) and a button in the accent that says how many results there are now ("Shfaq 1 234 njoftime", "Asnjë njoftim"), which folds the sheet and brings up the results' heading. Neither has a button to apply it: as soon as something changes (a box ticked, a choice picked, a typed field left or entered, a slider let go, the map stopped after a drag or a zoom) the page refreshes in place (`refresh.js`: Turbo fetches the address and morphs the page into the new one, keeping the scroll, the address replaced rather than added to the history), the card staying open as it is while the results, their count and the chips follow under it; closing the filters applies whatever was typed but not left. Each card has the listing's number as its id, so the refresh keeps the cards that stay. The orders open in a menu under the order, like the in-page dropdowns, closing on a tap outside or Escape (`dropdown_controller.js`); only one is open at a time: opening one says so (`section:open`) and the others fold. Filtrat says which filters are on but the place, which the map's button says, and which has its own chip. Over the listings, a heading names the order they come in, a section's title as the canvas has it: on a category page "Njoftimet më të reja" ("Newest listings"), elsewhere the order alone ("Më të përshtatshmet", "Më të lirat"). The view's button, the row's last, opens a menu like the order's under it, with three views, each with its icon, the one now ticked (`Cards` in `browse.rs`); a pick switches the cards at once and folds the menu (`cards_controller.js`). Small cards, as many to a line as fit; a list, as the canvas draws it, one listing a line 16 px under the one before (14 px on phones), the page's whole width, with no box round it but under the pointer or the keyboard's focus, where the canvas's white box shows (the surface, rounder corners, the soft shadow; a box round every one until the user's ask of 2026-10-11): the photo at its left, 240 by 180 with corners of 12 px (a square of 116 px on phones; to the browser picking a copy, 240 px and 116 px), the price on it at its foot over the shade and the heart white at its top right, as a card's are; beside it, 20 px from the photo (14 px on phones), its lines 6 px apart (5 px on phones), roomier since the user's ask of the same day, the words the size they were ("rather the spaces"), the title (17 px semibold, two lines at most), the details line under it, muted (its town left out, as the foot has it), the start of the description, as many lines as the room left under the title and the details holds (`lines_controller.js`, which measures it as the card changes size and sets `--lines`; two without scripts, one on a phone: the user's ask of 2026-10-11, two at most before, none on phones), ending in "…" when it has more, the card itself opening the listing for the rest (a card carries the first 400 characters, its lines run together, cut at a word and ending in "…" when longer: `description` in `ListingCard`, `description_excerpt`), and at the foot, level with the photo's bottom, where it is, the zone and town or the town, whatever the search is in (`ListingCard::place_text`), and when it was posted or last brought up ("Blloku, Tiranë · Dje"; "Sot", "Dje", "para 3 ditësh", the date from a week on: `ListingCard::when_text`), muted (`.cards-list` in `page.css`, the photo's box giving its parts to the card's own grid, the box as tall as the photo, the foot's row taking what the words over it leave; it was a plain line once, the price beside the photo in the text's colour, the heart off the photo); or big, one a line no wider than 40rem (two on a wide screen), the photo 4:3 with every photo of the listing to slide through (`slides_controller.js`: a finger slides them, and so does a mouse, pressed and moved, settling on the nearest photo when let go or on the next one when flicked, the click that ends the drag opening nothing; under a mouse, arrows step them, a photo in a fifth of a second, round and round (the next from the last back to the first, the previous from the first to the last), each a small arrow on a soft dark circle, showing only while the mouse is in the quarter of the photo at its edge, its button and click as big as ever; and dots say which shows, a click on one going to its photo), and over its top left corner, as the price is at the bottom, the seller, small so as not to cover the photo: their initial in a white ring of 24 px, as the listing page has it in the main colour's, their name at 12.8 px, and under it, at 11 px, since when they are a user ("Përdorues që nga tetor 2026"). A cookie keeps the choice (`cards=small`, `cards=big` or `cards=list`), so the next pages and the infinite scroll come the same way; Prona's pages and those of the categories under it show big cards until the visitor chooses another view there, and keep that choice apart (`cards-prona`, `BIG_CARDS_IN`), so that one made on other pages leaves them as they are; and the photos' `sizes` follow it, a WebP photo's `<source>` as well as its JPEG, so big cards fetch bigger copies and small ones and the list's the card-sized (`Cards::sizes`; see "Photos"). The map page's list stays small, and "Më të klikuarat" is always a row of small cards. In the sheet every filter is a section of its own under a hairline (`--border-soft`), its name over it in the words' colour, semibold, one under another, and whichever of the row's panels is open has its opener filled with the main colour, as every picked pill is (the view's button while its menu is open, a picked filter, a price choice pressed). "Filtrat" says which filters are on; first where, Qyteti (the towns by county, which replaces the map's point) with "Afër meje" beside it (the visitor's point, which replaces the town), and in property, under them, Zona, the town's zones where it has some (Tirana's, Durrës's, Vlora's), "Të gjitha zonat" first: every such town's zones are in it under their town, those of the town picked showing (`Filters::zone_groups`), so that the zones of a town Qyteti picks show at once and another town's zone picked before drops (`narrow_controller.js`), Zona hiding for a town without zones (`filters_controller.js`); then the price, then the filters buyers narrow by most: those the category asks of every listing or shows on its cards, and a few more (`PRIMARY` in `filters.rs`: a car's body type and gearbox, the makes and brands, a property's furnishing, a job's contract and place), a make with its models; the rest wait under "Më shumë filtra", folded unless one of them is on (`Filters::fields`; a category with six or fewer, or only two more, shows them all). A property's kind of listing (Shitje, Qira) comes first; elsewhere it waits with the rest. Their options are chips like the posting form's, as the redesign's canvas has them: round-ended on the grey of secondary controls, a picked one filled with the main colour, its words semibold after a check (a car's body types keep their icon over the name instead), under quiet grey names; the options apply by themselves (`filters_controller.js`; without JavaScript, with a button), one column on every screen (the small ones stood side by side once, in the card the filters were); a make picked there narrows the models to its own at once, as in the posting form (`narrow_controller.js`). The price is typed, from and to, the currency beside them, a two-part toggle (€ | Lekë, radios, the one picked raised; the prices it is on at the name's line's end, muted); under them one track with two ends, the lowest and the highest, which stop at each other (`price_range_controller.js`): its scale grows to the right, from 10 € to about a million (a hundred times as many Lekë), prices kept to two figures, and either end at its edge is no limit; and under the track four quick choices (chips, the picked one filled with the main colour), each about a quarter of the category's listings by price in its usual currency ("deri 3 000 €", "3 000 – 6 000 €", "6 000 – 12 000 €", "nga 12 000 €"): the 25th, 50th and 75th percentiles of its prices, kept to round figures (`db::price_quarters`, `Filters::price_choices`), worked out again every six hours, and offered once 20 of its listings have a price. Each of the three moves the others, and the choice the prices are shows pressed; tapped again, it lets them go. A range whose unit has steps is picked from two dropdowns, from and to (`range_steps` in `filters.rs`): the years from next year back to 1970 (1950 for a building's), the kilometres, square metres, cm³, horsepower, inches and rim sizes in round numbers, a value from an older link standing in its place among them; other ranges are typed. A car's body types (Karroceria) take a line of their own, on every screen, each box with the car's side drawn over its name (`BODY_TYPES` in `properties.rs`). A listener in `application.js` drops empty fields from the URL before Turbo fetches it.
- After the filters picked, the row has dropdowns of the filters buyers narrow by most that aren't on yet, as the canvas has them (`Filters::drops`, `filter_drop.html`): grey pills like Filtrat with the filter's name and a chevron ("Marka ⌄"), five at most in the row with the chips (`ROW_FILTERS`), so that a filter picked takes its dropdown's place as a chip. They are the category's own filters in the form's order, its dropdowns, its boxes and its years (not a car's body types, whose icons want the sheet's room, nor a range in a unit, kilometres or square metres, nor a box to tick); on a search across every category the town comes first, in Prona the kind of listing, and after the category's own the condition, and elsewhere the kind of listing; the price stands fourth, or before the last of fewer (after a lone one): Makina has "Marka, Modeli, Viti, Çmimi, Karburanti", Automjete "Gjendja, Çmimi, Lloji i njoftimit", a search "Qyteti, Çmimi, Gjendja" (in English "Brand, Model, Year, Price, Fuel"). Each opens a white card under it, at least 280 px and at most 360 px wide (the menus' surface, rounder corners and shadow; on phones, where the row slides, under the head and as wide as it), its pill filled with the main colour while it is open, one open at a time with the row's others (`dropdown_controller.js`). The card is a form of its own that carries the page's other filters, its words and its order along hidden: a dropdown's options are a list in the card (the picker, inline: `data-picker-inline-value`, a make's models folded under their makes, the make chosen's open), and picking one applies at once, as does a kind of listing, a town or a price's quick choice; boxes (Karburanti, Gjendja), a range's two lists and the price (the sheet's, `filter_price.html`: typed, its toggle, its track and its quick choices) have "Shfaq rezultatet" across the card's foot, and apply as the card folds too, on a tap outside or Escape, if anything changed (`filter_drop_controller.js`); the page refreshes in place, as for the sheet's filters (`refresh.js`). Without JavaScript they are gone, the sheet's form being in the page.
- Where to look is in the map's card, as two white pills 40 px tall with the soft shadow over its top (`.map-pill`), the first with a crosshair: "Vendndodhja ime" (the browser says where the visitor is, once they let it, and the map moves there, its point applying) and beside it the towns' menu, which says the place now ("Gjithë Shqipëria", "Durrës", or the map's point, "Tiranë · 10 km") and opens the towns by county, folding under their county as the in-page lists do (`picker_controller.js`), all of Albania first (`Filters::town_menu`). A town puts the page in it, its filters kept, as the filters' Qyteti does, and all of Albania drops the place; the page refreshes in place (`refresh.js`, `refreshTo`), the card staying open and the map moving onto the town, without a circle until a point is set again by moving it (`map_controller.js`, `pickTown`). The filters' card has Qyteti and "Afër meje" too, and in property Zona: a zone of the town (`zone=plazh`, which goes with `city`, as zone names repeat from town to town, and means nothing without it or outside property), whose chip comes after the town's: the zone's leaves the town, the town's takes the zone with it, and a zone with nothing in it offers "Kërko në gjithë qytetin" (`Filters::town_url`); on `/search`, the category's chip and "Pastro filtrat" take it with the category. The towns' menu, all of Albania and the map's point drop the zone with the town; another category keeps it where its listings can say one (`Filters::category_url`). The town the page is in is kept in a cookie for a year (`qyteti`, which `place_controller.js` sets from the page's element as it shows and as a refresh changes the town), so the next category or search opens in it (`Params::remembering_town`) unless its address names a place, a town or a point; all of Albania in the map's card, the town's chip, the filters' own "Gjithë Shqipëria" and "Kërko në gjithë Shqipërinë" forget it (`forgetTown`; the links carry `data-forget-town`, `application.js`, and are never fetched ahead, `data-turbo-prefetch="false"`, since that request would still carry the town). The map page has its own point.
- How many listings a page found is counted with the page up to 1 000 (`db::COUNT_LIMIT`); past that the count, beside the heading, says "1 000+ njoftime" and, once the page shows, it asks its own address with `numri=1` for the count up to 10 000 (`db::PRECISE_COUNT_LIMIT`, `filters_controller.js`), which fills in that line and the filters' button. Counting that far with every page would slow them all: a town's cars take some 70 ms more to count to 10 000 than to 1 000.
- When nothing is found the page says so in a white box, as the canvas has it (`.empty-state` in `browse.css`: a magnifier in a grey circle, "Nuk u gjet asnjë njoftim." semibold, what would find more under it, muted, and grey buttons; the results' heading over it for screen readers alone), and offers what would find more, a tap away: around the map's point, a wider circle ("Zgjero largësinë në 25 km", then 50), all of Albania with a place picked ("Kërko në gjithë Shqipërinë"), and with filters on, a word that the chips above each drop one; and with words searched, how to search for more ("Kontrollo si i ke shkruar fjalët, ose provo më pak fjalë, a më të përgjithshme") and a wanted ad for what no one sells yet ("Shto një njoftim „Kërkoj të blej“", the posting form). A search that found nothing said so in one line before, and an address with nothing, or a failure, left the visitor a line of text: the 404 page now offers a search in every category with the ways home and to posting (`not_found.html`), and a failed request shows the site's frame with what to do (`error.html`).
- The map page (`/harta`) finds listings around a point. As on Facebook's, the pin stands fixed in the middle of the map and the map moves under it, starting in the middle of Tirana (`TIRANA` in `filters.rs`, which the map page's results follow until another point is set); "Vendos hartën", in the box beside the map, sets its middle as the point. As the canvas lays the page out, under its heading and how to use it the map, 560 px tall, has a white box of 320 px beside it (`.map-controls`: the surface, rounder corners, the soft shadow) with what sets it, one under another: how far, Kategoria, Qyteti (a town to move the map onto, "Zgjidh qytetin" until one is picked), "Vendndodhja ime" (a grey button with a crosshair), "Vendos hartën" in the accent and the grey button that folds the map; the results come under both, the page's whole width and four to a line, after how many there are (17 px bold) and, where more have a point than the map shows, that it shows the first 500. Below 960 px the box goes under the map (440 px tall), and on phones the map takes the screen's whole width, 340 px tall and square-cornered, with what sets it under it in no box (it stood above the map in a line once, the results in two columns beside the map, which stayed in sight while they scrolled). Round the pin, a circle stays the same size in the middle of the map whatever the zoom (38% of the map's smaller side), and how far to look is what it covers: zooming in makes the distance smaller, zooming out bigger, from 1 to 50 km in whole kilometres, said over the map as it changes ("Largësia 9 km", `map_radius.html`); once the zoom has been still for 400 ms the nearest whole kilometre applies, the map staying as it is (snapping it to the zoom of exactly that many was a visible jump), and it zooms no further than 1 km in or 50 km out (any zoom, not only whole ones: `zoomSnap: 0`, and a tap on + or − is half a zoom, the distance about 1.4 times smaller or bigger). An address may name any whole distance up to 50 km, which the map starts on; and the results panel, a `<turbo-frame>`, reloads with what lies inside the circle, nearest first, each card saying how far away it is ("Tiranë · 850 m · 2+1 · 101 m²"). The address bar follows (`/harta?lat=41.32833&lng=19.81806&radius=10`), so a map search can be bookmarked and shared, and category and search pages take the same parameters. "Vendndodhja ime" moves the map to where the visitor is, if the browser may say, and the towns' dropdown over it, by county, onto a town (its point in `locations`; the dropdown has no name, so it sends no town of its own); the distance and the place both wait for "Vendos hartën". The map has a dot for every result, up to 500 (`db::MAP_POINTS`), while the list loads 24 at a time. Zoomed out, results near each other show as one numbered bubble and a lone one as a small dot; neither takes taps, so they don't get in the way of moving the map. From zoom 15 (`CLOSE_ZOOM`, streets readable), or from the closest the circle goes if that is further out, each result is its own dot, and a tap on one opens its title and price. It is 560 px tall beside the box (440 px under 960 px, 340 px on phones); the mouse wheel scrolls the page rather than zooming the map, and the + and − buttons, a pinch and a double tap zoom around the middle, so the pin stays where it is.
- Every category and search page has the same map over the results, in a card that always comes folded, its button saying where the results are from (above); the button opens it, and only then do Leaflet and the results' dots load (the dots from the same page with `harta=1`, which Turbo takes their `<turbo-frame>` out of). Its pin starts on the point set before, or on the town picked, or on Tirana (`Filters::map_start`), the circle round it from the first, before a point is set. The card is a square from the row's left edge, as wide as the row or as high as the screen has left under the row, whichever is less, and the map fills it, edge to edge, nothing above or below it: "Vendndodhja ime" and the towns' menu float over its top, the distance the circle covers is a badge at its bottom left ("10 km", following the zoom), Leaflet's zoom buttons sit at its bottom right, the mouse wheel zooms it too (around the middle, as everything does), and what the map has to say ("Po kërkoj vendndodhjen…") shows over it only while it has something. As soon as the map stops after a drag or a zoom, or "Vendndodhja ime" moves it, the page refreshes in place for the map's middle and the distance, in place of a town, so the heading, the subcategories, the filters and the sort stay, the card stays open with its map as it was, the dots follow the new results, and the point shows as a chip, whose cross drops it and searches everywhere again. The filter form and the category pills carry the point along. Nothing links to the map page (`/harta`) since the welcome page's link went, but its addresses still work.
- `map_controller.js` draws the map with [Leaflet](https://leafletjs.com/) 1.9.4 (in `static/vendor/` with its BSD licence, 46 KB gzipped), which it fetches only when a map shows: on the map page, or once a category page's map card is opened. The tiles come from OpenStreetMap's tile server (see "Before production"); a CSS filter tones them down to the canvas's calm map, on every map of the site: pale grey ground and white roads, a hint of the parks' and the water's colours left, and dark grey on the dark theme. As the canvas draws them, the dots are the main colour edged in the page's own (white on the light theme, on the dark light dots edged in its dark), their numbered bubbles too; the pin is the location colour edged in white, with a white dot; Leaflet's + and − are white round buttons of 40 px in one pill, and its credit and a dot's popup stand on the surface.
- Anyone can post a listing ("+ Shto njoftim", `/shto`) with just a phone number or an e-mail address: no password, and no code. The listing is published at once, at its town's point on the map, or a property's or a car's where its seller put it on the form's own map (below), and buyers reach the seller through the phone button, "Shfaq numrin", a phone and the words in a wide plain pill (the grey of secondary controls, as the share and the report beside it are grey circles; the accent's light tint for a moment on 2026-10-12, until the user asked it "to the previous theme"; `.contact-bar` in `listing.css`), a little taller than the circles beside it (an envelope, "Shfaq email-in", for sellers who signed in by e-mail), which shows the number in its place, marked as what is picked is (the coral's tint, a border of it, the number in it), with a button beside it that copies it (`copy_controller.js`), and after that, for a number WhatsApp can reach (a mobile, +3556…, or one abroad; not an Albanian landline), a circle with WhatsApp's mark (`icons::WHATSAPP`) that opens a chat with it in a new tab or the app, its first message typed in for the buyer to send or change, naming the listing and its address so that a seller with many knows which ("Përshëndetje! Ju shkruaj për njoftimin „Volkswagen Golf 1.6, 2016“ në Pluribus: https://…", percent-encoded, a space %20 rather than a form's "+"; `listings::whatsapp_url`). In Albania WhatsApp is where buyers and sellers talk, and the copied number had to be pasted into a new chat by hand. The number stays out of the page until the reveal, which counts it, so a chat started that way is a contact like a call; on a split page from 960 px up the number, its copy and WhatsApp's circle take the line's whole width, and the share and the report stay in the row under it (`listing.css`). Sellers edit their listings from the listing page ("Ndrysho", seen only by them, with "Çaktivizo" and "Fshi" after it, each with its icon, a pencil, two bars and a bin, plain pills on a line of their own under the phone's on a split page, the share's circle beside the phone's) or from "Njoftimet e mia" (`/njoftimet-e-mia`); an edit goes through the same checks, keeps the listing's place in "newest first", and only the listing's own seller, while it is active or reserved, can make it. Sellers can also take a listing down for a while ("Çaktivizo", status `PAUSED`, migration 0011): it leaves the site but stays on "Njoftimet e mia" with "Aktivizo" to put it back where it was; its page, which "Njoftimet e mia" links to, stays for its seller and the moderators, "Çaktivizuar" by its path and a note that no one else sees it, "Aktivizo" among the seller's buttons, and the listing can be edited while it is down (its page answered "not found" to its own seller, and it couldn't be edited, before). A listing on sale or for rent (`db::SELLABLE_KINDS`) can also be said to be reserved ("Rezervo", status `RESERVED`: still up, "Rezervuar" by its path, still reached; "Hiq rezervimin" takes it off) or sold ("U shit", or "U dha me qira" for a rental; status `SOLD`, with `sold_at`, which the schema asks of a sold listing): its page stays for the links shared and kept, "Shitur" ("Dhënë me qira") by its path and no way to reach the seller, schema.org's `SoldOut` and `noindex` as before; it leaves every list but the kept listings of those who keep it, whose card says so over its photo (below), and "Aktivizo" puts it back on sale, `sold_at` cleared (`db::change_own_listing`, `ListingAction`, `format::sold`). A wanted ad, a job or a service has neither. The report reason "Nuk është më në dispozicion" was the only way to say a listing was gone. Each of the seller's buttons comes back to the page it was pressed on, the listing's or "Njoftimet e mia" (`saved::referring_page`), but "Fshi", after which the listing's page is gone. "Fshi" deletes it for good (status `DELETED`, after the browser asks to be sure). Both are on the listing page too, for its seller, and the status history records who did it. A listing stays up 60 days from when it is published, renewed or put back up (`db::LISTING_DAYS`, `expires_at`; those from before there was an expiry have none, and stay): every 15 minutes the server takes off the site those up or reserved whose time is up (status `EXPIRED`, the history saying "Skadoi"; `expiry.rs`, `db::expire_listings`), and tells their sellers three days before and when it happens (`WARN_DAYS`; for a store's listing, whoever posted it while they still post for the store, and else its owner, who may renew it as well, so that a warning doesn't go to someone who has left): a notification each (`EXPIRING`, with the expiry it warns of, so that a renewed listing is warned of again; `EXPIRED`) and an e-mail where the seller signs in with an address and allows e-mail, with the link to "Njoftimet e mia" (phone numbers have no WhatsApp template for these, so theirs go to the log). It looks through an index of the listings up or reserved with an expiry alone (`idx_listings_expiry`, migration 0031), so that the million listings without one cost it nothing. Within a week of it, "Njoftimet e mia" says when by the listing's price ("Skadon pas 3 ditësh", "Skadon nesër", "Skadon sot"; `format::expires_soon`, by Albania's clock) and offers "Rifresko" first among its buttons, as the listing's page does for its seller, the same words in its tooltip. An expired listing keeps its page for its seller and the moderators, "Skaduar" by its path and a note that no one else sees it, with "Rifresko" and "Fshi" alone among the seller's buttons, and stays on "Njoftimet e mia" so; those who keep it find it under "Nuk shfaqen më", "Skadoi", and a conversation about it says it is no longer on the site. "Rifresko" (`POST /listing/{number}/rifresko`, `db::renew_listing`) gives it 60 days from then, an expired one back up, and brings it back to the top of "newest first" (`bumped_at`) if it was published or last brought up a week ago or more (`BUMP_DAYS`), so that renewing is no way to stay on top; a paused or sold listing gets its 60 days when put back up, and neither expires nor renews meanwhile. The help page and the terms say so. The form is one page in three sections, each under its heading: "Çfarë shet" (the title, the description, the photos and the AI's button), "Kategoria dhe çmimi", and "Vendi dhe kontakti" ("Vendi" alone for someone signed in, whose name and contact are their account's: a typed name was kept, and shown nowhere, before); every field has its name over it, and a placeholder is an example ("p.sh. Golf 7, 2016, naftë"), where placeholders were all it had, which went as one typed and read badly to screen readers. A property or a car can say where it is more exactly than its town ("Vendi në hartë", under the town): a map opens under its button on the town chosen, the pin fixed in the middle as on the map page, and the map moved under it; the point is kept to about a hundred metres (three decimals of a degree, near enough for a buyer, not a private seller's front door) and must be within 40 km of the town (`POINT_NEAR_KM`), or the form says so; the listing's page, the map's dots and "Afër meje" all go by it (`map_pin_controller.js`, `db::set_listing_point`). Other categories stand at their town's point, as before. A new listing's form is kept in the browser as it is typed, the photos aside, which a page can't keep: back on an empty form, it offers what was begun ("Ke një njoftim të pambaruar, „Golf 7, 2016“, të ruajtur dje në 18:02"), and "Vazhdo" fills it in, the category's own fields too, or "Fshije" lets it go; a listing that goes up takes it with it (`draft_controller.js`). On a wide screen, beside a new listing's form, "Që të shitet më shpejt" says what helps (the first photo, a title with the make, model and year, a plain price, the 60 days and "Rifresko"), and stays in sight as the form scrolls; a phone has the form alone. The form asks for the chosen category's own properties too (a car's make, model, year, mileage, fuel...; `web/properties.rs`), which reload in a turbo-frame (`/shto/fushat?category=makina`) when the category changes; models narrow to the chosen make, required properties and numeric limits (`validation_json`, and next year at most for a year) are checked, and the values feed the cards, filters and search like the sample listings' do. Where a list can't be complete (car makes and models, phone brands: `attributes.allow_other`), it ends with "Tjetër", which opens a box to type the option in. A typed option is saved as an inactive option (`attribute_options.added_by_user_id` says who added it): the listing shows it and search finds it, but the form and the filters don't offer it until a moderator approves it. Moderators are the phone numbers and e-mail addresses `MODERATORS` in `.env` names; signed in with one, "Njoftimet e mia" links to "Për miratim" (`/moderim`, `web/moderation.rs`), which lists what waits, makes before their models, with how many listings have each and who typed it. "Mirato" approves one, its spelling put right if need be, and the lists offer it after the names that come before it alphabetically; "Bashko" merges it into the listed one the seller meant, chosen already when the names are alike (pg_trgm), and its listings take that one, along with a typed make's typed models. Anyone else gets "not found" there. Typing an option that exists ("bmw", or a make someone else typed) reuses it. Posts can have up to 8 photos (see "Photos").
- "Njoftimet e mia" tells sellers what their listings drew (`db::ListingStats`, `my_listings.html`). Over the list, three tiles add all of them up, each with its icon and its name, the count since the listings were posted, and the last seven days' under it ("+56 këtë javë", or "Asnjë këtë javë"): "Shikime", how many times their pages were seen (the views "Më të klikuarat" counts, the seller's own left out, in `listing_daily_stats.detail_views`); "Kontakte", how many times their number or e-mail address was shown ("Shfaq numrin", `phone_reveals`), which a line under the tiles explains; and "Ruajtje", how many people keep them with the heart (`saved_listings`; in the week, those kept in it). Under each listing's price, its own three counts by the same icons (the eye, the handset, the heart), each with a tooltip that gives the week's as well ("1 203 shikime, 3 këtë javë") and a name for screen readers. Views of the last minute show a minute later, when `views.rs` writes them; deleted listings and their counts are left out. The page ends in "Dil nga llogaria", which signs out (`/dil`), a little apart from what is over it.
- "Cilësimet" (`/llogaria/cilesimet`, `web/settings.rs`, `settings.html`), the last pill of every account page's row (`account_nav.html`: "Njoftimet e mia", "Lajmërimet" with how many are new, "Mesazhet", "Të ruajturat", "Cilësimet", the page it is on marked as what is picked is), holds the account's settings, in a column of 600 px in the middle of the page as the canvas has it, a section each under its heading, each form coming back to it with a line at its top saying what it did, or sent back with its own field saying why not: the name it shows by, tidied as a new account's (`account::display_name`); its phone number and its e-mail address, either added, or changed for a new one, by a code sent to the new one, the code page saying it saves rather than signs in (`login_codes.user_id`, migration 0033; `CodePurpose::adds_to`), after which it takes the place of the one of its kind on the account and on its listings that showed that one, verified as signing in verifies one, and the account's identity stops being a contact, which someone may sign in with one day (`auth_subject` becomes `user:<id>`); one the account has, or another account's, is refused before any code goes; either can be taken off while the other stays, its listings then showing the other; what it hears of, a box a line: the messages it left unread, the prices that drop on the listings it keeps, the saved searches' new listings, and for one with an address whether by e-mail too (`db::NoticePreferences`, `notification_preferences`, all on until it says otherwise), every e-mail of these ending with a link that stops its kind without signing in, as its box does (`/ndalo/{token}/…`, `web/unsubscribe.rs`), which mail programs offer as a button of their own (`List-Unsubscribe`, `List-Unsubscribe-Post`): opened, the link asks first, so that a link checker stops nothing, and "Rikthe" takes it back; those it blocked, each after their initial with "Zhblloko" (they were on "Njoftimet e mia" before); how many devices it is signed in on, with "Dil nga pajisjet e tjera" and "Dil nga llogaria"; and "Fshi llogarinë", which says what closing it does and takes FSHI typed, then does what "Users are anonymised, not deleted" says, signs out and shows "Llogaria u fshi", which names the stores closed with it where it owned any (it said nothing of them once). The privacy page says so, where it asked for an e-mail before.
- The form asks for what the seller writes first, the title, the description and the photos, then the rest; and with the AI set up (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in `.env`), "Plotëso pjesën tjetër me AI" between the two fills the rest in from them (`web/ai_fill.rs`, `ai_fill_controller.js`): the category, its properties (a car's make, model, year, mileage...; a flat's rooms, area, floor...), the kind of listing, the condition, the price and the town, and a property's zone. On an edit, whose form comes filled in, it fills only what is missing (the properties left empty), what the seller wrote standing, and looks at the listing's own photos too, the cover first (`db::listing_photo_keys`, read from the store), before any just picked. The server sends the title, the description and the first four photos (made 1024 px on their longer side) to Claude (`src/ai.rs`, Haiku 4.5 unless `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` names another), with instructions that list the form's own choices, built from the database, so it picks codes rather than inventing values; it answers by calling a tool, so its answer is data. The tool asks for the category's properties inside `properties` and takes nothing beside its fields, but Haiku sometimes puts them beside the category all the same (a Golf's make, model, year and the rest, for some photos, which left the form's details empty), or sends the group as JSON text: they count wherever they are (`given_properties`), and a property the form doesn't take is logged. Every value is checked as the form checks what a seller sends (`properties::check` and the like), and what doesn't fit is left out; a price only when the seller wrote one. The form then fills in as a seller would fill it: the category first, then its fields once they load, a make before its model, each picker following its select. A field the seller set themselves stays as they set it, and those the AI filled are tinted until changed; nothing is published until the seller publishes. A fill takes a few seconds and costs about a cent with three or four photos (the instructions, about 24 000 tokens with every category's properties and every make's models, are kept in Anthropic's prompt cache between fills); a seller (their account, or their network when not signed in) gets 10 an hour, and the whole site 2 000 a day. The title, the description and the photos go to Anthropic to be read; say so in the privacy policy. Without a key, the button isn't there and the form is as it was.
- Accounts come by themselves: someone who posts without being signed in types a phone number or e-mail address with the listing, which goes live at once under the account that contact has, or one made there and then, active, under the name typed ("Përdorues" without one; `db::poster_account`). Posting signs no one in, though, and its page says once that it is up and that changing it later takes signing in (the `posted` cookie). Posted signed in, its page says once that it is up too ("Njoftimi yt u publikua. Ndaje, që ta shohin më shumë njerëz.", the same cookie, `just_published`); and under either note, for a listing up, the ways to share it: "Ndaje", the phone's share sheet or the link copied where there is none, "WhatsApp", a message with its title and address to send anyone, and "Njoftimet e mia" (`share_note.html`). A seller had nothing to say their listing went up, nor a nudge to share it. Signing in takes a six-digit code sent to the contact, and typing it back signs the seller in for 30 days (a `session` cookie; only a SHA-256 hash of the token is stored, likewise for the codes); so typing someone else's number can put a listing under it, but never opens their account. The account button at the top bar's right end (`/llogaria`) signs in, or creates an account with a name, or leads a signed-in seller to their listings; its page says both, "Hyr ose krijo një llogari", a number used before signing in and a new one making an account, the name only for a new one (it said "Krijo një llogari" alone once, which a returning seller took for the wrong page). (Listings posted before this waited as a `DRAFT` for the code, `login_codes.listing_id`; an account still `PENDING` from then becomes active when it posts again.)
- A property says its zone in the town, where the town has known ones (Tirana's Blloku, Komuna e Parisit, Don Bosko... 41 of them, and Durrës's and Vlorë's; migration 0019): the posting form's "Zona", after the town, shows for property only, with the chosen town's zones (`narrow_controller.js`), and isn't required. On a new listing it follows what the seller writes: as they leave the title or the description, pick the town or change the category, the form asks `POST /shto/zona` for the zone the words name (`named::zone_in`, which reads it as the search does), the title before the description, which may name the places nearby too, and picks it, tinted as the AI's fields are (`listing_form_controller.js`, `guessZone`): in the town picked ("Apartament 2+1 në Bllok", in Tirana, is Blloku), or, with no town picked yet, a zone the words name with its town ("Plazh, Durrës"), which is picked too. A zone named with no town to say whose ("pranë plazhit" could be any seaside town's), one in another town than the one picked, and two zones (a description's "5 minuta nga qendra" beside "në Bllok") pick nothing. The zone follows the words as they change until the seller, or the AI, picks one, which then stands, as does one the form comes back with; a category whose listings have no zone asks nothing, and the edit form leaves the zone to the seller. A zone is a place under its municipality (`location_type` 'AREA'), the listing's own place: the listing page says "Blloku, Tiranë" and has "Zona" first among its details, the card "Blloku, Tiranë" (only "Blloku" where the search is in Tirana already), filtering by Tirana takes in its zones, the browse pages' Zona narrows to one of them (`zone`, above), and search finds the listing by its zone; a property search that names one puts Zona on (`named.rs`, below). The zones have no point of their own yet, so their listings stand on the map at their town's.
- A property on sale says under its price what a square metre of it costs, from its area: "925 €/m²" for a house at 185 000 € with 200 m² (`ListingDetail::area_price`), in its own currency and to the whole euro or lek; rents, wanted ads and "price on request" don't. It is a circle at the price's line's end, after the loan calculator's, its icon alone, as a car's price has it (the figure said to screen readers and under the pointer; a pill with the figure, half the line, until the user asked for the same on 2026-10-11), which opens how it was worked out (`area_price.html`): the price, the area, and the one over the other, rounded. The price and its buttons share the price's line (the user's ask of 2026-10-11; with "Llogarit kredinë" the price took a line of its own and the pill and the circle stood under it until then).
- In a zone with enough sales (Blloku, Plazh, Lungomare...), that bubble also compares the property's square metre with what one costs there on average among the zone's sales in its category ("Mesatarja e zonës"): green when it is 200 € or more under the average, red when it is as much over it, yellow in between (`ZONE_MARGIN_EUR`), and its card says the average and by how much, in the listing's currency. The averages are kept in the database, in the materialized view `zone_area_prices` (migration 0020): for each zone and category, the mean of the euros a square metre the active sales with a price and an area ask, leaving out the cheapest and the dearest tenth so that a mistyped price or area doesn't pull it, where at least five listings make one. The app rebuilds it along with `search_words`, at startup and every 15 minutes. A starting price ("nga") isn't compared.
- A car's page says how its price compares with what cars of its model ask, two years either side of its year: a round button at the price's line's end, after the loan calculator's, its tag alone on the colour of the verdict (a light one under our black tag on the light theme, a deeper one under our white on the dark: `--below-fill`, `--near-fill` and `--above-fill`; the verdict's words are said to screen readers and under the pointer; until the user asked for the circle on 2026-10-11 it was a bubble with the words, under the price), green "Çmim i mirë" 15 % or more under their median, yellow "Rreth mesatares" within 15 %, red "Mbi mesataren" 15 % or more over it (`car_prices.rs`, `car_price.html`), whose card, opening from the circle's right edge, says the verdict in its colour, the median and the middle half's prices ("Shumica kërkojnë 9 500–11 400 €"), in the listing's currency, rounded to the hundred euros or the ten thousand lekë, from how many cars, and that mileage, condition and options move a car's price besides. A price under half the median says nothing: it is likely mistyped, lekë as euros, or a lure. The cards of clear bargains say "Çmim i mirë" in a green pill at the photo's bottom right, as a dropped price has its own (one whose price dropped lately keeps the drop's pill alone); the verdict is worked out as the page is, from the price as it stands, so an edit shows at once. The figures are the materialized view `car_model_prices` (migration 0041): for each model and year among the cars up for sale at a price, the median and quartiles of the euros the cars of that model two years either side ask, where at least eight make one, cars for parts and starting prices left out; rebuilt with the others every 15 minutes, in about a second over the development database's 216 000 cars (11 460 models and years). The listing form says the same as a car's price is typed: once its model and year are chosen, under the price, "Volkswagen Golf 2014–2018 në shitje: shumica kërkojnë 9 500–11 400 €, mesatarja 10 300 €", and with a price, how it will come out on its page, or, far under, whether it is right and in the right currency (`/shto/cmimi`, `price_hint_controller.js`).
- Things on sale for more than 500 € or, about the same, 50 000 Lekë have the loan calculator at the price's line's end (`loan_controller.js`), as the user asked on 2026-10-11: a grey circle 36 px across, as tall as the price's line (48 px until the user asked on 2026-10-11 for it "a bit smaller, size of the row of the price"), its calculator's icon alone ("Llogarit kredinë" said to screen readers and under the pointer), before a property's price per square metre or a car's price comparison, which opens the calculator in a card under the line, over the page, as wide as the line, the circle filled with the main colour while it is open; a tap outside or Escape closes it. (Until then, as the canvas had it, a grey pill 48 px tall the line's whole width under the price, its icon muted before its words, its calculator in a white box under it in the page; a bubble beside the price before that, and a pill on a car's page alone.) The years and the yearly interest, 6% to start, give the monthly payment of an ordinary bank loan (equal payments), the down payment being 35% of the price, what Albania's banks ask for a used car, which they finance 60 to 65% of (Intesa Sanpaolo 65%, Tirana Bank 60%; `LOAN_DOWN_PERCENT` in `listings.rs`, which the page hands the controller), shown among the sums, and the loan the rest, with the interest and the total paid. It works in the page alone; rents, wanted ads and "price on request" don't have it (`ListingDetail::loan_amount`).
- Every listing page has its buttons under the price: the phone and the message, wide pills that say their names, the message filled in the page's colour, as one bar (`.contact-bar` in `listing.css`, an `aside`), which on a phone stands at the foot of the screen, from edge to edge on the page's colour, frosted in the page's own colour (`--frost-page`: the page's colour at 80%, the listing showing through it as it scrolls under, blurred; the user's asks of 2026-10-11, the header's white glass for a while that day, `--frost-bar`, until "make background of number and message button in phone same as the site background", and the page's grey before that), with a hairline over it, staying there while the listing scrolls, a slight shadow rising from it while it floats over the page (`sticky_controller.js`: an `IntersectionObserver` with the screen's bottom edge moved up a pixel tells floating, the bar on the edge and not showing whole, from settled), and settling after its description, each pill half of it (the page is a grid, `.listing`, so that the bar can be its last part there, sticky, and from 720 px up share a line with the circles); then the share and "Raporto", a flag in a circle like the share's link (for a car, Makina, the page is laid out as FINN has its, from 960 px up: the path at the page's top, without the banner's room over it that other listings keep, then the photos, a mosaic as the canvas has it, on a page 60rem wide: the slider two thirds of it, 430 px tall, its photo filling it, and the next two photos beside it, one over the other, 8 px apart, each going to its photo in the slider, the second saying how many more there are ("+4") and showing the filmstrip of them all as well (`.mosaic-side`, `slides_controller.js`'s `tile`; under 960 px, and with one photo, the slider alone, four by three; the photos ran across the page once, four by three); and, 22 px under those, two columns, at the left the title, 28 px as on every listing (24 on a phone), the one the seller wrote, with the one made from what the car is (`generated_title`: the make, the model, the engine, the year) at the head of the kind's line under it, at that line's size (`.listing-variant`; a car without a made title has its own alone, and the description no longer repeats the seller's over it), and the kind, then where it is, when it went up and its number ("Libohovë · 31.08.2026 · Nr. 833042", which a photo's corner says on every other page), a hairline under them, then the car's key facts, four to a line in equal columns (two on a phone), another hairline under them (the page's only two), the year, the mileage, the gearbox and the fuel, each with its icon, 24 px with 2 px lines on a round grey ground of 40 px (22 on 36 on a phone), equal grounds giving icons of every shape one weight and one edge for the words, and its name, 12 px and muted, over its value, 14 px semibold (the user's asks of 2026-10-11: "make it more uniform. Make logo bigger, name smaller and the value to be left as is", then, of the icons grown to 36 px bare, "it isnt doing it for me ... balance it yourself based on best practices") (`CAR_FACTS` in `listings.rs`, `.key-facts`), the description, the other details in a grid, a name with its icon over its value, three to a line (`.spec-grid`), the options, the details with several values, in columns with a check each under their own names (`.options`), and the map under its heading, "Lokacioni", which moves into the column at the right, under the buttons, a square as wide as the column, where that column leaves room under its content for it and its heading, the left column running on that far (`map_aside_controller.js`: one reading of the layout as the page shows and as the two columns' box changes size, the window or the description unfolded; it goes back under 960 px or when the room is gone, and loads as it comes near the screen either way); at the right, in a column of 21rem (wide enough for the message's pill and the two circles beside it), which goes with the page (it stuck under the top bar at first), the price, with no card round it (`.price-box`; it was in one until 2026-10-11), beside the title, in its row, as tall as the title's block, so that the two read as one band across the page, the figure's line level with the title's first, what qualifies it level with the kind's line and its hairline with the title's (the user's ask of 2026-10-11; it stood at the head of the deal's column, a little lower), 24 px and bold as on every listing, what kind of listing it is and what qualifies it ("Në shitje" in the accent's colour, "i diskutueshëm") on a line of its own under the price's, quietly and without brackets, as on every listing (`kind_label`, `price_negotiable`), and the loan calculator's circle at the price's line's end, as every listing has it (below), then the ways to reach the seller and under them, after a hairline with no heading, the seller (the user's asks: the seller over the ways to reach them until 2026-10-12, "put seller below buttons in desktop"; under "Shitësi", a heading as every page has it, until the user asked on 2026-10-11 to "remove shitesi label and separate shitesi with a line on top"; a small quiet line inside the card once), no box round either, as the canvas has it (`seller_card.html`'s `.seller-card`; a card like the price's until 2026-10-11): the initial in a ring of 48 px, as on every listing, since when they are on the site and after a dot how many listings they have, on one line that breaks before the dot if it must, each part kept whole ("Që nga tetor 2026 · 6 njoftime"; a business says so, "Biznes · që nga mars 2026 · 1 njoftim"; `seller_since_line`, `seller_count_text`), the shield beside the name, as on every page (it said "Numër i verifikuar" in its words on a line of its own once), and 16 px under them the bar's pills on a line each, 48 px tall and 8 px apart, the message's first, in the accent, its sides a little tighter, with the share and the report, circles of 46 px, beside it and level with it (they are one partial, `listing_actions.html`, which on the seller's own page stays under the seller with their own buttons; the bar is one partial, `contact_bar.html`, which a car's page includes under the seller and every other under the price; under 960 px the section and its card are no box, `display: contents`, so that the bar is the phone's foot bar or shares the circles' line as on every page, its heading and the seller's row ordered after the description as the section was): the deal (`.listing-deal`), which on every other page, and on a phone, is no box at all, its parts in the flow with the rest; `car_page` in `listings.rs`, `.listing-car` in `listing.css`. A property's page (Prona and every category under it) is laid out as a car's, as the canvas has it (`deal_page` in `listings.rs`, MAPPED; its photos ran across the page once, the rest under them in a column as wide as a line reads well, its details as pills): the mosaic, the title, the line with where it is, when it went up and its number, its key facts in the row between the hairlines, its rooms, area, floor and furnishing (`PROPERTY_FACTS`), the description, the details left in the grid (its zone first, where it has one), its amenities under their own name with a check each (the multi-select's options, as a car's equipment is), the map under "Lokacioni", and the deal at the right, the loan calculator and the price per square metre at the price's line's end; every other listing's page is split from 960 px up, as the classifieds sites have it, and narrower than the site's column, 60rem in the middle of it, the rows of listings under it with it, and in the middle of the screen too, as much room under the photo as the banner's room over the path (below) (the page's box, `.listing-page`, which is no box at all otherwise): the photo at the left in a frame taller than wide, three by four, seven twelfths of the page (58%, 541 px at most, 2rem from the column), its blurred copy filling what it doesn't, and in a column of the other five twelfths at its right the title, level with the photo's top, the kind and the price, the bar's two pills on a line each, the message's first, the share and the report beside the phone's under it, the details, the description and the seller's card, packed at the column's top rather than spread over the photo's height, the listings like it under both; the photo and its filmstrip stay put under the top bar while the column beside them scrolls, and go with the page once it has all gone by (sticky within their grid row; the frame no taller than the screen leaves room for under the bar, so that the strip stays in sight); `.listing-split` and `.listing-side` in `listing.css`, for every listing but those `deal_page` in `listings.rs` takes, Makina or any category under Prona, which is the map's test too (`wide_photos` until 2026-10-11)). The seller comes after the description, under a hairline with no heading ("Shitësi" its name to screen readers; the user's ask of 2026-10-11, "remove shitesi label and separate shitesi with a line on top": it had a heading of its own, as the details and the description have theirs; the seller's own car or property keeps its "Njoftimi yt", the line over that), as far under it as those stand under what is over them (1.75rem, on a phone too), the hairline 20 px over the seller, and from 720 px up under the ways to reach them, which come first (the user's ask of 2026-10-12, "put seller below buttons in desktop"; on a phone the seller stays over the bar, which settles under them, and the page keeps the seller first for screen readers), in a card across the page: a small initial, the name, since when they are on the site and how many listings they have, linking to their profile with all their active listings (`/perdorues/{id}` for a private seller, `/biznes/{slug}` for a business); the ways to reach them are the buttons under the price alone, but on a car's page, whose card holds them (above). The category's path sits above the photo as a category page's does, in pills: the back button, three levels down the round way beyond them (the category above its parent's, by its icon), the category above the listing's, quieter, and the listing's own, filled ("Automjete", "Makina"; `.listing-path`, below). At the line's right end, as the classifieds sites have them, three round buttons like the back one (`.listing-steps`): to the category's list, by the list's icon as the view menu has it, and to the listing before this one on that list and the one after it, in the list's own order, newest first, among the active listings not from an account the visitor hides (`db::listing_neighbours`, by the page's sort key, `sort_at` then `id`, which the category's index serves, the row comparison its condition); one with nowhere to go stays, greyed, so the three keep their places. On a phone, where the path's pills are their icons alone but the listing's own, a long name on that one ("Mbushës për makina elektrike", until the categories' names became one word on 2026-10-11) left the three no room on its line and pushed them under it: the pill goes to its icon alone too then, round and in its tint (`fit_controller.js`, which tries the buttons on its line as the page shows, as the fonts come in and as the screen's width changes, and `.is-tight` in `listing.css`), its name still the link's for screen readers; without scripts the buttons take the line under. A description longer than a dozen lines shows that many, the last three fading out, with "Shfaq të gjithë përshkrimin" under them in the page's colour with a plus, which unfolds it for good (`fold_controller.js`, which folds only one longer than that, so that nothing is hidden before its length is known; without scripts it shows whole). A description longer than a dozen lines folds (`fold_controller.js`, `.description-fold`); its button, "Shfaq të gjithë përshkrimin", waits with the `hidden` attribute until the controller finds the description long, and its own display rule would override the attribute, so `listing.css` hides it by the attribute too (`.description-more[hidden]`): it showed under a two-line description once.
- Under the seller's card, a property's page (Prona and every category under it) and a car's (Makina) show where it is on a map (`ListingMap`, `listing_map_controller.js`), half as tall as the photos over it: 12:5 across the page's column on a phone, where the photos are 6:5, and on a wide screen half their height, `min(35vh, 19rem)`, across the page, as wide as the seller's card over it (`listing.css`). A listing's point is its place's, its town's for a zone without one of its own (`db::listing_by_public_no`'s latitude and longitude), so the map has a circle 600 m round it, in the location colour, rather than a pin, fitted into the map, and the place's name over its top left in a pill like the photo counter's ("Blloku, Tiranë"). Leaflet and the tiles load only as the map comes near the screen (an `IntersectionObserver`), sharing `controllers/leaflet.js` with the browse pages' map; a finger scrolls the page over it, two pinch it about its middle, a mouse drags it, the + and − at its bottom right zoom it, and the wheel scrolls the page. It shares the map pages' dark theme (`.map-canvas`), and its layers stay under the sticky header.
- Under a listing, "Njoftime të ngjashme" shows the listings like it from other sellers, at most two from any one, the most alike first (`db::related_listings`), as cards like the browse pages', eight at a time, the next eight loading as the last come into view, as the browse pages' do (a lazily loaded frame asking `/listing/{number}/te-ngjashme?nga=8`, then `nga=16`, `listings::related`, which fetches one more than it shows to know whether to ask on; without scripts its link leads back to the listing), and nothing when there are none. The section starts just out of view: the listing itself is at least a screen tall (`.listing`'s `min-height`, the screen less the header and the gap over the section, in `svh` so that a phone's toolbars count), so that a short listing's page shows the listing alone until the reader scrolls, as a long one does. Its rows pack at its top rather than share that height out (`align-content: start`; a grid stretches its rows otherwise, which grew the path's row apart from the photo under it), so that the path and its buttons stand at the same height on every listing as one steps through them. From 960 px up a split page has room over the path for a banner, empty for now and kept for an ad (`.listing-banner`, the page's first part, so that its min-height takes it in), a gutter under it: half of what the screen leaves round the path and the photo's frame, so that the listing stands in the middle of the screen, as much room under the photo as over the path, "Njoftime të ngjashme" at the screen's foot, and, since the frame is the same on every split listing (three by four of seven twelfths of the page, or as tall as the screen leaves it), the path keeps its height whatever the column beside the photo holds, a column taller than the photo running on under the screen (`.listing-split > .listing-banner`: half of the screen less the header, the gutters, the path's buttons and the frame, none where the frame takes all the room); none on a car's page, which starts at the top, nor on a property's, where a fifth of the photos' height, `min(14vh, 7.6rem)`, stood empty under the header until 2026-10-10 and read as a gap; it can come back with a banner to fill it. As the big sites do it, on a small scale: a few hundred candidates come cheaply off the category's indexes (the closest in price either way, the nearest, the newest, and those sharing a narrow choice such as the model), and a score ranks them by the choices they share (a model three, any other a half, so that many small likenesses such as the colour don't outweigh the price), the price (none at half or double), the place (none 50 km away) and how new they are (none two months on). The big sites add what visitors do: listings viewed in the same visits as this one, and learned likeness (embeddings) over titles and photos; both need the views recorded first.
- What keeps spam out without passwords: a listing posted without signing in belongs to the contact typed, which nothing proves (the price of posting without a code: anyone can type anyone's number, and buyers who tap the phone button see it), so a network (IP address) posts at most 20 listings an hour that way (in memory, `post.rs`); signing in proves the contact: a code works for 10 minutes and 5 guesses, and a contact gets one code a minute and five an hour, and a network twenty an hour, which also caps what a bot could spend on SMS; an account posts at most 5 listings a day, never the same title twice in a day, and at most 2 links per listing; a hidden field only bots fill in, and a form sent less than 3 seconds after it was shown, are refused; and forms posted from other sites are refused (`Origin` check, `SameSite=Lax` cookies). The limits are constants at the top of `account.rs` and `post.rs`.
- Anyone can report a listing ("Raporto", a flag in a circle among the buttons under the price; `/listing/{number}/raporto`, `report.html`): what is wrong with it, among seven reasons, each with what it covers ("Mashtrim", "Njoftim i rremë", "I ndaluar ose fyes", "Spam ose i përsëritur", "Nuk është më në dispozicion", "Të dhëna të gabuara", "Tjetër", which needs a word on what), and an explanation if they like (`web/safety.rs`, `db/safety.rs`); the thanks, once sent, offer to block the seller too (below). The page is as the canvas draws it: a column of 640 px in the middle of the page, its heading (30 px, 26 on a phone), the listing under it as a small white card, its photo beside its title and its price and town under it, a chevron at its end (`db::report_target` reads the first photo, the price and the municipality; its title alone, a link, once), the reasons a row each in one white box between hairlines, each its radio, its name and what it covers (boxes of their own once, the one picked outlined in the accent), then the explanation and the button, 18 px apart; the thanks a green notice with a check, what blocking does, and "Blloko shitësin" and "Kthehu te njoftimi", grey, side by side, each across the column on a phone. It takes being signed in: someone who isn't is asked to, and comes back to the report after (the `kthehu` cookie the hearts use), since reports take listings off the site and a stranger shouldn't be able to do that to someone else's. A person reports a listing once until a moderator decides on it, and 20 listings a day at most; its own seller can't. When three people (`HIDE_AFTER`) have reported a listing for a serious reason (a scam, fake, prohibited or offensive, spam: not "wrong details", which is for a moderator to see to), it comes off the site (`SUSPENDED`): gone from the pages and its link, but for its seller, whose "Njoftimet e mia" says "Në shqyrtim" and whose page says why, and the moderators; the status history and `moderation_actions` record it as the rule's doing. On `/moderim` the reported listings come first, those taken off the site first, each with how many reported it, the reasons with how many gave each, what they wrote, and two buttons: "Mbaje" dismisses its reports and puts it back as it was (active or reserved), "Hiqe" takes it down for good (`REJECTED`, "Hequr nga moderatorët" to its seller, who can still delete it). "Për miratim (N)" on a moderator's "Njoftimet e mia" counts these too.
- A visitor who has reported a listing can block its seller ("Blloko shitësin" on the report page's thanks, which say what it does, and then that the seller is blocked, with where to let them be again; `/listing/{number}/blloko`, `user_blocks`; the listing's page itself offers no block, so that its buttons stay few): the account that posted the listing, and so all it posts, leaves their welcome page, category and search pages, the map, "Më të klikuarat" and the listings under another, filtered in the queries (`ListingFilter::hidden_accounts`) or, for the rows read from memory, after (`safety::without_hidden`). A blocked seller's listing still opens from its link, saying "E ke bllokuar këtë shitës" and that "Cilësimet" lets them be again, which lists those blocked ("Ata që ke bllokuar"), each with "Zhblloko". It is the one block a conversation's "Blloko" makes too (`user_blocks`, `web/messages.rs`): whichever page it was made on, the blocked person's listings leave the blocker's pages, neither can write to the other, and either page lets them be again. The pages find the visitor's blocks with one query when there is a session and none otherwise (`blocked_for_session`).
- A listing can be promoted (`db/promotions.rs`, `promotion_products`, `listing_promotions`), until there are payments by a moderator, on `/moderim`, from its number: "I veçuar" (3 or 7 days) puts it at the top of the results of its category and of every search it matches, two such there in the results' own look (small cards, big ones or the list's lines, as the page shows them), each with its price on the photo on a band of the accent across the figure's foot, as the listing's page marks its price (`.price-amount`), the month and "(i diskutueshëm)" off it (`.card-figure`, the figure a span of its own on every card; screen readers hear "I promovuar" before it), in place of a small dark pill saying "I promovuar" over the photo's top left, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (the band the dark theme's, the accent's deep tint, in both themes, `--price-mark-photo`, as dark as the photo's shade under the white figure: the user's pick of the same day, the accent itself before it), taken in another order each time from up to 8 so that each has its turn (`db::featured_cards`, `ListingPage::featured`), and not shown again among the first page's (until 2026-10-11 "Të veçuara", a row of small cards that slid sideways over the results, its note "Të promovuara · Si?" leading to `/promovo`: the user asked for the listings to come sooner on a phone); "I theksuar" (7 days) gives its card a panel in the warm yellow's tint wherever it shows (`.card.is-highlighted`), the page colour's tint being for what is picked or open; "Ngritje në krye" takes it to the top of "newest first" at once, as "Rifresko" does but whenever; "Në krye të kategorisë" (7 days) does that every day while it lasts (`promotions.rs`, `db::tick_promotions`, from the promotions on, never a scan of the listings). Given again before it ends, its time adds to what is left. What each does is copied onto the listing (`featured_until`, `highlighted_until`, `bumped_at`), which every card reads, without moving `updated_at`, which the sitemaps give search engines. Its seller hears of it (a notification, `PROMOTED`, a megaphone), and under their buttons on its page reads what promotes it and until when, as "Njoftimet e mia" says by its price; with none, "Promovoje" leads to `/promovo`, which lists what each promotion does and costs (the seeded prices, placeholders in euros) and how to get one: by writing in with the listing's number, in the e-mail's subject. A moderator ends one before its time with "Ndalo" (the listing keeping what another of its kind still gives); each giving and ending is in the moderators' record (`moderation_actions`, `GRANT_PROMOTION`, `END_PROMOTION`), and a listing's page offers a moderator "Jep një promovim", its number already in the form.
- Signing in with a code proves the phone number or e-mail address it was sent to (`user_verifications`, type `PHONE` or `EMAIL`, set by `account::check_code`). A listing whose shown number (or address) is one the account that posted it proved has a shield beside the seller's name, in the green of a good price, which says "Numër i verifikuar" (or "Email i verifikuar") as a tooltip and to screen readers; a profile says it in words under the name, and a business's when one of its people proved its public number. A number changed since isn't verified. Since anyone can post under any number without a code, a listing posted that way under a proved number shows it too: it goes to that number's account, whose owner sees it on "Njoftimet e mia" and can delete it. In test mode (`SIGN_IN_CODES_ON_SCREEN`) anyone can prove any number.
- Every select is chosen from a list in the page, not the phone's own picker (a wheel on iPhones, a dialog on Android): categories, towns, the kind of listing, the condition, the price, the currency, the order, a car's make and model. `picker_controller.js` puts a button where the `<select>` was and opens a list under it, styled like the order's menu and as the redesign's canvas draws its dropdowns (raised over the page with no outline, its corners 16 px, its rows about 48 px tall and grey under the pointer, the choice in the main colour), groups under their headings (a town under its county). A list with groups folds, towns under their county and models under their make, as does a category list (`data-picker-tree-value`): a group's or a category's arrow opens it, the list opens with only the path to the choice open, and a list of one group (the models of the make chosen) comes open; a list wider than a narrow field grows from the field's side nearer the middle of the screen; where a category can be chosen itself (the welcome form, the filters, the map), its name chooses it and its arrow opens it. The posting form's groups nest by their path ("Automjete › Pjesë"), the other lists by the levels `category_choices` gives them. The select stays, unseen, and is what the form sends; choosing sets it and fires its `change`, and the button follows the select however a script sets it (the kind of listing a category takes, its currency, a model that no longer fits its make), the list being built anew each time it opens. It takes the arrow keys (right and left open and close a category), Enter, Escape and a first letter; without JavaScript a select is just a select. In the browse row's filter dropdowns the list is the picker itself, always shown in its card with no button (`data-picker-inline-value`), named as the select is and reached with Tab, the choice or the first option taking the focus, the empty choice ("Të gjitha") left out, as nothing is picked yet; Escape there folds the card.
