- A category page says where it is by its name (`browse.html`): its way back, a chevron (`back_controller.js`'s link), stands before the page's name, in the words' colour, a grey circle under the pointer, in a target as tall as the name's line and .03em higher, within a pixel of the middle of its capitals (`.title-back` in `browse.css`; on a circle in the accent for a moment on 2026-10-11, as the user asked, a listing's path's way back with it, and back to the bare chevron at their word the same day), and goes to the page before as the browser's does when that was one of the site's pages in this tab (Turbo numbers its history entries, `restorationIndex`), and a level up otherwise or without scripts (the welcome page from a main category), keeping the words, the town and the map pin as the category links do (`parent` in `browse.rs`); a subcategory's page names the category above it nowhere else, the user having asked on 2026-10-11 for the small muted line over its name that did to go (`.browse-up`, which had stood there since the breadcrumb went earlier that day). The page's heading, its name, is 30 px bold (26 px on phones; `.browse-title`). Until 2026-10-11 a breadcrumb stood over the name, as the canvas draws it (`.breadcrumb`, which a listing's page keeps, below): a line of words 40 px tall, the chevron first, then the category above in the muted grey ("Kreu" over a main category), and after a › the page's own name in the text's colour, semibold; the user asked for a main category's to go (it said "Kreu" over the page's name), then, so that the listings come sooner on a phone, for a subcategory's (it said the page's name twice). The breadcrumb's words stand at the font's own line height (`line-height: normal`), so that their capitals sit in the line's middle, level with the chevron's, at every text size (within half a pixel at 13 and 16 px, the rest exactly): at the page's 1.55 the browser rounded their baseline up to 1.4 px off it, which left the chevron low beside them (the user, 2026-10-11). (It said where it was in pills once: back and, three levels down, a round button to the category above its parent, the way up quieter, and the page's own filled, the heading, which showed the categories at its level after it on a tap; the canvas's breadcrumb took their place on 2026-10-11, and with it the other categories at a page's level went, a page's way up leading to them.) Under the name, its subcategories, when it has any, as the welcome page has its main categories: one line of tiles that slides sideways on every screen, each its icon on one of Material's soft shapes over its name, a finger or a trackpad sliding it and a mouse dragging it (`.tiles` in `welcome.css`, `drag_row_controller.js`; from 720 px up `.subcategories` in `browse.css` takes back the tiles' room at the line's ends, so that the first shape stands where the page's column starts, as the results do), as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (pills until then, a touch smaller than the welcome page's, wrapping on a wide screen). The heading, the subcategories and the row of buttons are one head (`.browse-head` in `browse.css`, a grid, in `browse.html`), one under another; what the row opens, the map's card, the filters' dropdowns and the order's and the view's menus, is placed by the head, under it, so that the row's line, which slides sideways on a phone, cuts none of them. On a phone the row is that one line (`.browse-filters-line`): Filtrat, the filters picked, the map, the order, a grey pill there as the others are, and the view first, the dropdowns of the other filters after them, so that the results start on the first screen once a filter is on (the map, the view and the order stood on a line of their own under the others until the user's ask of 2026-10-11). Once a town or a point is picked, the map's button is its pin alone, round, the place's chip in the row saying which (it said "Tiranë" beside the chip "Tiranë"), the place its tooltip and what screen readers hear (`.map-button.is-set`). The five parts of the site that had colours of their own (Elektronikë and Punë in Claude's blue, Shtëpi / kopsht and Prona in its olive, Automjete in a dark red, named on the body as `data-section`) lost them on 2026-10-11, in the redesign's first step: one main colour on every page (`base.css`). The row's pills and round buttons are a chip's 36 px (`--row`, `--size-chip`), as every other pill on the site is, the filters' sheet's and its dropdowns' choices among them, their words 14 px (13 px on phones) and their icons 16 to 17 px, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 ("make the filter pills a bit smaller": 40 px, the back button's size, with words of 15 px until then); on a phone (under 720 px) a finger's target is bigger than the button it sees: half the gaps round it (an `::after` of the link, from the border's outer edge), its neighbours' meeting it halfway. (Until 2026-10-11 the subcategories were pills there too, about 35 px tall, on one line that slid sideways; they are the welcome page's tiles since, on every screen.) A listing's page says where it is in the same breadcrumb, at its top, above the photo (`listing.html`, `.listing-path`): the way back, leading to its category without scripts, then the category above its own, quieter (over a main category the welcome page, "Kreu"), and its own in the text's colour, underlined in the accent as the site marks where the reader is (the user's ask of 2026-10-12, "underline the current category in the breadcrumb"; under the pointer only until then), a link to its page too (`parent` and `category` in `listings.rs`); its status and the step buttons share the line (until 2026-10-11 it had the back button, three levels down a round way beyond to the category above its parent, `way_up.html`, and two pills, its own filled, which on a phone went to their icons where the line had no room, `fit_controller.js`; and a split page kept a banner's room over it, empty). Search engines get the page's trail as schema.org `BreadcrumbList` data instead (`seo.rs`), and the count is plain words under the head, in the muted colour, small, before the filters' chips ("3k njoftime" then "Elbasan ×"), the chips filled with the main colour, as the filters on are, smaller, their words semibold, its thousands said as "k", whole ones (`format::short_listing_count`: "850 njoftime", "1k+ njoftime" as the page's count stops at 1 000, "10k+ njoftime" as the one asked for after it does at 10 000). Under "Më të klikuarat", or under the head where the page has none, the listings have a heading of their own, like that row's, a section's title as the canvas has it, 22 px bold (`.results-head`): on a category page their order ("Njoftimet më të reja", "Njoftimet më të lira"; in English the order's word first, "Newest listings"; `Filters::sort_adjective`), elsewhere the order alone ("Më të përshtatshmet"), so that the list says what it is where it starts; the order itself is the row's last button (on a phone one of its first). On a phone it is closer to what is over it and to the cards (20 px and 10 px, as "Më të klikuarat"'s title is), the user's ask of 2026-10-11. (The heading's order's words opened the orders once, and named the category and the kind of listing after them.)
- The import map in `base.html`'s head (`web::import_map`) loads the JavaScript modules directly, with no bundler. To upgrade Turbo or Stimulus, put its file in `static/vendor/` and change its name in `web::TURBO` or `web::STIMULUS`.
- Every script comes minified from the server (`web::script`, `web::minified_js`, with Oxc): without its comments and white space, the names it declares shortened (a module's top-level ones too, which nothing outside it sees; not a property's, which another module or the page may use, a controller's `static targets`, its `connect`), and what nothing refers to taken out; a licence notice that opens the file stays. Turbo goes in 26 KB gzipped rather than 45, Stimulus in 11 rather than 15, the site's own modules in about half their bytes (`application.js` 1.7 KB rather than 3.8; the welcome page's scripts 45 KB in all rather than 77), which was Lighthouse's "Minify JavaScript" (31 KiB) and, with less than 20 KiB of Turbo unused on a page load, its "Reduce unused JavaScript". The files in `static/` stay as written; a script is minified when first asked for and again once its file has changed, so a change shows at once, and one that doesn't parse (one being edited) goes as it is, with a warning in the log.
- The site follows the device's light or dark setting until the visitor picks a theme with the menu's switch ("Tema e errët"; the header had a toggle too until 2026-10-11). The choice is kept in `localStorage`, and a small inline script in `base.html` (`web::THEME_SCRIPT`) applies it before the first paint, so pages never flash the wrong theme; so it does the rest of the look the menu's "Pamja dhe gjuha" offers, kept the same way (`look_controller.js`): the size, compact, normal or large (`data-size` on `<html>`, the user's ask of 2026-10-11, "compact normal and large view", in place of the text's four sizes and the page's four of earlier that day; `web::THEME_SCRIPT` turns a text's or a page's size picked before into the size nearest it, once: a smaller page or text compact, a large text large). Large scales the root's size, and every size in rem with it, by 1.1875 (`--text-scale`), the bar's and the buttons' heights staying in px. Compact draws the whole page at 90%, a finger's targets still 36 px at least on a phone, or, with a mouse (`pointer: fine`), where targets can be smaller, at 80% with the text 1.1875 times bigger in it, so that the words are about as big and more of the page shows (measured on a 1280 px screen: twelve cards whole in the first screen of a category's page where normal shows eight) (the page's size, 70, 80, 90 or 100%, was the user's ask of the same day: "make everything smaller ... photo size would still be the same"); it draws the whole page smaller as a browser's own zoom does (`zoom` on `<html>` by `--page-scale`: the text, the buttons, the fields, the gaps and the icons together) while what fills the screen's width keeps its size on it (a phone's two photos to a line; the frame's column, `--content-width` divided by the scale, and with it the desktop grid's least column, so four photos as big; a list view's photo, a listing's photos and a big card divided by it too; the maps drawn at their own scale, `zoom` the other way on `.map-canvas` and `.map-pin-canvas`, where Leaflet measures the pointer in the screen's pixels; the shelves' cards shrink, more of them in sight), every length measured on the screen (vh, svh, dvh, vw) divided by `--page-scale` where it is set and every script that turns the screen's pixels into the page's (a mouse dragging a row or the photos, the message sheet's place, height and drag, the filters' room under the row, the map's place beside the price) dividing by the element's `currentCSSZoom`; and the accent, mint, the user's pick of 2026-10-11 (lime before it), or one of four as bright as it, lime, coral, orange and blue, as the user asked the same day, or a deep royal blue, #275BFF, as they asked on 2026-10-12, with white words on it where the others have dark ones and its lines a lighter blue on the dark theme (`data-accent`: the logo's dot, the unread dots and counts, the front page's search button and the bookmark, "Posto" and every button that does a page's thing, a filter that is on, the band across a listing's price; four darker ones before, dark red, terracotta, blue and olive, which `web::THEME_SCRIPT` turns into coral, orange, blue and mint for a visitor who had picked one). The language, the menu's last row, goes through `/gjuha` as the footer's does, so it is kept on the account too.
- Every page's header starts with the menu's three lines ("Menyja" for screen readers; over the rail's middle from 720 px up), which open the menu as the canvas draws it: a drawer 380 px wide at the screen's left (on a phone nine tenths of the screen, the dimmed page showing beside it, as the user asked on 2026-10-11; the whole screen until then, four fifths for a while that day), white over the page dimmed, appearing without sliding, its cross, a tap on the dimmed page or Escape closing it, focus going to its cross as it opens and back to the button as it closes; the page behind stays still and out of reach meanwhile (a modal `<dialog>`), and Turbo keeps the page closed for the back button. Under the pointer the button sits on a grey circle (its word, shown until 2026-10-11, was underlined). The drawer's link rows underline their words in the accent under the pointer and fill their icons with it, rather than the row taking a grey ground (2026-10-11; `.menu-row-word`); a row with a control, the theme's switch among them, has no hover of its own, and the links about the site underline the same way.
- From 720 px up the rail, the menu folded, stands down the screen's left under the menu's lines, as the user drew it on 2026-10-11 (`.rail` in `base.css`, `base.html`): 72 px wide (`--rail-width`, 0 on phones, which what is measured against the screen's width leaves out), the bar's colour with a hairline at its right, staying put as the page scrolls, the page a grid from that width (the English offer and the bar across the top, the rail beside the page and the footer). It is the open menu's places in icons alone, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 ("a replica of the opened menu in the sidebar of the desktop version. Use only icons"): at its top posting, a square of 56 px with soft corners in "Posto"'s colours and the plus; then the account's pages (Njoftimet e mia, Të ruajturat, Kërkimet e ruajtura, Lajmërimet, Mesazhet, Cilësimet), a hairline, every main category in the menu's order with its icon (`browse::menu_categories`), a hairline, the dark theme's switch (`theme_controller.js`, pressed while it is on) and the other language (English, or Shqip on an English page, through `/gjuha`), a row of 40 px each, its icon 22 px, drawn a little thinner, on a pill 48 by 32 px, its name for screen readers and the pointer's tooltip (`title`), the one the page is on a grey pill (`web/rail.rs` keeps the request's path while the page is made and says which of the account's places it is, a conversation the conversations'; a category's page, a subcategory's too, says its main category, `set_category`); under the pointer the icon fills with the accent, as the menu's rows' do. The notifications have their count of those new and the conversations of those unread (`inbox_controller.js`, by its values for the notifications), the listings kept the dot in the accent while some dropped their price unseen (`heart_controller.js`). Taller than the screen, the rail scrolls by itself, its bar unseen. The menu's size and accent, which need a choice, and its pages about the site stay the menu's (the footer has those too). Until then the rail was 88 px wide, with posting, the front page ("Kreu"), the search ("Kërko"), the listings kept and the conversations, each its icon over its name. Phones have no rail: the menu holds its places.
- Every response tells the browser how to keep the site safe (`web::security_headers`). Its Content-Security-Policy lets a page run scripts from the site's own files and, of those in the page itself, only `base.html`'s two, by their SHA-256 hashes (the theme's and the import map, worked out once as the server starts), so a script that found its way into a page, in a listing's words or a crafted link, doesn't run; a script added to a page needs its hash there, or a file of its own under `static/js/`, which `responses_tell_browsers_how_to_keep_the_site_safe` checks. Styles come from the site and the page (a card's placeholder photo, Leaflet's positions, Turbo's progress bar); pictures from the site, from anywhere over HTTPS (OpenStreetMap's tiles, a CDN's photos with `MEDIA_URL`), and as `data:` and `blob:` (the placeholders, the posting form's previews); requests go only to the site; no plugins, no form sent to another site, and no other site may frame a page (`frame-ancestors 'none'`, and `X-Frame-Options: DENY` for older browsers). The rest: `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, a `Permissions-Policy` that gives no page the camera, microphone, payments or USB and the location to the site's own pages alone ("Afër meje", "Vendndodhja ime"), and `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin`. `Strict-Transport-Security` is for whatever serves the site over HTTPS to send (Cloudflare can), since the app itself speaks plain HTTP behind it. An analytics or other outside script added later needs its origin in `script-src`.
- Everything but the logo is set in one typeface, [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), Vercel's: headings, prices and text differ only in weight and colour. It is a variable font, every weight in one file (29 KB, Latin subset, which covers Albanian), in `static/vendor/fonts/` with its licence (`OFL-Geist.txt`). The site serves it itself, so visitors make no extra connection and their IPs don't go to Google or Vercel, and `base.html` preloads it so text rarely waits for it. `base.css` names the weights it uses: light (300) for the welcome page's intro line, regular (400) for body text, medium (500) for chips and detail values, semibold (600) for headings, labels, card titles and buttons, bold (700) for prices, and extra bold (800) for the initials in avatars. Its sizes are a scale of seven, `--text-xs` to `--text-3xl` (12.8, 14, 16, 18, 20, 25.6 and 36 pixels, in rem so that they shrink with the page on phones), from captions and labels through body text, leads, section headings and page titles to a listing's price; the only others are fixed where a pixel size matters (form fields at 16px at least on phones, which iPhones zoom into otherwise; the posting form's car fields at 14px; the map's bubbles at 11px), and a card's title, at .95rem (13.3px on a phone), a step over the details line under it, whose town read nearly as large before; that line's pin is the size of its letters. Headings (`h1`, `h2`, through `--font-heading`, which is `--font` now, in bold) and the logo were set apart until 2026-10-10, in Inter Bold and Playfair Display SemiBold (a display serif of the kind Medium's wordmark is set in), each a file of its own (48 and 23 KB) that `base.html` preloaded too; since then Geist is the site's one font, in its black weight for the logo (below), and a page fetches one font file rather than three. While the look is being settled, `TYPE_TRIAL=true` puts an "Ff" button in the top bar whose panel tries other typefaces on the page for that browser alone: the headings' and the text's each from a list of the site's own and seventeen of Google Fonts', Inter and Playfair Display among them (fetched as picked; the policy lets Google Fonts in only then), with sliders for the headings' weight and the subheadings', the text's and its strong's (`type_trial_controller.js`, which sets `--font-heading`, `--font`, `--heading-weight`, `--subheading-weight`, `--regular` and `--semibold` on the root and keeps the choice in `localStorage`; "Rikthe" puts the site's own back). The same panel tries an icon library on the page, Lucide, Tabler, Phosphor or Heroicons: each icon that has a name in the library (the UI's, tagged `data-icon` with its Lucide name; the categories' by their slug, the pill's link or the page's path; the three views' by their class) gets the library's drawing in its own `<svg>`, fetched from unpkg as picked, with the library's box and strokes (Phosphor's filled, Heroicons' at 1.5); the details' icons and any the library hasn't stay the site's own, so a page is always a mix, as it would be if one were adopted. Phosphor was adopted on 2026-10-11 (`icons.rs`), so the panel's Phosphor shows the site's own icons but for their tone shapes.
- Every page ends in a footer with the pages about the site (`web/pages.rs`): "Si funksionon", "Këshilla sigurie", "Kushtet", "Privatësia" and "Kontakt", where people look for them when deciding whether to trust a site; as the canvas draws it, in the muted grey under a hairline, the links in a row at the left with the other language after them, underlined ("English", or "Shqip" on an English page), and the copyright at the right, in the page's column (on phones the links wrap and the copyright goes under them), each link 40 px tall to tap; the page is a column at least a screen tall, so that the footer stands at the screen's foot under a short page too. Each is a column of text written to be read, with its description and canonical address for search engines, and in the pages' sitemap. The privacy page says what Albania's law on personal data (no. 124/2024, in force since 2025, which follows the GDPR) asks a site to say: who is responsible for the data (`OPERATOR`) and how to reach them (`CONTACT_EMAIL`, else the contact page); what the code keeps (the sign-in contact and name, hashed codes and sessions with the IP a code was asked from, listings, messages, the listings kept, blocks and reports, views counted by day without who saw them); for what and on what ground (the service asked for, under the terms; keeping the site safe and useful, a legitimate interest; "Plotëso me AI" only when asked; the law); the cookies, all the site's own, needed or remembering choices, so no consent is asked and no banner shows; who else gets data (Cloudflare, which the site is served through, the code's channel, the photo store, OpenStreetMap's tiles, Anthropic for "Plotëso me AI") and that some of them work outside Albania, mostly in the United States; for how long (a sign-in code request, with its contact and IP, a day: `db::forget_old_codes` deletes them as the search's words are rebuilt); the rights (to see, correct, take away, object to or restrict, and delete, answered within 30 days) and a complaint to the Information and Data Protection Commissioner (idp.al); and that accounts are for those of 18 and over. Change it when what the code does changes, and its date (`PRIVACY_UPDATED`). Where people hand over what it keeps, a line under the form says they accept the terms by going on and links the privacy page: signing in, and a new listing (not an edit). The contact page gives the address `CONTACT_EMAIL` names.
- The site is called Pluribus. Its logo is the name in lowercase, "pluribus", the word alone, in Clash Display Semibold (Indian Type Foundry's, from Fontshare, whose free licence allows it in a logo; Geist Black until 2026-10-11, and Playfair Display SemiBold, a serif, until 2026-10-10), the logo's typeface alone, drawn as shapes rather than set in the font (`templates/logo.svg`, which `base.html` includes), so that no font has to load for it and its i's dot can take a colour of its own: the letters in the text's colour (`--text`); on the light theme the dot black as they are and the inside of the b in the accent (`--highlight`, mint unless another is picked), on the dark theme the dot in the accent and the b open (`--logo-dot`, `--logo-counter`), as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (the dot was in the accent on both themes until then, with a dark edge inside it on white, `--logo-edge`). The b's inside is its counter drawn again under the letters (`.logo-counter`), grown by a line of 24 units all round so that no hairline of the bar shows at its edge, the letters round it setting it off on white with no edge of its own. The shapes are the font's own outlines in its units (1000 to the em), y down from its ascender, each letter after the last by the font's advances (it has no kerning), in a box as tall as the font's line, ascender to descender (`viewBox="0 0 4126 1140"`), so that the drawing sits in the bar as the word would; they were read from the font's TTF (its `glyf`, `loca`, `hmtx` and `cmap` tables), the i's dot being its contours above the stem. The drawing is 26 px to the font's em, 107 × 30 px, and 24 on phones, 99 × 27 (`.logo`, `.logo-mark`), in pixels, as the round buttons beside it set the bar's height; screen readers read the word, hidden from sight (`.visually-hidden`). The logo stays from page to page (`data-turbo-permanent`), so as the theme changes its colours slide to the other theme's over half a second rather than changing at once. The creature that stood before it, spinning and changing shape (`thinking.svg`, in the buttons' coral; the file went on 2026-10-11, when the icon drawn from it did too), left the header: its changing shape was redrawn by the main thread every frame (SVG's own animation, which the GPU can't take over), which on Chrome's phone profile (the CPU four times slower) kept the main thread busy a third of the time while a listing's page sat still, and half of it on the welcome page. Titles and text say "Pluribus" ("Shto një njoftim | Pluribus", "Në Pluribus që nga tetor 2026"); the code, the crate and the database keep the name marketplace. The site's icon, in browsers' tabs and on phones' home screens, is the logo's p, light on eerie black, with the i's square in mint over its bowl (`static/img/`, `site.webmanifest`; lime until mint became the accent there is, and the creature, in coral, before that, both on 2026-10-11), and its picture in link previews is the wordmark on the page's grey (`pluribus.png`).
- Phones get everything smaller: the root size drops from 16px to 14px below 720px (13.5px at 360px and under), and everything sized in `rem` follows. Form fields keep 16px text, because iPhones zoom into a focused field with smaller text, and get less padding instead. A listing's page has a few things smaller still there, so that its top isn't all large type: the price 24.5px (1.75rem) rather than 31.5, a little over the title's 22.4 (36px from 720px up), the path to its category in the welcome page's phone pills (above), and its detail pills at 13px with 16px icons. The pill over its photo says where and when on one line, the when in words within the week ("Sot", "Dje", "para 3 ditësh"; `format::when`) and as a date from a week on, both by Albania's clock (`format::date`, which follows Europe's summer time); its number ends the line under the title ("E përdorur · Numri i njoftimit: 1096191"; what kind of listing it is, "Në shitje", headed it until the user asked on 2026-10-12 for it under the price, in the accent's colour).
- Turbo, Stimulus, Leaflet and the fonts carry their version in the file name, and the site's own CSS and JavaScript a fingerprint of the files in their address (`base.css?v=8d6d43be`, and every module through the import map, which names each with it; from `web::asset_version()`), so browsers keep them all for a year without asking again (`immutable`, `web::cache_headers`). A page loaded in full, after the first, asks the server for none of its CSS and JavaScript, where it asked again about 33 of its 34 before painting and starting: on Chrome's phone profile (the CPU four times slower, 150 ms a request) the welcome page paints in 228 ms rather than 624, and its scripts are ready in 0.2 s rather than 1.3. A deploy that changes the files changes the fingerprint, and so their addresses; and since Turbo keeps the stylesheets and scripts of the first page a visitor opens while they click around, it tracks those tags (`data-turbo-track="reload"`): after a deploy, an open page reloads in full on its next click instead of running on with the old files. An address with another fingerprint, from a page loaded before a deploy, still gets the file, but checked on every use. A page preloads the modules it uses and no others (`<link rel="modulepreload">`): `web::render` reads the page it made for every controller it names (`data-controller`), and puts in its head `application.js`, those controllers and what they all import of the site's own modules (`web::module_preloads`), so a first visit fetches them at once, alongside the stylesheets, rather than `application.js` and only then what it imports: the welcome page 11 of the 33 modules, a category's 22, a listing's 14, the posting form 12, a page about the site 7 (every page fetched all 33 before). `application.js` registers a controller only once the page names it, importing its module then (as the browser has it already, preloaded, that takes no request), and a part of the page that comes later (a frame, a stream, the next page Turbo shows) brings its own: a `MutationObserver` watches for elements with `data-controller`. The hearts' and the town's controllers and `refresh.js` it imports from the start, as every page needs them (the header's heart, or the welcome page's, forgetting the town, a GET form's empty fields). The stylesheets are all on every page still: the pieces every page needs are spread over the eight (the theme's variables in `posting.css` too, `html`, `h1` and `.button` in `listing.css`, the logo in `welcome.css`, the header's unread count in `messages.css`), so a page's own sheets would first need those moved into `base.css` and `page.css`. The fingerprint is worked out once per server start, so restart the server after changing CSS or JavaScript (`dev/deploy.sh` does).

### Templates

Askama templates in `templates/`, compiled into the binary, so a template change needs a build. `base.html` holds what every page shares; the rest extend it or are included in one another.

| Template | What it renders |
|---|---|
| `base.html` | Every page's frame: English offered above the header, in English, to a visitor who hasn't chosen a language and whose browser reads no Albanian (`i18n::offer_english`, "English" and "Vazhdo në shqip"); the head (the theme set before the first paint, view transitions, the site's icons and manifest, the fonts preloaded, the eight stylesheets, the import map with every module at its fingerprinted address, the modules the page uses preloaded, which `web::render` puts where `MODULES_HERE` is, `application.js`), the stand-in's parts as the server words them (`#stand-in-parts`, an inert `<template>`: the description's heading and fold button, the contact bar's pills), the header with the menu's lines ("Menyja" for screen readers; on phones the count of conversations unread at their corner) and the logo (on phones in the bar's middle; with `TYPE_TRIAL=true`, the "Ff" typeface panel after it), the search (the `header_search` block, global, which `browse.html` fills with the category's and `home.html` empties; folded away on a listing's page, `search-folded` from the `header_class` block), and "Llogaria" at the right end, after the search's magnifier, which shows on a listing's page alone (`search_fold_controller.js`), and after the front page's "Posto" on phones (the `header_post` block, which `home.html` alone fills), with a dot while a notification about one of the user's own listings is unread (`account_controller.js`); the rail from 720 px up, the open menu's places in icons alone (posting, the account's pages, the notifications and the conversations with their counts, the listings kept with their dot, every main category, the dark theme's switch and the other language, the page's place marked: `rail::here`, a category's page its main category, `rail::category`); the menu, a modal `<dialog>` the header's "Menyja" opens (`menu_controller.js`): "Posto njoftim", the account's pages (Njoftimet e mia, Të ruajturat, Kërkimet e ruajtura, Lajmërimet and Mesazhet with counts in the accent asked for as it opens, Cilësimet), the main categories (`browse::menu_categories`), "Pamja dhe gjuha" (the size, compact, normal or large, its three words, the dark theme's switch, the six accents as radios, `look_controller.js`, and the language, Shqip | English through `/gjuha`) and the pages about the site; and the footer with the pages about the site and the other language, named in itself ("English", "Shqip"); `<html lang>` the page's language, and an English page's scripts' texts (`#i18n-en`) |
| `meta.html` | What a page says about itself in its head (`web::seo::PageMeta`): its description and canonical address, `noindex` on a search's results and a sold listing, the Open Graph tags of link previews, a listing's price and its schema.org data; the pages without it have `base.html`'s description of the site |
| `home.html` | The welcome page: a row with the search's grey pill asking "Çfarë po kërkon?", Material's search bar (its magnifier alone at its end, dark, in the accent on the dark theme), opening as Google's does, over a phone's whole screen with its way back first (`.search-back`, "Mbyll kërkimin"); no search in its top bar (it empties `header_search`), but on phones "Posto" there, its plus on a grey circle just before the account (it fills `header_post`), which the rail has from 720 px up (it stood after the search until 2026-10-11, and the listings kept and the conversations after it; the menu has them); under it the main categories, the four most looked for first (one line of tiles that slides sideways, each its icon on a shape over its name, `.shortcut-shape`, `.shortcut-name`); then the listings its visitor saw lately, "Shikuar së fundmi" with "Pastro", in a row over "Më të klikuarat" when there are some, "Më të klikuarat" and the newest listings, or in their place those recommended for the visitor, "Të rekomanduara për ty" (`recommended-heading`), the newest after them |
| `browse.html` | A category or search page: its head (`.browse-head` in `browse.css`): a category's heading, its way back before its name and on a subcategory's the category above, small over it, or a search's heading, the subcategory tiles under either, with how many it found beside it and "Ruaj kërkimin" at that line's end ("Kërkimi u ruajt" once kept), the top bar's search searching the page's category with its filters kept (its `header_search` block), the row of buttons on a line of its own (Filtrat, the filters picked as chips and the dropdowns of the others, the map, the cards' view and the order; on phones one line that slides sideways, `.browse-filters-line`) and what they open (the map, the cards' view's menu and the orders'), and the filters' sheet with its head and foot; then "Më të klikuarat", the listings' heading (on a category page their order, "Njoftimet më të reja"; elsewhere the order alone), the listings, the first two a promotion features first, each with its price marked in the accent, and what to do when there are none |
| `filters.html` | The filter form, from the category's filterable properties: the place, the price (`filter_price.html`: typed, the currency a two-part toggle of radios, its track, its quick choices), the filters buyers use most, the rest under "Më shumë filtra" |
| `filter_drop.html` | One of the results' row's filter dropdowns (`Filters::drops`): its pill, and its card, a form of its own with the page's other filters hidden: a list of choices (the picker, inline), boxes, a range, a year, the town, the kind of listing, the condition or the price, and "Shfaq rezultatet" where it isn't a single choice |
| `filter_field.html` | One property's filter: a dropdown, boxes, a range typed or picked from steps, a year, a box to tick |
| `filter_price.html` | The price filter, the sheet's and the row's dropdown's: typed, from and to, the currency's toggle, its track and its quick choices |
| `filter_type.html` | The kind of listing (Shitje, Qira...), first for property and under "Më shumë filtra" elsewhere |
| `map.html`, `map_radius.html`, `map_town.html` | The map page as the canvas lays it out: the map, and beside it a white box of its controls (the distance around the pin, which the zoom sets, the category, a town to move the map to, "Vendndodhja ime", "Vendos hartën" and folding the map away); the results under both (a category's card has its own, in `browse.html`) |
| `listing_grid.html` | A grid of listing cards, and the frame of the next page |
| `listing_card.html` | A listing's card: its photos (one, or all on a big card), the price, by how much it dropped lately or, for a car well under what cars of its model and year ask, "Çmim i mirë", where it is (in the list alone), the title, the start of its description (in the list alone), the details line, when it was posted (shown in the list alone), the heart, its views on "Më të klikuarat", and on /te-ruajturat, over the photo, "Rezervuar" or "Shitur" for a kept listing that is so since; its id, after its row's word in a row of cards ("most-viewed-1002"), and for one a promotion features at the results' top "featured-1001"; one a promotion highlights, `is-highlighted`; one it features there, `is-promoted`, its price marked in the accent (`.card-figure`, the figure a span of its own; "I promovuar" before the price for screen readers); for the stand-in a tap shows, data attributes on its head that no search engine reads as words (`data-deal`, `data-heading`, `data-loan`, `data-compare`, `data-line`, `data-facts`, `data-up`, `data-here`, and the description's start with its lines, `data-description`, what the contact bar offers, `data-reveal`, `data-closed`); "(i diskutueshëm)" after the price a span of its own (`card-negotiable`), which a narrow photo leaves out |
| `listing_frame.html`, `next_page.html` | The next page of cards, which Turbo loads as the frame scrolls into view |
| `card_row.html` | The row of cards that slides sideways, a macro: "Shikuar së fundmi", a seller's other listings; and "Më të klikuarat"'s two big cards (`card_pair`) |
| `heart.html` | The heart over a listing's photo |
| `description.html` | A listing's description under its heading, a car's own title over it, folded past a dozen lines (listing.html) |
| `seller_card.html` | The seller under their heading, "Shitësi", on the page with no box round them, as the canvas has it: the initial in a ring of 48 px (a store's logo, or its office block), the name, the verified shield, since when and after a dot how many listings ("Që nga tetor 2026 · 6 njoftime"; "Biznes · që nga …" for a store), how quickly they answer and when they were last here ("Përgjigjet zakonisht brenda 1 ore · Aktiv sot"), leading to the profile (listing.html), and under them, on every listing, the contact bar (on a car's or a property's page in the deal, at the right, 16 px under them; on a phone the bar at the screen's foot, which settles there, the section and its card no boxes, `display: contents`, so that it stays sticky); after the section, a section of its own, a hairline over it, the share and the report (`.listing-share`, listing_actions.html), as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (the bar stood under the price on most pages, the share and the report beside it or beside the message); on the seller's own car or property "Njoftimi yt" heads it in place of "Shitësi", with no contact bar under them (their own number was there once) and their own buttons there (listing_actions.html) |
| `contact_bar.html` | The contact bar: the phone, "Shfaq numrin" (the e-mail, "Shfaq email-in"), on the grey of secondary controls, and the message, "Shkruaji shitësit", filled in the accent, wide pills, which on a phone are the bar at the screen's foot; under the seller on every page (seller_card.html; it stood under the price on most of them until 2026-10-11); 48 px tall; never on the seller's own page (`!own`: their own number was there once, for them to reveal to themselves) |
| `listing_actions.html` | The share and the report, circles, and on the seller's own page their own buttons before them (Rifresko, for one expired or expiring within a week, when in its tooltip; Ndrysho; for one on sale or for rent, U shit or U dha me qira, and Rezervo or Hiq rezervimin; Çaktivizo, or Aktivizo for a paused or sold one; Fshi): the share and the report in a section of their own under the seller's, a hairline over it (seller_card.html, `.listing-share`; beside the contact bar, or the message's pill, until 2026-10-11); on the seller's own page their buttons under the price, on a car's or a property's page under the seller's card: on a car's or a property's, two to a line, each half of it, Fshi across the last line beside the share's circle, level with it (`.actions.own`), as the canvas has them; beside a photo small, 40 px and 14 px, as wide as their words, on lines that wrap; Fshi for a listing waiting for a moderator too (`PENDING_REVIEW`, which deleting refused once) |
| `loan_calculator.html` | The loan calculator's fields, the years and the interest, and sums, the down payment first, in the white box under "Llogarit kredinë", under a price (listing.html) |
| `listing.html` | A listing's page (its bar's search folded away behind the magnifier: `header_class`, `search-folded`): a car's laid out as FINN's from 960 px up (`.listing-car`: the photos across it, then the title, the key facts, the description and the details at the left, and the deal, `.listing-deal`, at the right), a property's the same way (`deal_page`), every other's split there (the photo at the left, the rest, `.listing-side`, at its right): where it is, in a breadcrumb at its top (back, the category above its own and its own, a link; at the right end the list, the listing before and the one after, grey round buttons of 40 px), the photos, the filmstrip of them over the photo's foot, hidden until the counter shows it, the place and date over them (on a car's page in the line under the title, its number shorter there, "Nr. 833042"), the views, the title, with what it is and its number under it, the price and its pill and circles (`.price-box`), the details, the description, the seller's card under its heading, "Shitësi", the contact bar under the seller (contact_bar.html), then the share and the report after a hairline, and a property's or a car's map under it, both across the page or the column, their other listings and the related ones; among the buttons, "Raporto"; the note that its seller is blocked, with where to let them be again; beside the seller's name, the verified shield; why it is off the site, for its seller and the moderators; its description folded past a dozen lines, "Shfaq të gjithë përshkrimin" unfolding it (`.description-fold`) |
| `report.html` | Reporting a listing: the listing as a small card (its photo, title, price and town, leading to it), the reasons, a row each in one white box, each with what it covers, and an explanation; for someone not signed in, the way to sign in; once sent, thanks, and "Blloko shitësin" with what it does, or that the seller is blocked |
| `area_price.html` | A property's price per square metre, its card, and its zone's average |
| `car_price.html` | A car's price against what cars of its model and year ask: a circle beside "Llogarit kredinë", its tag alone on the verdict's colour, its words for screen readers and under the pointer, and its card, the verdict, the median, the middle half's prices, from how many cars and what moves a car's price besides |
| `phone_stream.html` | The seller's number or address, revealed in the contact bar in the phone button's place, in a grey pill, 17 px bold, as the canvas has it, with its copy button and, for a number WhatsApp can reach, the circle that opens a chat with it |
| `listing_form.html` | The posting and edit form, as the canvas has it: in a column of 720 px in the middle of the page, in white boxes 14 px apart (`.form-box`): the title, the description and the photos (the zone across the box, the camera in a grey circle beside "Shto foto" and what it takes; with photos, squares of 110 px, each with its cross and its arrows, the zone a square after them); then, between the boxes, the AI's button with what it does beside it; the category, its kind and its condition side by side, then the category's own fields; the price, its kind, the amount and its currency, euros or lekë, a two-part toggle as the filters' price has (`.currency-choice`; a list once); the town and its zone, and the map; and, for someone not signed in, the name a new account takes and the contact, a signed-in seller's being their account's; the button across the column under them, 50 px tall, on an edit "Anulo" before it (in three sections under headings once, "Çfarë shet", "Kategoria dhe çmimi" and "Vendi dhe kontakti", with "Që të shitet më shpejt", four tips, beside a new listing's from 1024 px up, which the canvas folds into the fields' hints); every field with its name over it and a placeholder that is an example ("p.sh. Golf 7, 2016, naftë"); at a new listing's top, hidden until the script finds one, the listing this browser kept from before, with "Vazhdo" and "Fshije" (`draft_controller.js`); for a property or a car, "Vendi në hartë" under the town (`map_pin_controller.js`), and why a point was refused; on a new listing's, for someone in a store, "Posto si" first in the first box (themself, or a store they may post for, chosen from the page's list), each choice carrying whom buyers reach then (`data-contact`: the store's number, or the poster's own contact for a store without one), which the lead names as it is picked and on a form sent back (`contact_shown`; the lead named the poster's own contact for a store too once); under a new listing's, the terms it goes up under and the privacy page; signed out, the lead saying it goes up at once, or once the code is typed for a contact an account has; under the price, for a car whose model and year are chosen, what cars like it ask and how the price typed compares (`price_hint_controller.js`) |
| `listing_properties.html`, `listing_properties_frame.html` | A category's properties in the form, as the canvas has them in the category's white box: two to a line, each its name over it (an asterisk after a required one's), a number's unit inside its field at its end ("km"), the boxes to tick and the options' chips across (a card of their own once, a car's main ones tiles with an icon, named inside them); and the frame that brings them when the category changes |
| `my_listings.html` | "Njoftimet e mia": for someone in a store, the pills of whose listings, their own and each store's they post for, over the lead; what the listings drew, all together (views, contacts, saves, each with the last seven days' under it), the account's row of pills over the heading (`account_nav.html`), the notifications' saying how many are new ("Lajmërimet · 2 të reja"); then the listings in one white box, as the canvas has them, a row each: its photo (`db::first_photos`), its title, its price with what follows it quieter, its status, when it expires if within a week ("Skadon pas 3 ditësh"), the promotions on it and until when, each in its pill, its own counts, and its buttons at the row's end: first, on one line, Ndrysho, Çaktivizo or Aktivizo, and Fshi as round buttons, their icons alone (a pencil, two bars or a triangle, a bin in red), their words said to screen readers and under the pointer, as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (`.my-listing-tools`), then under them the rest, in words: Rifresko, with its icon, U shit, and Rezervo or Hiq rezervimin (`.my-listing-more`); all under the rest on a phone; a box a listing once, with no photo, the buttons under its title wherever they would leave it less than 16rem); "Shto një njoftim" and "Dil nga llogaria" with their icons; and last "Dil nga llogaria", which signs out (`/dil`); those blocked and what the account hears of are in "Cilësimet" |

### Stylesheets

`static/css/`, eight files, which pages get as one stylesheet, `/static/css/site.css` (`web::site_css`): the eight one after the other in this order (`web::STYLESHEETS`), minified (`web::minified`): without their comments, which are half their bytes, and with no more white space than the browser needs, one space kept where it parts two words (before "(" too, as "and(" would be a function) and none round the punctuation that ends or joins rules (38 KB gzipped as written, 18 without the comments, 17 minified). Every page renders the same from it as from the eight files: compared element by element, every computed property and those of `::before` and `::after`, on the welcome page, a category (Elektronikë's in its blue, and Prona's big cards), a listing, the posting form and the map on a wide screen, the only differences were custom properties, whose text browsers keep as written ("a,b" for "a, b"), which give the same values where they are used. A page shows nothing until its stylesheets have come, and one request comes sooner than eight: Lighthouse counted 300 ms for the eight through the tunnel. Every page also names it in a `Link: <…>; rel=preload; as=style` header (`web::render`), which Cloudflare, in front of the site's own domain, sends ahead as a 103 Early Hints answer, so the browser fetches the stylesheet while the origin is still building the page; a quick tunnel passes the header on as it is, and the browser starts the preload as the page's headers arrive. The server reads the files each time it is asked for it, as it serves the files themselves, so a change shows at once; at the fingerprinted address browsers keep it for good. The cascade follows the order, so a rule meets the others where it would in one file. Colours, sizes and weights are custom properties in `base.css` (`--main`, `--highlight`, `--text-sm`, `--semibold`...), with the dark theme's values beside the light theme's. `--gutter` (1rem: 14 px on a phone, 16 on a computer) is the room between the page and the screen's sides (`.container`), and as much stands between the header and the first line of the welcome page, of a category page and of a listing's page (its path; `.listing` in `listing.css`).

| File | What it styles |
|---|---|
| `base.css` | The fonts, the colours of both themes (the "Calmer Pluribus" palette since 2026-10-11: the greys, `--bg` the page, `--surface` the header, cards, fields and menus, `--surface-muted` grey buttons and the other secondary controls (`--surface-muted-hover` under the pointer), `--border`, `--border-soft` and `--border-strong`, `--text`, `--muted` and `--placeholder` (a field's example words, 4.5:1 on its white in either theme, as WCAG's AA asks: #767676 and #8c8c8c, the canvas's lighter #949494 and #7d7d7d once), `--muted-on-grey` for words on the grey of secondary controls (a grey badge, a chip's count, the reports' reasons, a closed conversation's note: 5:1 where the muted grey made 4.2:1 on the light theme), and `--control-edge` for an unticked box's or radio's edge and a switch's track, 3:1 as a control's outline needs; one main colour, `--main`, the text's, with `--main-hover` and `--on-main`, for what is picked; `--post`, `--on-post` and `--post-hover` for "Posto", the site's main thing to do, the accent in both themes as every filled button (the main colour on the light theme until 2026-10-11); the accent, `--highlight`, mint by default (the user's pick of 2026-10-11, lime before it) or one of four as bright by `data-accent` on `<html>` (`lime`, `coral`, `orange`, `blue`, `--choice-*`, the same in both themes), or the deep `royal` blue (`--choice-royal`, #275BFF, the user's ask of 2026-10-12), with `--on-highlight` for words and numbers on it (dark on every one but the royal blue, 6:1 at least, and white on that, 5.2:1), `--highlight-line` for the accent as a line on the dark theme (the accent itself, but the royal blue's seven tenths of it in white, 4.5:1 on the dark grey where its own is 2.7:1), `--highlight-hover` for a button in it under the pointer (a shade darker on the light theme, lighter on the dark), `--price-mark` for the band across a listing's price (the tint a filter that is on takes, `--filter-on`, quiet behind the figure; 30 points more of the accent at first, which the user found too loud), `--price-mark-photo` for a promoted card's on its photo (the dark theme's band in both themes), `--highlight-icon` for the front page's magnifier and a phone's plus in its bar, the accent as a line on the dark theme and the text's dark on the light one, where the accent is only ever a background, behind dark icons (`--search-knob`, the magnifier's circle, went on 2026-10-11 at the user's word; `--highlight-knob` did the same for "Ruaj kërkimin"'s bookmark until it went plain, 2026-10-11), `--dot-edge`, a dark ring inside an unread dot on the light theme, none on the dark theme (the logo's dot had a line inside it too, `--logo-edge`, until it went black there on 2026-10-11); `--logo-dot` and `--logo-counter`, the logo's i's dot and the inside of its b (black as the letters and the accent on the light theme, the accent and none on the dark), and how much of it a filter that is on takes (`--filter-share-light` and `--filter-share-dark`, each accent its own, for `--filter-on`); the states, `--red` for a kept listing's heart (the same in both themes, as the canvas has it: the heart sits on the photo), `--location` for places, `--danger` and its tint `--danger-bg` (the dark theme's red a shade lighter, #fa7a70, so that a danger badge's words make 4.5:1 on its tint), and `--below`, `--near` and `--above` with their tints for a price under, about and over an average, and `--below-fill`, `--near-fill` and `--above-fill` for a car price's circle (a light green, yellow or red under our black; a deeper one under our white on the dark theme); `--promo` and `--promo-edge` for a promoted card's panel; `--shadow` (white boxes, a segment picked), `--shadow-float` (what floats) and `--scrim`; the frosted glass of the header, a listing's contact bar at a phone's foot and its message sheet there, as Apple's bars and sheets and very slight, as the user asked on 2026-10-11: `--frost` (what passes under them blurred, 20 px, its colours livelier, 180%) behind `--frost-bar`, `--frost-sheet` and `--frost-page` (the header's colour, the surface's and the page's, each at 80%: the header and a phone's message sheet in the header's glass, the sheet at 92% at first, and a phone's contact bar in the page's own, as the user asked on 2026-10-11, in the header's glass for a while that day), whole where the reader asks for less transparency (`prefers-reduced-transparency`); the glow round a listing's photos, as YouTube's ambient light (the user's ask of 2026-10-11): `--glow` (the photo blurred 80 px, its colours livelier, 140%), `--glow-fade` (the radial mask that fades it to nothing at its box's edge) and `--glow-strength` (40% on the light theme, 55% on the dark: the PluribusGoogle canvas's values, the user's ask of 2026-10-11; before them 26% on both, blurred 110 px and 170% livelier, tuned by the user from 45% and 60% through 12% and 22%); the focus ring, `--focus-field` (the search bar's, `--focus-search`, went with its ring on 2026-10-11) (and no box of the browser's own over what a finger taps, the translucent blue one on Android: `-webkit-tap-highlight-color` transparent on the page, a link held showing its words underlined in the accent instead, 2 px, `a:not([class]):active` and each link's own rule beside its hover, the menu's rows and the rail's places filling their icons as under the pointer; the user's ask of 2026-10-11, which changes nothing a search engine reads); on a photo, `--photo-shade`, `--on-photo`, `--photo-label` and the good-price tag's `--photo-tag` on `--photo-tag-bg`, the same in both themes; `--size-header-row` its first row), and `--content-width` (1068); the spacing steps `--space-4` to `--space-30`: every name as the Pluribus design system has it (its `tokens.json`, https://claude.ai/artifact/Q8WaQRsCXVpzSVmTJM9uM6); ### JavaScript

`static/js/application.js` starts Stimulus, registers the controllers below as the page names them (the first `data-controller="map"` imports `controllers/map_controller.js`; see "How the frontend works") and holds the few behaviours every page shares: on a phone the bar's search row tucked away as the page scrolls down and brought back as it scrolls up (`search-tucked`, `base.css`; the user's ask of 2026-10-11), the view transitions skipped where the browser animated a back or forward itself (or can't say it didn't: Safari's engine, which every iPhone browser runs on, and Firefox), each as it starts (`skipTransition`), the setting left in the page so that Turbo's copy of the page left keeps it and that page slides in when opened again (after a swipe back on Android it came at once until 2026-10-11), but for the page's own way back, which slides back everywhere (`back_controller.js` says so first, `back:button`; on an iPhone it didn't slide until 2026-10-11), the photos of the page Turbo keeps for going back shown at once, a tapped card keeping its title underlined in the accent while its listing loads and a tapped category's pill its icon filled with it (`.pressed`), on a touch screen a page that hasn't come yet sliding in at once as a stand-in that it replaces as it comes (`standIn`, a visit's `snapshotHTML`, Turbo's preview; not for a page fetched ahead that has arrived, nor one Turbo kept), a tapped card's or pill's mark coming once the page has taken a tenth of a second to come (`PRESS_AFTER`: one fetched ahead shows at once, with no mark and no wait for one, as the user asked on 2026-10-11; the next page was held 200 ms after a tap so that the mark was seen until then), iPhones and iPads kept from zooming into a field as it takes the focus (the viewport's `maximum-scale=1`, set again after every page Turbo shows, there alone: Safari still lets a person pinch to zoom, which Android's Chrome wouldn't), a listing's page reporting its view, and a link that drops the town forgetting it (`data-forget-town`). `controllers/refresh.js` is what the filters' and the map's controllers share: refreshing the page in place; `controllers/leaflet.js` fetches Leaflet, once, for the maps, and gives them OpenStreetMap's tiles; `controllers/locate.js` asks the browser where the visitor is, for "Afër meje" and "Vendndodhja ime"; `controllers/signed_in.js` tells the hearts' and the messages' controllers whether someone is signed in (the `llogari` cookie), so that they ask the server for nothing otherwise. `application.js` also starts fetching a page as a finger touches its link (see "How the frontend works"). Every module goes to the browser minified, as Turbo and Stimulus do (`web::script`; see "How the frontend works").

| Controller | What it does |
|---|---|
| `account_controller.js` | The top bar's account button: a dot while a notification about one of the user's own listings is unread, one about to expire or expired (`/lajmerimet/numri`), but on `/lajmerimet` |
| `ai_fill_controller.js` | "Plotëso pjesën tjetër me AI": sends the form's title, description and photos, and fills in the answer as a seller would, leaving what the seller changed (a group of radios, the currency's toggle, by picking the one of its value); on an edit, whose form comes filled in, what it holds stands and only what is missing fills in, and the listing's number goes too, for its own photos (`data-ai-fill-listing-value`) |
| `back_controller.js` | The breadcrumb's way back, a category page's and a listing's: the page before when it was the site's, in this tab, saying so first (`back:button`) so that the page slides back as a drawer in every browser (`application.js`); else its link, a level up (the listing's category, from a listing) |
| `cards_controller.js` | The view menu: small cards, big ones or the list, remembered in a cookie |
| `code_boxes_controller.js` | The sign-in code's six boxes: the one input the code is typed, pasted or filled into (`one-time-code`) lies over them, its digits shown in them, the next one ringed while it has the focus; without it, the input alone |
| `conversation_controller.js` | A conversation, on its page or in a listing's message sheet, which it scrolls in place of the page: opens at its end, reads what it shows, asks for new messages every 8 seconds; the box that grows, Enter to send, the quick messages |
| `copy_controller.js` | Copies the seller's number or address, a check for a moment |
| `picture_pick_controller.js` | A store's logo or cover on its form: "Zgjidh foton", a label, opens the file field's picker, the field out of sight once the script runs (without it the field shows and the label goes), and the picture chosen shows in its frame at once (an object URL, let go after) |
| `drag_row_controller.js` | A mouse drags the row that slides sideways ("Shikuar së fundmi", a seller's other listings, and from 720 px up the front page's line of categories, the controller's own element), flicking it on, the click that ends a drag opening no card |
| `dropdown_controller.js` | A `<details>` menu closes on a tap outside or Escape; one of the browse row's (the order, the view, the filter dropdowns), open one at a time |
| `filter_drop_controller.js` | One of the results' row's filter dropdowns: a single choice applies at once and folds it; boxes, a range or a price apply as it folds, or with its button, if they changed, the page refreshing in place |
| `filters_controller.js` | The filters' sheet: applies them as they change, keeps what is open while the page refreshes, fits the row's cards under the row; its Qyteti, Zona (where the town has zones) and "Afër meje"; asks for the count past 1 000 for its button and the count's pill under the heading |
| `fold_controller.js` | A listing's description longer than a dozen lines, folded to that many with a fade, and "Shfaq të gjithë përshkrimin" under it, which unfolds it for good; a listing's stand-in's too (`application.js` registers it on every page, so that the stand-in is folded before it is first drawn) |
| `lines_controller.js` | On a browse page's results, the list's descriptions: each fills the room its card leaves under the title and the details, as many whole lines as fit (`--lines`, the last ending in "…"), measured again by a ResizeObserver whenever its box changes size (the view menu, the size, a title wrapping) |
| `i18n.js` | Not a controller: the scripts' texts in the page's language, keyed by their Albanian (`t`, `tf`), the English from the JSON an English page carries; the controllers that say anything import it |
| `heart_controller.js` | The hearts: fills those of the listings kept, keeps one or lets it go at a tap; puts the dot on the rail's heart (on phones the welcome page's, in the row with its search) while kept listings dropped their price unseen or saved searches have alerts unseen, saying which to screen readers |
| `inbox_controller.js` | The count of conversations with messages unread: on the rail's "Mesazhet", and on phones on the menu's button; and by its values (`url`, `name`, `counted`) the count of notifications new on the rail's "Lajmërimet" (`/lajmerimet/numri`) |
| `listing_form_controller.js` | The listing form follows its category: its kinds of listing, condition, currency (the toggle's radio picked, `pickCurrency`, until the seller picks one) and properties; on a new listing, "Zona" follows the zone its words name (`guessZone`); "Posto si" changes whom the lead names (`posted`, each choice's `data-contact`) |
| `listing_map_controller.js` | A property's or a car's map under its seller: loads as it comes near the screen, a circle round its point fitted into it |
| `map_aside_controller.js` | A car's map into the column at the right from 960 px up, where that column leaves room under its content for a square map as wide as it, and back when it doesn't |
| `loan_controller.js` | The loan calculator under a price: the down payment a set share of the price, the loan the rest, paid back over the years at the interest given |
| `map_pin_controller.js` | The listing form's "Vendi në hartë", for a category whose pages show a map (`data-mapped`): a map opened under its button on the point given before or the town chosen (each town's point from `data-map-pin-towns-value`), the pin standing in the middle as on the map page and the map moved under it; once the seller moves it, the middle is the point (the hidden `latitude` and `longitude`); "Hiqe pikën" takes it off; a town chosen meanwhile, with no point yet, moves the map to it |
| `map_controller.js` | The map: its pin in the middle, the circle, the same size whatever the zoom, the distance the zoom sets, the dots, "Vendndodhja ime" |
| `message_sheet_controller.js` | A listing's message sheet: on a phone a card at the screen's foot, floating 8 px in from its sides and bottom edge with its corners round, sliding up from its bottom, the page dimmed under it, its bar or head dragged down or flicked, or a tap on the dimmed page, closing it (never a tap that began in the sheet: rising under the finger as its box takes the focus, the sheet left the shade where the finger lifted, and closed the moment its box was tapped once), and rising to float 8 px over where the keyboard will be as the box to write in takes the focus, the keyboard's height remembered from the last time, the page under it unmoved (`raise`, `follow`, `lower`); from 720 px up a popup card from the button, under or over it, closed by a click outside; closes on "Mbyll", Escape or Turbo caching the page, emptying its frame; a response without the frame opens as a page |
| `most_viewed_controller.js` | The button that shows a category page's "Më të klikuarat" or folds it away, remembering the choice in a cookie |
| `narrow_controller.js` | A select that depends on another (a car's model on its make, a town's zones) offers only what fits |
| `photos_controller.js` | The listing form's photos, one list of those the listing has and those picked (a few at a time, or dropped onto their zone from a computer's files, the zone lit while a file is over it): the first the cover, marked "Kopertinë", each moved by its arrows or dragged, the cross over its corner taking it out ("Hiq" under it once); the order and those taken out sent beside them (`photo_order`, `remove_photo`); shrunk before sending; kept across a form sent back with an error (`photos_kept`, `data-photos-again-value`) |
| `picker_controller.js` | A select chosen from a list in the page rather than the phone's own picker; its button and list stay through a refresh in place; its empty choice in the placeholder's grey, but where it is a choice itself ("Posto si: Unë", `data-picker-placeholder-value="false"`); in the row's filter dropdowns a list always shown, with no button (`data-picker-inline-value`) |
| `place_controller.js` | Keeps the town a browse page is in, in a cookie, for the next pages |
| `price_range_controller.js` | The price filter: typed, its currency's toggle (the radio checked), its track with two ends and its quick choices, each moving the others |
| `draft_controller.js` | A new listing's form kept in this browser as it is typed (`localStorage`, `pluribus-draft`), a moment after each change: every field but the photos and the form's own hidden ones (of a group of radios, the currency's toggle, the one picked, which comes back picked); back on an empty form within 30 days, "Ke një njoftim të pambaruar … të ruajtur sot në 14:32" with "Vazhdo", which fills it in as the seller would (the category first, its fields once loaded, a make before its model), and "Fshije"; a listing that goes up, or waits for its code, takes the draft with it (`turbo:submit-end`), a form sent back keeps it |
| `price_hint_controller.js` | The listing form's hint under the price, for a car whose model and year are chosen: what cars of that model two years either side ask, and how the price typed will compare on the car's page, or that it is so far under them it is likely wrong (`/shto/cmimi`), asked again a moment after the typing stops, the model and year read from the properties' frame, the currency from its toggle's radio picked |
| `recent_controller.js` | "Pastro" on the welcome page's row of the listings seen lately: forgets them, the cookie and the row |
| `search_fold_controller.js` | The magnifier before the account on a listing's page, whose bar folds its search away: opens the search where the other pages have it, its field focused within the tap (a phone's keyboard up) and, empty, offering the last searches; folds it again on another tap, on Escape once its suggestions are closed, and before Turbo keeps the page; hidden and idle in every other bar |
| `share_controller.js` | Shares a listing, or copies its link: the share circle among a listing's buttons, and "Ndaje" in the note on a listing just posted (`share_note.html`) |
| `slides_controller.js` | A big card's photos, and a listing's on its page (with its counter, "2 / 5"): a mouse drags them, the dots say which shows and go to theirs on a click, the arrows' quick steps, round and round, each arrow showing near its edge; and the listing's filmstrip over its photos' foot, shown and hidden by the counter, each thumbnail going to its photo, the one showing marked and kept in view, the strip stepped by arrows under a mouse; and the glow behind a listing's photos turned to the one that shows as they slide, its tiny copy fading in over the last's in 0.4 s, at once for visitors who ask for less motion (`shine`, by what the glow shows, so that a page Turbo kept glows with its first photo again) |
| `sticky_controller.js` | A listing's contact bar on a phone: a slight shadow over it while it floats at the screen's bottom, none once it settles after the description |
| `suggest_controller.js` | The search boxes' suggestions as words are typed, and the keys that go through them; while a box is empty, the last searches (`recent_searches.js`) and the row that forgets them, the list named for which it holds; the welcome pill's cross, shown while it has words, which empties it; the welcome pill's search open as Google's: over a phone's whole screen as its words get the focus (`is-open`, for a box with a way back, its back target), closed by it (`dismiss`), Escape, Android's back (a `CloseWatcher`) or the page left, and the form marked while its list shows (`has-list`) |
| `type_trial_controller.js` | With `TYPE_TRIAL=true`, the top bar's "Ff" panel: the headings' and the text's typeface from a list, the site's own and Google Fonts' (fetched as picked), and their weights (h1, h2, text, strong), put on the page as the root's custom properties; and an icon library, Lucide, Tabler, Phosphor or Heroicons, whose files (unpkg) go into the site's `<svg>`s in place of its own drawings where the library has the icon (the UI's by `data-icon`, the categories' by their slug), the rest staying; kept in the browser |
| `theme_controller.js` | The light or dark theme, remembered: the menu's switch, on every page (`theme:changed`) |
| `menu_controller.js` | The menu: opens the header's "Menyja" dialog as a modal one, closes it by its cross, a tap on the dimmed page or Escape (and before Turbo keeps the page), keeps the button's `aria-expanded`, and asks for the unread notifications and conversations as it opens, for the counts by their rows (`/lajmerimet/numri`, `/mesazhet/numri`) |
| `look_controller.js` | The look the visitor picks, kept in `localStorage` and put back before the first paint by `web::THEME_SCRIPT`: the size (`data-size`: compact, normal or large) and the accent (`data-accent`: mint, or `lime`, `coral`, `orange`, `blue` or `royal`), in the menu's "Pamja dhe gjuha" (`look:changed`) |

## Categories and their properties

Every category except Të tjera has subcategories, and listings go into those: the posting form groups them under their category, and a category page shows its subcategories as links and its listings with theirs (`/c/prona` lists every property, `/c/apartamente` flats only).

| Category | Subcategories |
|---|---|
| Automjete | Makina, Motoçikleta, Tregtare, Pjesë |
| Prona | Apartamente, Garsoniera, Vila, Dyqane, Zyra, Truall, Garazhe |
| Elektronikë | Telefona, Laptopë, Kompjuterë, Tableta, Televizorë, Konsola, Fotoaparate, Audio |
| Shtëpi | Mobilje, Elektroshtëpiake, Kopsht, Dekor |
| Punë | Turizëm, Ndërtim, Shëndetësi, IT, Shitje, Transport, Operatorë |
| Shërbime | Riparime, Transport, Pastrim, Mësime, Fotografi, Rikonstruksion |
| Veshje | Xhaketa, Këpucë, Fustane, Çanta, Ora, Aksesorë |
| Sport | Biçikleta, Fitnes, Muzikë, Kamping, Ski, Të tjera |
| Kafshë | Qen, Mace, Zogj, Peshq, Aksesorë |
| Makineri | Ndërtim, Bujqësi, Energji, Industri |

Every name is one word but "Të tjera", as the user asked on 2026-10-11 (migration 0042): they were two once, "Konsola lojërash", "Goma / disqe", "Makineri / biznes", which the tiles and the path cut short. Two are their old name's first word, where a new one would have named searches about other things: Mbushës, the electric cars' chargers ("karikues telefoni" is a phone's), and Aksesorë, the pets' ("pajisje" is any equipment). The English names follow (`i18n/en/data.txt`): "Konsola" is "Console", as the consoles' property is called.

Most subcategories were a "type" property before (`property_type`, `electronics_type`...). Migration 0008 turned each of its options into a subcategory (`categories.replaces_option_id`) and moved the listings; the property stays on the listings, and on new ones posted in that subcategory, only so that search finds them by it ("telefon" finds the phones). `categories.allow_listings` is false for a category with subcategories.

Pjesë goes one level further, as on MerrJep: Rezervë, Rrota, Multimedia, GPS, Tuning, Tapete, Rimorkio, Mbushës and Të tjera (migration 0012; the spare parts, Rezervë, kept their address, `/c/pjese-kembimi`, and their listings). Nothing in the code counts levels: a category page's pills lead one level up and across its own, however deep it is, the posting form groups the kinds under "Automjete › Pjesë", and a category's page lists everything under it, however deep.

A category page shows its subcategories under its heading as tiles like the welcome page's main categories, each with its icon where it has one (`icons::subcategory` in `icons.rs`: every subcategory has one, from Automjete's car from the front, motorbike, truck and wrench to the kinds of parts' nut and tyre, Prona's blocks of flats, Elektronikë's laptop and headphones, and Makineri's excavator and tractor; a subcategory added later shows its name alone until it has one), all at once on as many lines as they need, or on a phone on one line that slides sideways. Links to a narrower category, the subcategory tiles and the menu's entries under the page's category, keep the page's filters, since a subcategory has its parent's properties: "Audi, 2006" on Pjesë / aksesorë stays on in GPS / navigatorë (`Filters::narrowed_url`). Links up the tree or across it keep only the search words, the town and the map pin (`Filters::category_url`).

### Properties

Each category's properties are rows in the database, not code. A browse page builds its filters from the category's `filterable` properties, listing cards show the `show_in_card` ones after the city ("Tiranë · 2016 · 148 000 km · Naftë"), and the listing page shows the `show_in_details` ones as small grey pills, 36 px tall with no outline, as the design system has small pills (14px, their icons 16; 13 and 15 on a phone), the `show_in_card` ones among them with their icon in the text's colour where the others' is muted (a property's zone's too; the icon stood in a small circle of the accent's tint once, one tinted thing more on a page with enough), the pill itself as the others, each with its icon, drawn as the welcome page's categories are (`web/icons.rs`): the category's own where it says more than the property's (a phone's brand is a phone, a dog's breed a dog), and the property's name beside it where the value alone wouldn't say what it is ("Kati 6", but "Euro 6"); a box ticked shows its name ("Pa aksident"). A new property needs its icon there, or a test fails. What `0003_category_properties.sql` sets up, and the migrations named after it; none of it is required but a car's make, model and year:

| Category | Properties |
|---|---|
| Makina | make, model (grouped by make), year, mileage, fuel, gearbox, body type, colour, options ("Opsionet"); customs ("E zhdoganuar"), plates, engine (cm³), power (HP), Euro class, drive, doors, seats, owners, and whether it is accident-free, has its service book, inspection and insurance (0023); the posting form shows them two to a line and in one size, the first eight with an icon before the name, the options last; its title is made from its make, model, engine and year ("Volkswagen Golf 1.6, 2016", the engine in litres to a tenth and left out when not given: "Mercedes-Benz S-Class, 2019"; `listings.generated_title`, kept by `listing_generated_title()` wherever `search_extra` is), the seller's own heading the description and the details leaving out the make, the model and the year (`properties::TITLE_CODES`; the engine stays there, to the cm³). Until migration 0027 it was the make, model, body type and colour ("Volkswagen Golf Hatchback, e zezë"), which made four Golfs under a listing four "Volkswagen Golf Hatchback"s |
| Motoçikleta, Tregtare (0023) | make, from lists of their own, kind (scooter, naked, enduro, ATV...; van, pick-up, truck, minibus...), model, year, mileage, customs, plates, fuel; a motorcycle's engine ("Kubatura"), licence (AM, A1, A2, A) and colour; a van's power, gearbox, payload and seats. Their forms are plain ones, the make first, without a car's icons |
| Prona | for every kind: area and amenities (lift, parking, garage, terrace, sea view, title deed...); and since 0023, a flat: rooms (1+1, 2+1...), bathrooms, floor and the building's floors, furnishing, year built, condition, heating, orientation, and for letting it, deposit, bills, pets and students; a studio the same without rooms and bathrooms; a house: its plot's area and its floors rather than a floor; a shop: its window, floor, age, condition and heating; an office: its rooms and toilets besides a flat's floor, age and heating; a plot: what it is for, its frontage, a permit and a road; a garage: its spaces and floor. Each kind offers its own amenities. Rooms, floor and furnishing were every kind's before 0023, and central heating an amenity |
| Elektronikë (0021) | for every kind: brand, warranty, what comes with it (the box, the receipt...); a phone: model (grouped by brand), storage, RAM, battery (%), colour, 5G / Dual SIM / eSIM; a laptop: processor, RAM, storage, disk, screen (inches), graphics, system; a desktop: what it is (gaming PC, All-in-One...), the same and whether a monitor comes with it; a tablet: storage, screen, Wi-Fi or SIM; a TV: size ("Diagonalja", inches), resolution, panel, Smart TV; audio: what it is, wireless, noise cancelling; a camera: what it is (DSLR, mirrorless, lens, drone...), sensor, lens mount, shutter count; a console: the console (grouped by maker), edition, games, controllers |
| Shtëpi (0023) | furniture: what it is, material, colour, size; appliances: which, brand, energy class, capacity, warranty; garden things: what, material; décor: what, material, colour |
| Punë | schedule; contract, where the work is done (on site, hybrid, from home), experience, education, languages and shift, and in tourism and building, board and meals (0023) |
| Shërbime (0023) | the areas served, whether they come to the client, give an invoice, years of experience, when; repairs, cleaning, photography and building work: what they do; lessons: subject, level, online or in person; transport: the vehicle, its passengers and load |
| Veshje | for whom, brand (each kind's own), colour; jackets and dresses: size, a child's size, material; shoes: size, EU 20 to 48, material; bags and accessories: material; watches: movement, material (0023; size was every kind's before) |
| Sport (0023) | a bicycle's kind, wheels, frame, brand, frame material and gears; fitness equipment; an instrument, its kind and brand; camping and fishing gear; winter sports gear, its length and a boot's size |
